### [88] [Decodable but Not Corrected by Fixed Residual-Stream Linear Steering: Evidence from Medical LLM Failure Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05715)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM 隐藏状态中存在可线性解码的失败信号（Overthinking），但由于表征纠缠，固定线性 steering 无法利用其进行修正。不过，这些失败结构仍可支持后生成可靠性估计（选择性弃权）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 LLM 隐藏状态中可线性解码的失败信号是否可用于修正模型错误输出。

Method: 提出 Overthinking (OT) 分析框架——一种在医学问答中稳定的失败模式（Jaccard ≥ 0.81，注释者一致性 94%）；测试 5 类固定线性 steering（29 种配置，跨架构 Qwen2.5-7B 和跨领域 MMLU-STEM）；使用 LEACE 概念擦除、共享方向 steering 及逐实例探针-干预相关性分析。

Result: OT 可线性解码（71.6% 平衡准确率，p < 10⁻¹⁶），但 5 类固定线性 steering（29 配置）均无效（Δ ≈ 0）。三个证据指向表征纠缠：OT 方向与任务关键计算重叠 85-88%（特异性比 ≤ 0.152）；非靶向共享方向 steering 损害准确率（-12.1pp）；LEACE 擦除损害准确率（-3.6pp，p=0.01），而 10 次随机擦除产生 Δ=+0.3pp。逐实例探针-干预相关性 r=-0.002（p=0.97）。积极面：同一探针可实现选择性弃权（留出集 AUROC=0.610，超越所有 5 个不确定性基线，p=0.009）。

Conclusion: 线性 steering 修正不可行，但可解码的失败结构可支持后生成可靠性估计（选择性弃权）。研究揭示了 LLM 表征纠缠现象，限制了固定线性干预的有效性。

Abstract: Can linearly decodable failure signals in LLM hidden states be leveraged to correct those failures? We investigate this classification-correction gap via Overthinking (OT)--a stable behavioral regime (Jaccard >= 0.81, 94% inter-annotator agreement) in medical QA where models answer correctly under resampling yet fail in extended chain-of-thought. OT is linearly decodable at 71.6% balanced accuracy (p < 10^{-16}). Yet five families of fixed linear steering (29 configurations, n=1,273) all yield Delta ~= 0, with identical null results cross-architecture (Qwen2.5-7B) and cross-domain (MMLU-STEM). Three convergent lines of evidence suggest representational entanglement: the OT direction has 85-88% overlap with task-critical computation (specificity ratio <= 0.152); non-targeted shared-direction steering damages accuracy (-12.1pp); and LEACE concept erasure damages accuracy (-3.6pp, p=0.01), while 10 random erasures produce Delta=+0.3pp. The per-instance probe-steering correlation is r=-0.002 (p=0.97). Positively, the same probe enables selective abstention (held-out AUROC=0.610, exceeding all five uncertainty baselines, p=0.009): decodable failure structure supports post-generation reliability estimation even when the fixed linear steering family cannot exploit it for correction.

</details>


### [89] [More Is Not Always Better: Cross-Component Interference in LLM Agent Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05716)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以完整因子实验（5 个智能体脚手架组件的全部 32 种组合 × 2 个基准 × 3 个模型家族/5 个规模，共 118 个配置、3.2 万+ 次评估）系统检验了"组件越多越好"这一默认假设。研究定义并实证了**跨组件干扰（Cross-Component Interference, CCI）**：组件组合时出现负边际收益，使"全量配置（All-In）"始终被某个真子集匹配或超越。结果显示最优组件数是任务相关（HotpotQA k*=1，GSM8K k*=3）且规模敏感（8B 差距 32%、70B 19%、Claude Haiku ≈0%）；精确 Shapley 值显示工具使用（T）独占约 70% 脚手架价值、规划（P）的 Shapley 值为显著负；325 个三元组中 183 个违反子模性（56.3%），导致贪心选择不可靠。作者据此建议用"按任务做子集选择 + 交互感知分析"取代"最大化装配"的默认实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 智能体系统通常由规划（Planning）、工具调用（Tool Use）、记忆（Memory）、结构化推理（Chain-of-Thought/Structured Reasoning）、自反思（Reflection）等脚手架组件堆叠而成，LangChain 等框架鼓励自由组合，但学界与工业界普遍隐含"组件越多越好"（more is better）的假设。已有工作要么只做单组件消融，要么只研究两两交互（如指令干扰、MTL 负迁移），缺乏对全部 2^5 组合的高阶交互全景刻画，因此无法回答"该开哪些开关"这一实际工程问题。本文动机是：把组件交互当作可测量的实证现象（而非机制猜想），用完整因子设计消除选择偏差，检验 All-In 是否真的最优。

Method: **组件集合与定义**：五组件 P/T/M/SR/R，配置 C⊆{P,T,M,SR,R}，共 32 种。CCI 定义为加入组件 s 使性能下降（φ(C∪{s})<φ(C)）；k*=argmax_K max_{|C|=K} φ(C)。**实验协议**：全因子 32 配置 × HotpotQA（token F1）与 GSM8K（精确匹配准确率），每配置 100 题、最多 4 步推理、temperature=0.1、top-p=0.9、每步 256 token；模型为 Llama-3.1-8B/70B（70B 用 4-bit NF4 量化）、Qwen2.5-3B/7B、Claude Haiku 4.5（AWS Bedrock）；主设置对 16 个关键配置做 10 随机种子复现（16,000 次评估），总计 32,000+ 评估。**统计方法**：单种子用 2,000 次 bootstrap 95% CI；多种子用配对 t 检验 + Wilcoxon 符号秩 + Holm/Benjamini-Hochberg 校正，主效应量为配对 Cohen's dz，辅以贝叶斯 JZS（Cauchy r=0.707）贝叶斯因子与 HDI；交互项用 50,000 次 BCa bootstrap。**分析框架**：6 参数主效应回归 vs 16 参数成对交互回归（R²/adj-R²/LOOCV、BIC/AIC）；在完整 32 个联盟值上精确计算 Shapley 值；325 个 (S,T,i) 三元组的子模性检验 + 任务级 cluster bootstrap（5,000 次）；Harsanyi 三体红利 INT₃ 估计。**稳健性检验**：长度匹配的无意义填充对照、3 个模板改写变体、Claude Haiku 饱和验证、5 种组件排序的前期测试、19 个任务的错误分析；并报告未获正面结果的机制实验（隐藏态探针、输出熵、数据流图预测）。

Result: **1) CCI 在 8B/HotpotQA 上被多方法确认**：T 单独 F1=0.233±0.039，All-In=0.177±0.049，配对 t(9)=2.74 (p=0.023)、Wilcoxon p=0.014、BF10=3.2、P(δ>0)=0.98、95% HDI [+0.010,+0.102]、dz=0.87（大效应）；T 在 8/10 种子胜出；从 T 出发的 6 次扩张全部退化，5/6 未校正显著、4/6 经 Holm 校正后显著，dz 0.87–1.44（为提示工程文献典型效应量的 2–4 倍）。**2) 任务依赖**：GSM8K 上 k*=3（T+SR+R=0.430），比 All-In（0.24）高 79%（McNemar p=0.010）；HotpotQA 上 k*=1；干扰来源也不同（HotpotQA 是"加任何组件即退化"，GSM8K 是"三组件核外加 P/M 才退化"）。**3) 规模/能力依赖**：8B 加 R 于 T 是 −0.072，70B 变为 +0.072（方向反转）；best-vs-All-In 差距 32%（8B）→19%（70B，单种子 p=0.13 不显著）→<1%（Claude Haiku，K=1..5 全部落在 F1∈[0.395,0.398] 的 0.25 点带内），呈"能力梯度/饱和"模式。**4) 结构分析**：主效应模型 R²=0.916、adj-R²=0.899、LOOCV R²=0.872，显著优于成对模型（LOOCV R² 降至 0.748，ΔBIC=25.3），表明 CCI 在聚合层面主要是可加的、每个组件施加近似恒定的边际成本；精确 Shapley 值 φ(T)=+0.177（z=9.1，占绝对 Shapley 质量的 70%）、φ(SR)=+0.028、φ(R)=+0.004、φ(M)=−0.016、φ(P)=−0.029（95% CI [−0.055,−0.003]，显著为负），P+M 对 T/SR 造成约 22% 的"CCI 税"；GSM8K 上 R 的 Shapley 升至 +0.065。**5) 非子模性**：325 个三元组中 183 个（56.3%）违反子模性，cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.43,0.66]（作者声明不主张违规率本身异于随机），但边际比中位数 γ=0.52（bootstrap CI [0.23,0.71]，显著低于 1）；top-20 违规中 90% 为符号翻转（组件在小脚手架中有害、在特定大组合中有益），T 出现在 95% 的高违规上下文中；贪心选择在 GSM8K 从 T 出发停在 K=1（0.220），无法达到真实最优 0.430（+95%）。**6) 三体协同（探索性）**：(T,SR,R) 的 Harsanyi 红利 INT₃=+0.175，BCa 95% CI [+0.004,+0.352]，p=0.027（单侧 t），GSM8K 上近零（+0.01），具任务特异性。**7) 稳健性**：真实组件比长度匹配填充高 6–9 倍（CCI 非 token 长度伪影）；3 个改写模板中 T>All-In 全部成立（+3%/+20%/+13%），但细粒度配置排序对模板敏感（Spearman ρ=0.14–0.49）；跨家族 Qwen 复现（3B 40% → 7B 8%）；Claude Haiku 上 Memory 仍有害（P+T+SR → P+T+M+SR 为 −5%）。**8) 错误分析**：19 个"T 成功而 All-In 失败"任务中，68% 为组件特异性干扰、21% 阈值崩塌、11% T 本身脆弱；P 涉及 84%、M/R 各 68%、SR 58%，13/19 个任务中至少有一个组件可安全加入，提示无单一"元凶"。

Conclusion: "最大化装配"的默认实践应被替换为按任务/按模型的子集选择与交互感知分析：先评估子集再上线组件；不要假设"少即是多"或"多即是少"，最优组件数随任务在 1–4 间变化、随模型能力单调衰减；不能依赖贪心式逐个加组件（子模性不成立、存在符号翻转）；工具使用是关键催化剂，记忆与规划通常是净负担。论文提出三类探索性干扰机制（Type I 带宽竞争、Type II 信号稀释、Type III 容量消解）与"上下文窗口拥挤（context-window crowding）"概念（区别于 MTL 的训练期梯度冲突），并建议发展推理期的"组件亲和度"度量。主要局限：仅 HotpotQA/8B 有 10 种子验证，其余为单种子方向性证据；工具使用组件耦合了 Finish[answer] 提交协议（混淆 T 的贡献）；70B 使用量化；仅两个基准、4 步预算、纯提示式实现；INT₃ 为探索性且 CI 宽；CCI 尚无确证因果机制，隐藏态探针、输出熵、数据流图预测等机制假设均为阴性结果；更大前沿模型（GPT-4/Opus）可能呈现真正的组件互补性而非饱和。

Abstract: LLM agent systems are built by stacking scaffolding components (planning, tools, memory, self-reflection, retrieval) assuming more is better. We study cross-component interference (CCI): degradation when components interact destructively. We run a full factorial experiment over all 2^5=32 subsets of five components on HotpotQA and GSM8K with Llama-3.1-8B/70B (96 conditions, up to 10 seeds). The All-In system is consistently suboptimal: on HotpotQA, a single-tool agent surpasses All-In by 32% (F1 0.233 vs 0.177, p=0.023); on GSM8K, a 3-component subset beats All-In by 79% (0.43 vs 0.24, p=0.010). Optimal component count is task-dependent (k*=1-4) and scale-sensitive: at 70B, combinations that hurt at 8B provide gains, though All-In still trails the best subset. We fit a main-effects regression (R^2=0.916, adj-R^2=0.899, LOOCV=0.872), compute exact Shapley values, and find 183/325 submodularity violations (56.3%), showing greedy selection is unreliable. A three-body synergy among Tool Use, Self-Reflection, and Retrieval (INT_3=+0.175, 95% CI [+0.003,+0.351]) is reported as exploratory. CCI replicates across model families (Qwen2.5) and is robust to prompt paraphrasing. Our findings suggest maximally-equipped agent defaults should be replaced by task-specific subset selection via interaction-aware analysis.

</details>


### [90] [ReFlect: An Effective Harness System for Complex Long-Horizon LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05737)
*Fan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReFlect 把 LLM 推理的"出错检测与恢复"从提示词内部外化为一个确定性的 harness（包装器）：用纯 Python 的形状分类器把问题路由到专用工具/校验器，格式不合法就以代码方式重试。六个领域、六个模型上任务成功率 41%–56%（较 Direct CoT 提升 +7~+29pp），SWE-bench 补丁结构质量从 0% 升至 82–87%，且每 token 成本最低（$0.36/100 正确）；harness 增益与模型基础能力负相关（斜率 -1.69，r=-0.76），即"越弱的模型获益越大"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程、多阶段推理任务（多文件代码、多文档科学综述、竞赛数学、具身规划）要求系统不仅局部推理正确，还要能判断"已走偏"、结构化校验中间产物并确定性恢复。现行范式 CoT、ReAct、Self-Refine、Reflexion、CRITIC、IterResearch 及工具使用框架均把检测—恢复逻辑留在提示词或模型自由文本轨迹内，共享两个在长程任务上失效的隐含假设：(1) 局部进展蕴含全局进展；(2) 模型重读自身输出即可有效自我纠错。后果是错误沿轨迹静默累积，形成"自我纠错盲区"，且没有确定性的恢复程序，缺乏可归因、可修复的失败根因。论文以实证先导实验（360 次受控推理）证明：提示词级自检在长程任务上系统性失效——100 个反思块中 90 个是公式化"无问题/继续"的模板，几乎不产生纠正（≤1.7% 纠正率），被测模型对错误答案的"错答接受率"至少 76%。据此提出核心命题：提示词级自批判 ≠ 结构性 harness（prompting for self-critique ≠ structural harnessing）。

Method: 提出 ReFlect harness：可靠性来自模型之外的包装层，而非提示词。设计上遵循三条原则：(P1) harness 而非 scaffold——问题分类、工具分派、格式校验、重试策略全部由确定性代码拥有，模型只在"结构受限槽位"内生成候选；(P2) 结构路由而非 LLM 自评——由基于问题内在特征（表格上下文、动作动词、推理规则、代码型输出、unified-diff 脚手架等，且不引用数据集名）的确定性分类器 Shape: x↦s 决定计算regime；(P3) 计算 regime 而非推理风格——异质失败被压缩为有限个 regime 失配，每个失配映射到一个确定性干预。

两种实例化：
(1) 轻量级（论文头条结果，算法 2）：Shape 分类器 → 七形状工具注册表（Symbolic→SymPy/Python 代码 + K=3 众数投票；Tabular→pandas/eval 数值计算；Logical→纯 Python Horn 子句前向链；Evidence→TF-IDF 检索 + 有据抽取 K=1；Procedural→ALFRED 动作前置条件状态追踪器（212 行 Python，无模拟器）；Artifact→SWE-bench diff 格式校验器（最多 2–3 次重试）；Fallback→K=5 自一致性 CoT）。工具内部使用 Validate Loop（校验器 + 重试）、K 采样众数投票或纯确定性计算；按工具设定不同温度（0.2/0.4/0.5/0.7），全程不跨调用保存状态。
(2) 重型级（算法 1）：在模型外维护结构化推理状态 S=(G,A,E,D,C,T,K,r,u)（目标树、带依赖级联的假设、来源证据、决策、冲突、压缩轨迹、检查点、regime、复合不确定度），四个反思算子 Inspect/Stabilize/Transform/Diversify（各自为独立程序化干预而非内联提示），以及基于规则的五状态 FSM 控制器（Explore/Execute/Verify/Recover/Consolidate），含 compile_view 的 regime 化提示构造与假设撤回的依赖级联。

实验：六个领域（SWE-bench Lite、QASPER、ProofWriter depth-5、AIME 2022–24、ALFRED、FinQA，每域 50 题共 300）× 六个骨干（Claude Haiku 4.5、gpt-4o-mini、Llama-3.3-70B、Qwen2.5-72B、GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5）；三个研究问题 RQ1 重型/RQ2 轻量/RQ3 增益归因（28 变体消融）；指标含域内正确率、SWE-bench 分层结构质量评分（0/0.3/0.6/1.0）、收敛率、token 效率、稳定错误复发率、验证器假阳率、救援/破坏率。

Result: (1) 先导实验（360 次推理，2 模型×3 方法×6 域×10 题）：Minimal ReFlect（ReAct+每 3 步插入 5 点检查单）在任何域上都从未优于基线；90/100 反思块为公式化样板，纠正率 1/60（Qwen）、0/60（Llama，Wilson 95% CI ≤8.9%）；重复退化循环 7%(Qwen)/23%(Llama)；Llama 25% 运行被反思开销截断；5 项根因归为"让模型在同一生成流中同时充当思考者与审计者"这一架构错误。(2) RQ1（70B，300 题）：重型族在 15.0–18.7% pair-mean 处停滞，与 Level-2 上限（Direct 15.0%、ReAct 14.2%、Self-Refine 13.4%、Reflexion 19.2%）相当；执行轨迹诊断显示状态抽取调用在 84% 题目上产出零证据（Llama 92%、Qwen 76%），算子触发率 Inspect/Transform ≤5%、Diversify 0%，regime FSM 从未到达 Consolidate（收敛 0%），即 70B 无法可靠填充其依赖的结构化状态（base-capability prerequisite）。放弃结构化状态、改用确定性 Python 路由的 HW-Code 跃升至 28.0% pair-mean（+9.3pp）。(3) RQ2：Full ReFlect 在六个模型上五域平均 41.3%–56.4%，每个模型相对 Direct CoT 提升 +7~+29pp；SWE-bench 结构质量 0%→82.6%–87.2%；ALFRED 0–5%→34–49%；能力补偿效应斜率 -1.69（r=-0.76），剔除 ALFRED/SWE-bench 的四域重拟合 -1.66（r=-0.84, p=0.036）；每模型提升中 19–22pp 来自两个与模型能力无关的确定性工具层（ALFRED 状态追踪器 +31~+47pp、SWE diff 校验器 +82~+87pp 结构质量），骨架贡献则随模型变弱而变大。(4) RQ3 消融（28 变体）：24 个 Level-2 提示词验证器（口头 SC、代码执行、确定性 linter、oracle、验证器合取、跨模型验证）全部落在 14–21% 窄带内，无一变体超过 L2-Verbal 峰值 20.5%，验证器假阳率恒为 76–98%（机制不变），REFLECT 成功率仅 5–16%，证明瓶颈是"让模型验证自身自由文本"这一任务本身；唯一突破天花板的是确定性计算（不要求模型验证自身输出）。(5) 效率：Full ReFlect 1,993 token/题、48.8% pair-mean、$0.36/100 正确，而 Reflexion 为 32,062 token、$10.26/100 正确——即在精度最高的同时成本最低（比 Direct CoT 精度 +21.9pp 且 token 成本相当）；收敛率 100%(Llama)/97%(Qwen)；救援:破坏 = 3.3:1（Claude Sonnet 4.5 达 34.6% 救援、零破坏）；全模型普遍失败题从 205/300 降至 137/300。(6) 局限与负面结果：SWE-bench 分数仅衡量补丁结构合法性而非修复正确性；QASPER 因无对应工具而净收益为零（提取能力受限）；确定性子程序带来更高"稳定错误率"（Llama 30.6%）的权衡；主结果使用 Together.ai/OpenRouter FP8 服务，与消融的 vLLM bf16 不可直接比较。

Conclusion: ReFlect 的核心论断是：长程推理的可靠性应来自 harness（模型之外的确定性包装层），而不是来自提示词或更多 LLM 调用。CoT 使推理可分解，Self-Refine/Reflexion 使其可自评，ReFlect 使其"可被 harness 化"——不要求模型想得更多，而是把每一次失败路由到能解决它的确定性机器（分类器、专用工具、格式校验器、retry-as-code）中。方法上模型无关、无需训练、纯推理期生效，且天然与 IterResearch/CoALA 类工作空间重建底座组合成"meta-harness"（内层负责确定性检测—恢复，外层负责长上下文记忆，未来还可由外层元控制器学习形状路由本身）。由此给出三条后续方向：(1) base-capability 前沿的可证伪预测——在前沿骨干（Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-4o）上检验结构化状态算子是否变得可用；(2) 在线学习形状路由（训练分类器或工具注册表上的元控制器），把手工 dispatcher 变为可自我扩展的 harness，同时保持确定性这一可靠性来源；(3) 领域扩展面透明——当某域瓶颈（如 QASPER 的 span 抽取 6–13%）不在 harness 覆盖内时，其精度上限由该能力而非 harness 决定，新增形状与工具是"即插即用"操作。

Abstract: Current reasoning paradigms for LLMs include chain-of-thought, ReAct, and post-hoc self-critique. These paradigms rely on two assumptions that fail on long-horizon, multi-stage tasks. As a result, errors accumulate silently across reasoning steps, leaving an open question: can a reasoning system effectively detect and recover from its own failures? We present ReFlect, a \emph{harness} system for LLM reasoning that creates standalone error detection and recovery logic as a deterministic wrapper around the model. Controlled experiments across 6 reasoning domains show that prompt-level self-critique produces formulaic templates that flag no issues in 90 of 100 audited reflection blocks, and the investigated LLMs wrongly accept a wrong answer in at least 76\% of cases. Our ReFlect harness achieves task success rates ranging from 41\% on gpt-4o-mini to 56\% on Claude Sonnet 4.5 across six models spanning small and frontier scale, with per-model gains over Direct CoT ranging from +7 pp on Qwen2.5-72B to +29 pp on Claude Sonnet 4.5, and additionally raises SWE-bench patch-structural quality from 0\% (Direct CoT) to between 82\% (Qwen2.5-72B) and 87\% (GPT-4o). Notably, the harness gain is inversely proportional to the model's Direct CoT task success rate (the fitted slope is -1.69 with r=-0.76): each pp lost in baseline success rate is mechanically recovered by 1.69 pp of harness gain. We spot that adding structured reasoning state and operators yields only 15.0--18.7\% pair-mean on Llama-3.3-70B and Qwen2.5-72B because models at this scale cannot reliably populate the state its operators require. ReFlect is model-agnostic, training-free, and operates entirely at inference time.

</details>


### [91] [TheraAgent: Self-Improving Therapeutic Agent for Precise and Comprehensive Treatment Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05963)
*Junkai Li,Yunghwei Lai,Tianyi Zhu,Zheng Long Lee,Weizhi Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TheraAgent是一个基于Agent架构的医疗治疗计划生成框架，通过迭代的生成-判断-优化管道替代传统的单次生成模式，有效提升了治疗计划的准确性、完整性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在治疗计划生成中存在推理粗糙、不完整且可能存在安全隐患的问题，因为它们主要依赖一次性输出而缺乏显式验证机制。治疗计划制定本质上是一个复杂的推理和优化任务，而非简单的生成问题。

Method: TheraAgent采用生成-判断-优化（Generate-Judge-Refine）的迭代管道，模拟人类专家迭代修订治疗计划的过程，逐步将粗糙、不完整的草案转化为精确、全面、安全的治疗方案。框架集成了TheraJudge模块，用于在推理循环中强制执行临床标准评估。

Result: 在HealthBench基准上达到最优水平，准确性（Accuracy）和完整性（Completeness）指标领先。在专家评估中，86%的情况下优于人类医生，靶向性（Targeting）和危害控制（Harm Control）表现优异。TheraJudge与HealthBench评估的高度一致性验证了框架的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过引入迭代式Agent架构和专门的治疗计划评估模块，TheraAgent显著提升了LLM在临床治疗规划中的实用性、安全性和专业性，为医疗AI系统提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Formulating a treatment plan is inherently a complex reasoning and refinement task rather than a simple generation problem. However, existing large language models (LLMs) mainly rely on one-shot output without explicit verification, which may result in rough, incomplete, and potentially unsafe treatment plans. To address these limitations, we propose TheraAgent, an agentic framework that replaces one-shot generation with an iterative generate-judge-refine pipeline. By mirroring the actual reasoning process of human experts who iteratively revise treatment plans, our framework progressively transforms coarse and incomplete drafts into precise, comprehensive, and safer therapeutic regimens. To facilitate the critical judge component, we introduce TheraJudge, a treatment-specific evaluation module integrated into the inference loop to enforce clinical standards. Experiments show TheraAgent achieves state-of-the-art results on HealthBench, leading in Accuracy and Completeness. In expert evaluations, it attains an 86% win rate against physicians, with superior Targeting and Harm Control. Moreover, the highly agreement between TheraJudge and HealthBench evaluations confirms the reliability of our framework.

</details>


### [92] [Novelty-based Tree-of-Thought Search for LLM Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06040)
*Leon Hamm,Zlatan Ajanovic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索将宽度优先搜索中的"新颖性"概念迁移到语言领域，以改进思维树(ToT)推理。提出一种可测量的新颖性指标，衡量新节点与搜索树中先前节点的差异程度，从而实现剪枝和降低搜索范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管链式思维、树状思维和强化学习等方法提升了LLM的推理规划能力，但它们仍存在脆弱性问题，未能达到人类水平的推理性能，且往往伴随高昂的时间和token消耗成本。

Method: 提出一种基于LLM提示的新颖性度量方法，利用预训练嵌入的通用知识来估计新思维节点相对于搜索树中已见节点的唯一性。该指标可应用于思维树搜索中的分支剪枝，有效缩减搜索空间。

Result: 尽管每个状态需要更多提示，但通过剪枝和缩小整体树规模，实现了总体token成本的降低。该方法在多个语言规划和通用推理基准测试中进行了测试和比较。

Conclusion: 新颖性可作为语言领域树状思维推理的有效剪枝策略，在保持性能的同时显著降低token消耗。

Abstract: Although advances such as chain-of-thought, tree-of-thought or reinforcement learning have improved the performance of LLMs in reasoning and planning tasks, they are still brittle and have not achieved human-level performance in many domains, and often suffer from high time and token costs. Inspired by the success of width-based search in planning, we explore how the concept of novelty can be transferred to language domains and how it can improve tree-of-thought reasoning. A tree of thoughts relies on building possible "paths" of consecutive ideas or thoughts. These are generated by repeatedly prompting an LLM. In our paper, a measurable concept of novelty is proposed that describes the uniqueness of a new node (thought) in comparison to nodes previously seen in the search tree. Novelty is estimated by prompting an LLM and making use of embedded general knowledge from pre-training. This metric can then be used to prune branches and reduce the scope of the search. Although this method introduces more prompts per state, the overall token cost can be reduced by pruning and reducing the overall tree size. This procedure is tested and compared using several benchmarks in language-based planning and general reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [On Time, Within Budget: Constraint-Driven Online Resource Allocation for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06110)
*Xinglin Wang,Zishen Liu,Shaoxiong Feng,Peiwen Yuan,Yiwei Li,Jiayi Shi,Yueqi Zhang,Chuyi Tan,Ji Zhang,Boyuan Pan,Yao Hu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCPP是一种用于智能体工作流资源分配的闭环规划方法，通过模拟执行直接估计约束完成概率，在保持预算和截止时间约束的同时最大化工作流成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在现实部署中面临具体的预算和截止时间约束，需要在满足这些约束的前提下最大化整个工作流成功完成的概率，而传统的平均效率优化方法无法直接解决这类硬约束问题。

Method: MCPP采用轻量级闭环规划器，通过蒙特卡洛模拟工作流执行来估计约束完成概率，并在观察到的结果后进行重规划，在管理剩余预算和时间的同时跨同时可执行的子任务分配模型和并行样本。

Result: 在CodeFlow和ProofFlow基准测试中，MCPP在广泛的预算-截止时间约束范围内始终提高约束完成概率，优于多个强基线方法。

Conclusion: MCPP通过模拟工作流执行和闭环重规划，直接估计约束完成概率，在多种预算-截止时间约束下始终优于基线方法，证明了该方法在智能体工作流资源分配中的有效性。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly solve complex user requests by executing orchestrated workflows, where subtasks are assigned to specialized models or tools and coordinated according to their dependencies. While recent work improves agent efficiency by optimizing the performance--cost--latency frontier, real deployments often impose concrete requirements: a workflow must be completed within a specified budget and before a specified deadline. This shifts the goal from average efficiency optimization to maximizing the probability that the entire workflow completes successfully under explicit budget and deadline constraints. We study \emph{constraint-driven online resource allocation for agentic workflows}. Given a dependency-structured workflow and estimates of success rates and generation lengths for each subtask--model pair, the executor allocates models and parallel samples across simultaneously executable subtasks while managing the remaining budget and time. We formulate this setting as a finite-horizon stochastic online allocation problem and propose \emph{Monte Carlo Portfolio Planning} (MCPP), a lightweight closed-loop planner that directly estimates constrained completion probability through simulated workflow executions and replans after observed outcomes. Experiments on CodeFlow and ProofFlow demonstrate that MCPP consistently improves constrained completion probability over strong baselines across a wide range of budget--deadline constraints.

</details>


### [94] [Rethinking Adapter Placement: A Dominant Adaptation Module Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06183)
*Suoxin Zhang,Run He,Di Fang,Xiang Tan,Kaixuan Chen,Huiping Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAGE（投影适配器梯度能量），一种基于梯度的敏感性探测方法，用于确定在预训练模型中放置少量LoRA适配器的最优位置。研究发现PAGE高度集中于单个浅层FFN下投影层，称之为"主导适配模块"。基于此发现，提出DomLoRA方法，仅使用约0.7%的 vanilla LoRA可训练参数即可超越其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LoRA方法将适配器广泛分布在模型各层，但研究表明使用更少的适配器仍可保持甚至提升性能。然而，如何在有限的适配器数量下找到最优放置位置以最大化性能的问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 提出PAGE（Projected Adapter Gradient Energy）：一种基于梯度的敏感性探测方法，用于估计每个候选LoRA适配器的初始可训练梯度能量。2. 通过PAGE分析发现，在两个模型家族和四个下游任务中，PAGE高度集中于单个浅层FFN下投影层。3. 提出DomLoRA：将单个适配器放置在主导适配模块的位置。

Result: 主导适配模块的层索引具有架构依赖性但任务稳定性。DomLoRA仅使用~0.7%的vanilla LoRA可训练参数，在指令遵循、数学推理、代码生成和多轮对话等多个下游任务上平均性能优于vanilla LoRA。

Conclusion: 主导适配模块是一个实用的放置策略指南，可应用于其他LoRA变体的改进。研究表明，通过精确放置少量适配器而非广泛分布，可以实现更好的参数效率和性能。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method that places trainable low-rank adapters into frozen pre-trained models. Recent studies show that using fewer LoRA adapters may still maintain or even improve performance, but existing methods still distribute adapters broadly, leaving where to place a limited number of adapters to maximize performance largely open. To investigate this, we introduce PAGE (Projected Adapter Gradient Energy), a gradient-based sensitivity probe that estimates the initial trainable gradient energy available to each candidate LoRA adapter. Surprisingly, we find that PAGE is highly concentrated on a single shallow FFN down-projection across two model families and four downstream tasks. We term this module the dominant adaptation module and show that its layer index is architecture-dependent but task-stable. Motivated by this finding, we propose DomLoRA, a placement method that places a single adapter at the dominant adaptation module. With only ~0.7% of vanilla LoRA's trainable parameters, DomLoRA outperforms it on average across various downstream tasks, including instruction following, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-turn conversation. This method also improves other LoRA variants, supporting the dominant adaptation module perspective as a practical placement guideline.

</details>


### [95] [OPSD Compresses What RLVR Teaches: A Post-RL Compaction Stage for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06188)
*Jaehoon Kim,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究OPSD（On-Policy Self-Distillation）在数学推理任务中的失效问题，发现OPSD主要起压缩作用而非纠正作用，并提出新的训练流程：SFT→RLVR→OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD作为RLVR的替代方案，在思考型数学推理任务中表现不佳，准确率增益减少甚至变负，需要理解其根本原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 将OPSD分别应用于正确和错误rollout组，隔离观察压缩和纠正效果；在思考启用的数学推理任务上进行实验。

Result: OPSD在思考启用的数学推理中最可靠地表现为压缩机制而非纠正机制：仅在正确rollout上训练可保持准确率同时显著缩短响应，而仅在错误rollout上训练会损害准确率。

Conclusion: 提出思考型数学推理的后训练流程：SFT → RLVR → OPSD，即先用监督微调，再用强化学习微调，最后用OPSD压缩。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has recently emerged as an alternative to Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), promising higher accuracy and shorter responses through token-level credit assignment from a self-teacher conditioned on privileged context. However, this promise does not carry over to thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, where reported accuracy gains shrink and sometimes turn negative. We hypothesize that hindsight supervision can specify better token-level alternatives in short thinking-disabled outputs, but in long thinking-enabled traces it more readily identifies redundancy than supplies better replacements. To test this, we applied OPSD separately to correct and incorrect rollout groups, so that compression and correction can be observed in isolation. Our results show that in thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning, OPSD behaves most reliably as a compression mechanism rather than a correction mechanism: training only on correct rollouts preserves accuracy while substantially shortening responses, whereas training only on incorrect rollouts damages accuracy. In light of these findings, we propose a revised post-training pipeline for thinking-enabled mathematical reasoning: SFT then RLVR then OPSD.

</details>


### [96] [The Granularity Axis: A Micro-to-Macro Latent Direction for Social Roles in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06196)
*Chonghan Qin,Xiachong Feng,Ziyun Song,Xiaocheng Feng,Jing Xiong,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了 LLM 中的「粒度轴」（Granularity Axis）：一个由宏观角色与微观角色平均隐藏状态之差得到的对比式潜在方向。在 Qwen3-8B 中该方向与角色表示空间的主成分 PC1 余弦相似度达 0.972、解释 52.6% 方差，且角色在其上的投影在 5 个粒度层级上单调递增；该结构在层、提示模板、留出集、评分过滤下稳定，并迁移到 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct。激活steering沿该轴能按预期方向改变输出粒度，但受各模型默认工作区间的制约（Qwen 保守稳定、Llama 响应更强但更易退化）。结论是社会角色粒度是模型内部有序、可被因果操控的表示原语，而非表层风格特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 经常被提示扮演从个体到机构、国家等不同社会规模的角色（担忧的家长、社区组织者、医院管理者、中央银行行长等）。现有研究不清楚模型内部是否真正区分这些角色的「粒度」（个体经验 vs 组织/制度/国家层面的系统性推理），还是仅靠一套共享的「角色扮演模板」产生风格差异。这一区分对 LLM 作为社会模拟器的有效性至关重要：若中央银行行长的回答与担忧的家长共享同一表示先验，那么文本上「多视角」的辩论在表示层面会坍缩为单一视角，即作者所称的「粒度混淆」（granularity confusion）。作者假设：如果角色在粒度上系统性不同，这种差异应在激活空间中表现为单一、有序的潜在方向，而非分散的角色簇。基于此提出可证伪的预测，并希望为多智能体模拟审计、部署时社会尺度控制、以及推广到其他分级社会维度（正式度、时间跨度、风险偏好）奠定基础。

Method: （1）角色构建：人工标注 75 个社会角色，划分为 5 个粒度层级（L1 个体、L2 群体/社区、L3 组织、L4 制度、L5 国家/超国家行为体），每层 15 个，附领域、角色类型、名称与描述元数据。（2）响应收集：每个角色配 5 种提示变体（身份指派、角色扮演指令、世界观强调、第一人称/时间跨度强调、真实性/实践约束强调），与 Assistant Axis 论文的 240 道通用抽取题组合，生成 75×5×240=90,000 条角色条件响应，另加 1,200 条默认助手响应，共 91,200 条；对角色扮演忠实度做 0–3 分评分用于过滤消融。（3）表示与轴构造：逐层提取生成 token 激活，对助手轮做均值池化得到响应向量，再对角色内的 (s,q) 求平均得角色级向量；定义粒度轴 g(ℓ) = 宏观角色均值（L4、L5）− 微观角色均值（L1、L2）。（4）几何验证：将 75 个角色向量中心化后做 PCA，报告 cos(g, PC1) 及各层级投影的均值、Spearman/Pearson 相关；对照轴包括 Assistant Axis、随机方向、替代端点定义。（5）因果探针：在 Layer 18 对生成 token 施加 h ← h + α·g（α ∈ {−4,0,+4}，仅作用于生成 token），在 40 条通用提示与 12 条 micro-targeted 提示上评估，主判官为 gpt-5.4-mini（gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview 做稳健性检查），记录 1–5 粒度分、退化率、响应长度；辅以 3 名盲评人类标注者做成对方向判断与 Likert 一致性。（6）稳健性：层扫描、端点消融、提示/问题/角色留出、模板消融、评分过滤、通用 vs 具体角色对照、领域与家族分层。

Result: 表示层面结论稳健：在 Qwen3-8B 的 Layer 18，粒度轴与 PC1 余弦 0.9720，解释 52.57% 角色空间方差，投影与层级序的 Spearman 0.9472、Pearson 0.9414；Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 对应为 0.9596、42.46%、0.9459、0.9373。两模型各层均值投影从 L1 到 L5 单调递增（L4–L5 出现饱和，跨约 7 倍绝对尺度差仍呈相同的「上升后饱和」形状），默认助手位于中宏观区域（Qwen 近 L3、投影 18.2572；Llama 近 L4、投影 2.5880），说明默认助手并非偏微观。稳健性上，Qwen 在 Layer 8–35、Llama 在 6–31 保持单调；替代端点余弦 0.93–1.00；提示留出 0.9468、问题留出 0.9494（Qwen）、角色留出 0.9335 但 Qwen 单调性不完美；五种提示变体均单调（0.9381–0.9568）；评分 ≥2/≥3 过滤后相关性略升；Assistant Axis 与随机方向无法复现粒度移动，排除了「任意扰动隐状态」的通用解释。行为层面结论为部分成立：Qwen 通用提示基线已饱和于 4.900，正向 steering 无headroom，微向提示上 α=+4 时 2.167→2.667 且无退化；Llama 微向提示 2.000→3.167，通用提示 4.525→3.125（α=−4）但退化率达 0.425，较大系数（|α|≥8–16）出现重复/无意义输出。人类成对判断在四个条件下均高于随机（0.639–0.903），与 LLM 判官 Spearman ρ∈[0.58,0.79]、Krippendorff α 0.60–0.78。

Conclusion: 社会角色粒度是 LLM 内部可区分、有序、可因果操纵的表示原语，而非仅仅表层文风变量：角色条件化作用在一个连续的社会尺度流形上，而非离散人格库。作者强调三点方法论教训：（1）先在表示层用留出的中间层级验证分级潜在方向，再解读 steering 行为——方向可能在表示上稳健而在行为上脆弱；（2）steering 结果必须结合基线在轴上的位置（headroom）、系数扫描阈值与逐条件退化率来解读，「更大的响应幅度不等于更干净的控制」；（3）单一对比轴只能揭示端点分离的主方向，规模、时间跨度、权威、正式度等相关维度可能塌缩进同一轴，需将角色向量投影残差后再做 PCA 以检验是否存在正交的次级结构（建议作为子空间处理）。局限包括仅两个 8B 指令模型、人工 75 角色分类法、LLM 评判为主，L4–L5 饱和区需进一步研究。作者将工作定位为三类应用的第一步：审计多智能体模拟中的粒度混淆、在部署时控制社会尺度、把「对比—投影」流程推广到其他分级社会维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are routinely prompted to take on social roles ranging from individuals to institutions, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations encode the granularity of such roles, from micro-level individual experience to macro-level organizational, institutional, or national reasoning. We show that they do. We define a contrast-based Granularity Axis as the difference between mean macro- and micro-role hidden states. In Qwen3-8B, this axis aligns with the principal axis (PC1) of the role representation space at cosine 0.972 and accounts for 52.6% of its variance, indicating that granularity is the dominant geometric axis organizing prompted social roles. We construct 75 social roles across five granularity levels and collect 91,200 role-conditioned responses over shared questions and prompt variants, then extract role-level hidden states and project them onto the axis. Role projections increase monotonically across all five levels, remain stable across layers, prompt variants, endpoint definitions, held-out splits, and score-filtered subsets, and transfer to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The axis is also causally relevant: activation steering along it shifts response granularity in the predicted direction, with Llama moving from 2.00 to 3.17 on a five-point macro scale under positive steering on prompts that admit local responses. The two models differ in controllability, suggesting that steering depends on each model's default operating regime. Overall, our findings suggest that social role granularity is not merely a stylistic surface feature, but a structured, ordered, and causally manipulable latent direction in role-conditioned language model behavior.

</details>


### [97] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将激活修补（activation patching）轮廓表示为修补效果图（patch-effect graphs），通过图机器学习方法分析transformer的因果电路。三个图构建方法：直接因果中介（direct-influence）、偏相关（partial-correlation）和共影响（co-influence）。在GPT-2 Small的IOI任务上验证，图结构保留判别信号，局部边特征优于全局图形状描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）通过激活修补识别因果电路，但跨多样化提示和任务家族扩展这些干预会产生高维、非结构化数据集，难以系统比较。

Method: 将激活修补档案重新表述为模型组件上的修补效果图（patch-effect graphs），引入三种图构建方法（直接因果中介、偏相关、共影响），并应用图核分析结构。

Result: 修补效果图保留判别结构信号；局部边槽特征比全局图形状描述符具有更高分类准确率；CI和PC选中的候选边比随机或低秩候选具有更强的激活影响效应；图特征压缩结构化修补信号，而原始张量和表面线索构成强基线。

Conclusion: 该框架为在控制基线下比较修补派生结构提供了压缩和评估流程，将稳健的切片判别证据与更强的任务通用因果电路主张区分开来。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [98] [SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614)
*Siru Ouyang,Jun Yan,Yanfei Chen,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Yizhu Jiao,Kaiwen Zha,Maohao Shen,Vishy Tirumalashetty,George Lee,Jiawei Han,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOS是一种基于经验驱动的强化学习训练方法,用于学习自我进化智能体中的技能管理。核心思想是将智能体执行器(检索和应用技能)与可训练的技能管理器分离,通过设计复合奖励和基于技能相关任务依赖的分组训练流来提供学习信号,使技能库随时间演化成结构更丰富的Markdown文件,编码更高级的元技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体多为一次性问题解决者,无法从过去交互中学习。技能管理面临手动管理费时、启发式操作不够灵活、短视野训练难以学习复杂长期管理策略等瓶颈,尤其在间接和延迟反馈场景下表现不佳。

Method: 提出SkillOS框架:1)冻结执行器负责检索和应用技能;2)可训练的管理器基于累积经验更新外部技能库。设计复合奖励函数,并基于技能相关任务依赖对任务流分组,早期轨迹更新技能库,后期相关任务评估更新效果。

Result: 在多轮智能体任务和单轮推理任务中,SkillOS在有效性和效率上均优于无记忆基线和强记忆基线。学到的管理器展现出更精准的技能使用,且技能库逐渐演化成结构更丰富的Markdown文件,编码更高层次的元技能。

Conclusion: 技能管理器具有良好的泛化能力,可跨不同执行器骨架和任务领域迁移。该工作验证了通过经验驱动的强化学习实现自我进化智能体的可行性,为构建持续学习的LLM智能体提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.

</details>


### [99] [MASPO: Joint Prompt Optimization for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06623)
*Zhexuan Wang,Xuebo Liu,Li Wang,Zifei Shan,Yutong Wang,Zhenxi Song,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASPO 是一个面向 LLM 多智能体系统（MAS）的提示（prompt）联合自动优化框架。其核心是用「多粒度联合评估」（Local Validity + Lookahead Potential + Global Alignment）替代依赖真值标签的局部评估来破解信用分配难题；用「失准感知采样」挖掘“局部正确但全局失败”的历史轨迹作为硬负例引导生成；用「坐标上升式拓扑调度 + Beam Refresh 刷新」缓解上游改动导致的下游输入分布漂移（非平稳性）。以 Qwen3-8B 为智能体骨干、Gemini-2.5-Pro 为优化/评估引擎，在 6 个基准上平均准确率比最强优化基线提升 2.9 个点（顺序型 MAS 70.39、层级型 71.05），并可零训练迁移到 DeepSeek-V3/GLM-4.6/Claude-Sonnet-4/Gemini-2.5-Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统（MAS）依赖角色化 system prompt 来定义分工与交互，但其提示的「联合优化」几乎空白：① 搜索空间是 S^N 的组合爆炸高维空间，且提示离散不可微；② 智能体之间功能耦合，改动上游 p_j 会改变下游 v_i 的输入上下文 C_i，形成协变量漂移与非平稳优化景观；③ 信用分配难题——中间智能体只负责推理/反思/总结，最终真值 o*_glob 只提供稀疏监督，难以归因到单步；④ 关键失效模式「Local-Global Misalignment」：某智能体完美满足本地指令却误导下游导致系统级失败。已有方案的问题：依赖真值标签的自监督方法（Xiang et al. 2025）作用域孤立，无法捕捉局部变化对全局的传播；TPE 类方法（MIPRO、MASS）只能从固定离散候选池中选择，无法开放式细粒度生成；单智能体提示优化器（SPO 等）无法处理非平稳的协作环境。

Method: **形式化**：MAS 表示为有向通信图 G=(V,E)，每个智能体 v_i 持有 LLM 推理函数 f_i 与可学习提示 p_i，输出 o_i=f_i(p_i, q, C_i)，C_i 由前驱输出按固定拓扑序拼接；目标为 argmax_P E[R(Φ(G,P,q), o*_glob)]。

**三大机制**：
1. **多粒度联合奖励（解决信用分配）**：R = α·I(o'_i ≻ o_i)[Local Validity] + θ·I(o'_glob ≻ o_glob)[Global Alignment] + β·(1/|N_out(v_i)|)Σ_{v_j∈N_out(v_i)} I(o'_j ≻ o_j)[Lookahead Potential]。≻ 由评估模型 M_eval 输出的成对偏好判定，不需真值标签；权重默认 α=0.4, β=0.4, θ=0.2。
2. **失准感知采样（Misalignment-Aware Sampling）**：当样本满足「局部更优」但「Lookahead 或 Global 不优」时判为 Misalignment Case，存入缓冲 B_mis，在生成时优先注入 K_mis=3 条此类硬负例作为 few-shot 轨迹引导优化器 M_opt 生成针对协作断裂的后代提示。
3. **自适应动态（演化束搜索）**：拓扑序上的坐标上升 + 交错演化（每轮每智能体 T=3 代，共 D=3 轮）；维护 Top-K 束（K=2），每个父代生成 K_sub=2 个变体，累计收益 J(p')=R(p',p_parent)+J(p_parent) 沿路径累加以抑制单样本噪声；**Beam Refresh** 机制在下一 epoch 重访智能体时丢弃过期分数，用相对当前全局最优 p_best 的中心化胜率 J_new(p)=R(p,p_best)-0.5 重锚定束，消除因上游演进而失效的历史评分；采用 Gauss-Seidel 式全局同步，优于者立即广播给后续智能体。
**评测模型**：Gemini-2.5-Pro 同时担任优化器 M_opt（温度 0.7）与评估器 M_eval（温度 0），智能体骨干为 Qwen3-8B（温度 0，非思考模式）；无标注样本池 |D|=50，每轮随机采 mini-batch 10 条收集轨迹与评估。

Result: **主结果**：6 个基准（MATH-500、AGIEval-MATH L5、AQuA、GPQA-Diamond、MBPP、HumanEval-ET）上，Sequential MAS 上 MASPO 达 70.39 平均准确率（对比 Vanilla 65.27、CoT 65.86、SC(CoT) 66.48、Self-Refine 66.27、AgentDropout 66.89、TPE 66.49、SPO 66.56），比最佳优化基线提升 2.90；Hierarchical MAS 上达 71.05（基线 68.32，+TPE 68.47，+SPO 69.01），提升 2.73。GPQA 上提升最显著（58.08 vs SPO 49.52，Hierarchical 下 54.04 vs 51.01）。

**消融**：Serial Search 68.10、Single Cycle 68.19、去掉 Beam Refresh 68.53、去掉 Joint Evaluate 67.77、去掉 Misalignment Sampling 69.68（均低于完整版 70.39）；用「成功案例采样」反而低于随机采样（69.29），验证硬负例信息增益更高；K_mis=3 最优（2/4/5 分别 69.77/69.72/69.83）。**机制解释**：跨轮次束候选评分的 Kendall top-1 重叠率仅 0.63，证实分数陈旧/分布漂移的存在。**Lookahead 深度**：2 步 70.42、3 步 70.41，相对 1 步 70.39 无实质提升，说明 Global Alignment 已捕获长程依赖。**公平性检验**：给 SPO 相同搜索预算或相同 Gemini 调用预算，仍只有 67.79/67.86，说明增益源自算法而非算力堆砌。**鲁棒性**：以 Qwen3-8B 自任优化/评估仍达 67.70；最小化初始化 69.95、错领域初始化 69.72，优于所有基线。**迁移性**：将 Qwen3-8B 上优化的提示零训练迁移，DeepSeek-V3 71.79→75.86、GLM-4.6 75.61→78.41、Claude-Sonnet-4 77.58→79.73、Gemini-2.5-Pro 84.93→87.14。**兼容性**：与结构优化框架 AgentDropout 叠加后 66.89→70.46，说明拓扑优化与提示优化正交可叠加。**样本池规模**：|D|=30/50/70 分别 69.13/70.39/70.43，超过 50 后边际收益饱和。

Conclusion: MASPO 证明，面向 MAS 的提示联合优化可以在完全不依赖真值标签的前提下完成，关键在于三点：把评估从「局部正确」升级为「局部正确 + 拓扑有用 + 全局对齐」的多粒度联合奖励；把「局部成功但系统失败」的失准案例当作高信息量硬负例显式注入搜索；以及用坐标上升调度 + Beam Refresh 主动对抗上游演化带来的输入分布漂移。该框架拓扑无关、初始化鲁棒、可跨模型迁移并与结构优化正交叠加，为动态角色专精与协作式 AI 系统的架构优化提供了可扩展的自动化路径。局限在于依赖强优化/评估模型（Gemini-2.5-Pro）的成对偏好判定、评估成本较高，且 Lookahead 仅一步、拓扑固定、提示长度与格式约束（250/300 词以内）可能限制表达空间。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based Multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in tackling complex collaborative tasks, where agents are typically orchestrated via role-specific prompts. While the quality of these prompts is pivotal, jointly optimizing them across interacting agents remains a non-trivial challenge, primarily due to the misalignment between local agent objectives and holistic system goals. To address this, we introduce MASPO, a novel framework designed to automatically and iteratively refine prompts across the entire system. A core innovation of MASPO is its joint evaluation mechanism, which assesses prompts not merely by their local validity, but by their capacity to facilitate downstream success for successor agents. This effectively bridges the gap between local interactions and global outcomes without relying on ground-truth labels. Furthermore, MASPO employs a data-driven evolutionary beam search to efficiently navigate the high-dimensional prompt space. Extensive empirical evaluations across 6 diverse tasks demonstrate that MASPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art prompt optimization methods, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 2.9. We release our code at https://github.com/wangzx1219/MASPO.

</details>


### [100] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper introduces ScaleLogic, a synthetic framework controlling logical reasoning difficulty along proof depth and expressiveness axes. It demonstrates that RL training compute scales as a power law with reasoning depth (T ∝ D^γ), with exponent γ increasing 1.04→2.60 as logic becomes more expressive. More expressive training settings yield better and more efficient transfer to downstream math/reasoning tasks, highlighting that training content significantly impacts generalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Systematic study of how RL training scales with task difficulty has been hindered by lack of controlled, scalable environments. Understanding the relationship between training complexity and computational requirements is crucial for developing efficient LLM reasoning strategies.

Method: ScaleLogic framework enables controlled manipulation of two difficulty axes: proof planning depth (horizon) and logical expressiveness across 5 logic types. The study trains RL agents with different configurations and measures compute requirements, then evaluates transfer on downstream math and reasoning benchmarks. Power-law analysis and curriculum learning experiments are conducted.

Result: RL training compute follows a power law with reasoning depth (T ∝ D^γ, R² > 0.99). The scaling exponent γ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness (1.04 to 2.60). More expressive training yields +10.66 point improvements on downstream benchmarks and more compute-efficient transfer. The power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

Conclusion: The study establishes that training expressiveness significantly impacts downstream transfer, and curriculum-based training can substantially improve scaling efficiency. The findings highlight the importance of what (not just how much) language models are trained on for reasoning development.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [LLMorphism: When humans come to see themselves as language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05419)
*Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: LLMorphism指将人类认知类比为大型语言模型的偏误信念。随着对话式AI的普及，这种偏误通过类比迁移和隐喻可得性两种机制传播开来。本文将其与相关概念区分，并探讨其对多个社会领域的深远影响，指出公共讨论往往忽视了对人类心智的贬低问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式LLM的兴起，人们可能从"人工系统产生类人语言"这一现象反向推论"人类思维像LLM"，这种推断具有偏误性，因为语言输出的相似性并不必然意味着认知架构的相似性。

Method: 区分LLMorphism与机械论、拟人论、计算主义、去人性化、具身化以及预测加工心智理论等概念；分析其传播的两个机制——类比迁移（将LLM特征投射到人类）和隐喻可得性（LLM词汇成为描述思维的文化显著词汇）。

Result: LLMorphism对工作、教育、责任、医疗保健、沟通、创造力和人类尊严等多个领域产生影响，涉及边界条件和抵抗形式的讨论。

Conclusion: 公共讨论可能遗漏了问题的一半：问题不仅是是否对机器过度归因心智，也包括是否开始对人类过度归因心智缺失。LLMorphism的边界条件和抵抗形式值得进一步探讨。

Abstract: LLMorphism is the biased belief that human cognition works like a large language model. I argue that the rise of conversational LLMs may make this bias increasingly psychologically available. When artificial systems produce human-like language, people may draw a reverse inference: if LLMs can speak like humans, perhaps humans think like LLMs. This inference is biased because similarity at the level of linguistic output does not imply similarity in cognitive architecture. Yet, LLMorphism may spread through two mechanisms: analogical transfer, whereby features of LLMs are projected onto humans, and metaphorical availability, whereby LLM vocabulary becomes a culturally salient vocabulary for describing thought. I distinguish LLMorphism from mechanomorphism, anthropomorphism, computationalism, dehumanization, objectification, and predictive-processing theories of mind. I outline its implications for work, education, responsibility, healthcare, communication, creativity, and human dignity, while also discussing boundary conditions and forms of resistance. I conclude that the public debate may be missing half of the problem: the issue is not only whether we are attributing too much mind to machines, but also whether we are beginning to attribute too little mind to humans.

</details>


### [102] [The Pedagogy of AI Mistakes: Fostering Higher-Order Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05472)
*Hadi Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper reframes AI limitations as educational opportunities by integrating AI into a database design syllabus to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generative AI's inherent errors and hallucinations, typically viewed as drawbacks, can serve as deliberate triggers for critical thinking and reflection when properly structured in course design.

Method: Mixed-methods approach examining structured AI interaction in a database design course; analysis focuses on metacognitive engagement, disciplinary rigor, and perceived competency outcomes.

Result: Investigation of how AI-generated errors support metacognitive engagement, reinforce disciplinary rigor, and relate to students' AI literacy and subject-matter competency in database design education.

Conclusion: The paper argues that AI limitations can be systematically leveraged as pedagogical tools rather than mere obstacles, contributing to higher-order cognitive development aligned with established learning frameworks.

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly integrated into higher education, its frequent errors and hallucinations, often seen as limitations, offer a unique pedagogical opportunity. By framing AI as a ``learning companion'' whose imperfect outputs prompt analysis, evaluation, and reflection, we argue that instructors can engage students in the fundamental processes of higher-order thinking. This paper presents a design-oriented study in which an AI-integrated syllabus in a \textit{database design} course deliberately leverages AI's limitations to foster critical thinking and higher-order cognitive skills aligned with Bloom's taxonomy of learning. Using a mixed-methods approach, we examine how structured interaction with AI-generated errors supports metacognitive engagement, reinforces disciplinary rigor, and relates to students' perceived AI literacy and subject-matter competency.

</details>


### [103] [Rigorous Interpretation Is a Form of Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05508)
*Isabelle Lee,Emmy Liu,Cathy Jiao,Brihi Joshi,Dani Yogatama,Fazl Barez,Michael Saxon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出当前机器学习模型评估局限于表面性能指标的问题，主张可解释性应作为更深层次的模型评估手段，并探索其在识别根本原因、检测隐蔽故障、预测潜在问题三个方面的评估功能，同时强调可解释性方法需满足可证伪、可复现、可预测的科学标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型评估主要依赖行为快照式的基准准确率、胜率和结果指标，但这种表面评估无法深入理解模型产生特定行为的原因。论文认为若信任可解释性，它应能作为比表面性能指标更丰富、更原则性的模型评估形式。

Method: 探索可解释性作为模型评估的三种方式：(1) 通过识别问题根本原因来修复缺陷；(2) 检测使模型输出失效的微妙故障机制；(3) 通过全面理解模型弱点来预测潜在问题。

Result: 论证了可解释性不仅可作为诊断工具，更可成为深层次的模型评估手段；提出可解释性评估功能的三个维度；确立可解释性方法应满足的科学标准。

Conclusion: 为实现可解释性的评估潜力，必须建立科学标准：可解释性方法需产生可证伪、可复现、可预测的论断。这为可解释性研究指明了新的质量标准和发展方向。

Abstract: Current machine learning models are evaluated through behavioral snapshots, with benchmark accuracies, win rates and outcome-based metrics. Model explanations and evaluations, however, are fundamentally intertwined: understanding why a model produces a behavior can be as important as measuring what it produces. If we trusted interpretability, we argue that it can serve not merely as diagnostics but as a richer and more principled form of model evaluation beyond surface-level performance metrics. We explore three ways interpretability can function evaluatively: (1) fixing problems by identifying the root causes of unwanted behavior, (2) detecting subtly faulty mechanisms that invalidate model outputs, and (3) predicting potential issues before they arise by fully understanding the model's weaknesses. To fulfill its evaluative potential, we argue that interpretability methods must generate claims that are falsifiable, reproducible, and predictive -- that is, interpretability must meet scientific standards.

</details>


### [104] [The Missing Evaluation Axis: What 10,000 Student Submissions Reveal About AI Tutor Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05648)
*Rose Niousha,Samantha Boatright Smith,Bita Akram,Peter Brusilovsky,Arto Hellas,Juho Leinonen,John DeNero,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出将AI导师评估从单纯的教学法质量扩展到行为维度（基于学生互动数据），通过对10,235份代码提交的分析，发现不同AI导师在学生参与模式上存在显著差异，且行为信号比教学法质量更能预测学生感知，证明了行为评估的重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI导师系统的评估主要关注反馈信息的教学法质量，忽略了关键问题：学生收到反馈后实际做了什么。单纯依赖教学法评估是不够的，需要从学生行为角度进行补充性评估。

Method: 研究者提出了一套行为评估框架，并应用于10,235份代码提交及其对应AI导师反馈的数据集，比较了不同学期间部署的两个AI导师的表现差异，测量指标包括学生是否采取行动以及行动是否正确。

Result: 两个AI导师在学生参与模式上存在显著差异，这些差异在仅基于教学法质量的评估中无法被发现。此外，学生感知反馈有用性的预测因素中，行为信号比单纯的教学法质量关联性更强。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，行为维度的评估能够补充传统的教学法评估，提供更完整、更具可操作性的AI导师性能评估视角。建议未来AI教育系统的评估应将学生互动数据纳入考量。

Abstract: Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based tutoring systems (AI tutors) are primarily evaluated based on the pedagogical quality of their feedback messages. While important, pedagogy alone is insufficient because it ignores a critical question: what do students actually do with the feedback they receive? We argue that AI tutor evaluation should be extended with a behavioral dimension grounded in student interaction data, which complements pedagogical assessment. We propose an evaluation framework and apply it to 10,235 code submissions with corresponding AI tutor feedback from an introductory undergraduate programming course to measure whether students act on tutor feedback and whether those actions are applied correctly. Using this framework to compare two deployed AI tutors across different semesters in a large-scale introductory computer science course reveals substantial differences in student engagement patterns that are not captured by pedagogy-only evaluation. Moreover, these engagement-based behavioral signals are more strongly associated with student perception of helpful feedback than pedagogical quality alone, providing a more complete and actionable picture of AI tutor performance.

</details>


### [105] [Breaking In and Reaching Out: Networking for Women in Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06195)
*Shalini Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向 ACM womENcourage 2026 的**研讨会（workshop）征召/提案论文**，主题为计算机科学领域女性研究者的职业社交（networking）体验。作者延续其先前关于软件工程中社交网络不平等分布的研究，提出一个1.5小时、以社区驱动的互动式研讨会，围绕地理、资金、国籍与监管、会议时长、语言、性别与身份、性格与神经多样性、照护责任等八类因素展开讨论，目标是让被忽视的障碍"浮出水面"，并推动更包容、公平、可及的学术社交实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络关系（collaboration、visibility、mentorship、影响力）是计算机科学职业发展的关键，但社交机会的分布极不均衡。研究表明网络广度与发表量、合作数、被引影响正相关（Barabási, 2002；Crane & Kaplan, 1973），软件工程中的合作多萌生于会议、研讨会与同事情谊圈（Storey 等, 2010），而这些场合中的"走廊交谈"等非正式互动往往才是真正塑造合作与可见度的机制。然而结构性障碍使早期研究者、女性学者及全球南方研究者被边缘化：社会经济与地理差异决定谁能负担国际会议，签证限制与机构经费约束使中低收入国家研究者受限（Gülel, 2025）；英语作为主导语言使非母语者在非正式社交场合承受额外负担（Lillis & Curry, 2011）。作者在既有工作（Chakraborty, 2026）中论证：社交机会的分配偏向拥有资源、流动性和成熟学术环境者。本工作坊聚焦女性（尤其早期职业研究者）在多元背景下的实际体验、协商与感知，弥补既有研究仅识别结构性障碍、缺乏交叉性视角的缺口。

Method: 方法为定性研究议程设计（非新实证研究）。以作者先前论文提出的**因素框架**（factor-based framework）作为讨论脚手架并加以扩展，共八个维度：地理与位置、资金与其他资源、国籍与监管、会议时间与时长、语言与沟通、性别与社会身份、性格与神经多样性、照护状态与个人责任。数据来源方面，动机来自作者在 ICSE'26 开展的问卷调查及在巴西软件工程社区通过在线社交平台收集的后续定性/定量数据（Chakraborty, 2026），但论文明确声明**并非该研究的延伸**，而是将其洞见作为召集议题的动机。实施形式为1.5小时互动式社区讨论：15分钟引言（研究背景与框架介绍）、60分钟按因素分组的引导式圆桌（分享经历、反思影响、补充遗漏因素）、15分钟自愿反思与分享。为保障安全感全程不录音、无技术设备要求、强调参与自愿且不对研究产出承担义务。目标读者为参加 womENcourage 的女性学者、研究生及产业从业者，同时欢迎多元视角参与交叉性讨论。

Result: 本文本身**不产生新的实证结论**，其"结果"体现为：（1）明确了工作坊的三项具体目标——理解女性社交的亲身体验与挑战、识别被忽视或新出现的因素、推动软件工程社区社交实践的包容与公平；（2）交付了可操作的活动流程、目标受众画像与讨论维度清单；（3）贡献了一个将 SE/女性研究议题与 womENcourage 社群结合的社区驱动议程，为后续跨学科讨论（含软件工程之外的多样化因素）奠定框架。初步证据（ICSE'26 问卷与巴西社区数据）已在既有论文中报告，本工作坊的产出为集体性洞见与可及性改进建议。

Conclusion: 工作坊结论性立场是：社交不应被视为个体技能，而应理解为受结构性、社会与个人因素共同塑造的过程；女性在计算机科学中的社交障碍需要被具体化、可见化并由社区共同回应。通过围绕八类因素的无记录、非正式、社区主导对话，工作坊希望促成共享理解、验证多样经验，从而支撑更包容、公平、可及的网络实践——尤其为早期职业研究者提供决定性的支持。局限在于缺乏标准化的数据收集与产出机制，成果偏经验性与议程驱动；后续可通过会后匿名问卷或主题归纳将讨论转化为可量化的社区建议与政策输入。

Abstract: Networking is central to careers in computer science, where a globally distributed and diverse community increasingly collaborates across institutional and geographic boundaries, often in hybrid and remote settings. However, access to effective networking is shaped by structural and personal factors, including geography, funding, language, identity, personality, and caregiving responsibilities. Building on prior work, this workshop focuses on women in computing to examine lived experiences of networking and the barriers they encounter. Through a community-driven discussion grounded in a factor-based framework, the workshop aims to surface overlooked challenges and foster shared understanding. Ultimately, it seeks to inform more inclusive, equitable, and accessible networking practices within the computer science community.

</details>


### [106] [A Benchmark for Strategic Auditee Gaming Under Continuous Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06340)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将持续合规审计形式化为Stackelberg博弈，揭示了静态审计设计中覆盖缺口与粒度缺口不可同时关闭的结构性限制(Observation 1)，并通过Periodic-with-floor和怀疑升级两种策略从正交方向突破这一限制，OffAuditDrift策略则展现了对两者防御的克服能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴法规(EU AI Act和Digital Services Act)要求的持续部署后合规审计创造了与以往单次输入/输出博弈截然不同的战略性博弈类别。监管系统可通过延迟结果报告、在合理噪声范围内漂移报告、利用纵向样本流失、以及在模糊指标定义间进行选择性采样等方式进行策略性博弈。

Method: 将连续审计形式化为审计者承诺时序策略与自适应被审计者之间的T轮Stackelberg博弈；识别了噪声感知静态审计设计的结构性特征——"覆盖机制"，其中覆盖缺口和粒度缺口无法同时关闭(观察1)；提出两种最小扩展策略：(1)样本量感知的静态规则(Periodic-with-floor)关闭粒度失效案例；(2)历史条件化的怀疑升级策略关闭朴素Drift策略的覆盖失效案例；设计OffAuditDrift策略利用Stackelberg承诺击败两种防御。

Result: 验证了观察1的预测：两种最小扩展策略各自沿正交轴关闭覆盖机制，但均无法同时关闭两类失败；样本量感知的Periodic-with-floor规则有效关闭粒度失败案例，历史条件化怀疑升级策略有效关闭朴素Drift策略的覆盖失败案例；OffAuditDrift策略利用Stackelberg承诺结构击败了两种防御策略。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了一个非加性危害分解框架(W, C)，揭示了流失效应如何将危害从监管可见的表面转移到监管盲区；提供了5个审计对象策略库(Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift)和5个审计者策略库，基于DSA透明度数据库统计校准；并开发了具有可扩展Python接口的可复现模拟器，为连续审计的实证研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Continuous post-deployment compliance audits, mandated by emerging regulations such as the EU AI Act and Digital Services Act, create a class of strategic gaming distinct from the one-shot input/output gaming studied in prior work. Regulated systems can delay outcome reporting, drift their reports within plausible noise envelopes, exploit longitudinal sample attrition, and cherry-pick among ambiguous metric definitions. We formalize continuous auditing as a $T$-round Stackelberg game between an auditor that commits to a temporal policy and an adaptive auditee, and identify a structural feature of any noise-aware static-auditor design: a cover regime in which coverage gaps and granularity gaps cannot be closed simultaneously. We make this formal as Observation 1 and show that two minimal extension policies, each derived from the observation, close the regime along orthogonal axes: a sample-size-aware static rule (Periodic-with-floor) closes the granularity-failure case, while a history-conditioned suspicion-escalation policy closes the coverage-failure case for the naive Drift strategy -- and neither closes both, exactly as the observation predicts; an audit-aware OffAuditDrift strategy that exploits Stackelberg commitment defeats both. To support empirical study we contribute a non-additive harm decomposition (welfare loss $W$, coverage loss $C$) that exposes how attrition shifts harm from the regulator-accountable surface to a regulator-invisible one; an initial library of five auditee strategies (Delay, Drift, Cherry-pick, Attrition, OffAuditDrift) and five auditor policies, calibrated to summary statistics from published audits of the DSA Transparency Database; and a reproducible simulator with a small, extensible Python interface.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Near-Tight Approximation Algorithms for Bottleneck Multiple Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05233)
*Lin Chen,Tingwei Hu,Yuchen Mao,Yong Chen,Lili Mei,An Zhang,Guangting Chen,Guochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了瓶颈多背包问题，当knapsack容量相同时给出接近最优的(2/3 - ε)-近似算法；当容量任意时给出(1/2 - ε)-近似算法并证明(1/2 + ε)- hardness，两种情况下的近似比均几乎达到紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究瓶颈多背包问题的近似算法设计，目标是在容量约束下最大化每个knapsack获得的最小利润，这是一个具有实际应用价值的组合优化问题。

Method: 采用近似算法设计技术，针对两种knapsack容量场景分别设计：(1) 相同容量场景给出(2/3 - ε)-近似；(2) 任意容量场景给出(1/2 - ε)-近似。

Result: 相同容量knapsack：(2/3 - ε)-近似（几乎匹配(2/3 + ε)不可近似下界）；任意容量knapsack：(1/2 - ε)-近似且证明(1/2 + ε)不可近似。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在两种场景下均接近最优近似比：当knapsack容量相同时，几乎达到了不可近似下界(2/3 + ε)；当容量任意时，与下界(1/2 + ε)仅相差一个常数ε。

Abstract: In the bottleneck multiple knapsack problem, we are given a set of items and a set of knapsacks, where each item has a profit and a weight, and each knapsack has a capacity. Our goal is to assign items to knapsacks so as to maximize the minimum profit received by any knapsack subject to the capacity constraint.
  When all knapsacks have identical capacity, we give a $(\frac{2}{3} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. This result almost matches the $(\frac{2}{3} + \varepsilon)$ inapproximability bound for the bottleneck multiple subset sum problem (Caprara et al., 2000).
  When the knapsacks can have arbitrary capacities, we propose a $(\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. We also prove a hardness bound of $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)$ for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$.

</details>


### [108] [Algorithmic Phase Transition for Large Independent Sets in Dense Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05618)
*Abhishek Dhawan,Nhi U. Dinh,Eren C. Kızıldağ,Neeladri Maitra,Bayram A. Şahin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究密集随机超图中寻找大独立集的算法可行性。在稀疏情形下，局部/低阶多项式(LDP)框架有效；但密集情形需要根本不同的范式。研究两类模型：r-一致Erdős-Rényi超图和γ平衡r-部分超图中的独立集问题。设计了达到r^(1/(r-1))和(max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1))逼近比的在线算法，并证明这些逼近比是最优的——任何在线算法都无法突破这些下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The sparse regime uses local/LDP frameworks, but the dense regime requires fundamentally different algorithmic paradigms. Even for classical Erdős-Rényi graphs, LDPs are conjectured to fail in regimes accessible to online algorithms, necessitating new approaches.

Method: Design of online algorithms for both models, combined with hardness reductions proving lower bounds.

Result: Precise threshold values for the size of largest independent sets in both models, with online algorithms achieving multiplicative approximation factors of r^(1/(r-1)) (uniform) and (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) (r-partite).

Conclusion: Two online algorithms achieve optimal approximation ratios: r^(1/(r-1)) for uniform hypergraphs and (max_i γ_i)^(-1/(r-1)) for r-partite hypergraphs, with matching lower bounds proving no online algorithm can do better.

Abstract: We study the algorithmic tractability of finding large independent sets in dense random hypergraphs. In the sparse regime, much of the natural algorithms can be formulated within either the local or the low-degree polynomial (LDP) framework, and a rich literature has subsequently identified nearly sharp algorithmic thresholds within these classes by exploiting their stability. In the dense setting, however, the algorithmic paradigms are fundamentally different: they are online and thus need not be stable. Perhaps more crucially, even for the classical Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, LDPs are conjectured to fail in the 'easy' regime accessible to online algorithms, thereby challenging their viability for dense models.
  Our focus is on two models: (i) finding large independent sets in dense $r$-uniform Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs, and (ii) the more challenging problem of finding large $γ$-balanced independent sets in dense $r$-uniform $r$-partite hypergraphs, where the $i$-th coordinate of $γ\in\mathbb{Q}^r$ specifies the proportion of vertices from $V_i$ in the independent set. For both models, we pinpoint the size of the largest independent set and design online algorithms that achieve a multiplicative approximation factor of $r^{1/(r-1)}$ in the uniform and $(\max_i γ_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ in the $r$-partite model. Furthermore, we establish matching algorithmic lower bounds, showing that these computational gaps are sharp: no online algorithms can breach these gaps.

</details>


### [109] [Label Correcting Algorithms for the Multiobjective Temporal Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05954)
*Edina Marica,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究单源多目标时序最短路问题在目标函数不满足单调性和等渗性条件下的求解方法，通过引入最大路径长度约束K并开发标签校正算法克服零时长循环带来的挑战，证明了在特定条件下算法可计算所有非支配解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法依赖于目标函数的单调性（推广非负性假设）和等渗性（保证路径扩展时目标值顺序不变），但在更一般的多目标优化场景中这些假设难以满足，需要研究无此假设的问题求解方法。

Method: 开发了通用标签校正算法处理带最大路径长度K限制的问题变体，并建立了若干充分条件证明在特定条件下无需K界约束即可获得全部非支配解。

Result: 在无单调性和/或等渗性假设下，通过引入最大路径长度K的约束实现了对非支配解的有效搜索，并证明了在特定条件下无需K界算法即可完整求解。

Conclusion: 提出了一般标签校正算法，并为不需要界K的充分条件提供了理论支撑，证明了算法能够计算所有非支配图像。

Abstract: Given a directed, discrete-time temporal graph $G=(V,R)$, a start node $s\in V$, and $p\geq1$ objectives, the single-source multiobjective temporal shortest path problem asks, for each $v\in V$, for the set of nondominated images of temporal $s$-$v$-paths together with a corresponding efficient path for each image. A recent general label setting algorithm for this problem relies on two properties of the objectives - monotonicity and isotonicity. Monotonicity generalizes the nonnegativity assumption required by label setting methods for the classical additive single-objective shortest path problem on static graphs, while isotonicity ensures that the order of the objective values of two paths is preserved when both are extended by the same arc.
  In this paper, we study the problem without assuming monotonicity and/or isotonicity. A key difficulty in this setting is that zero-duration temporal cycles may need to be traversed an arbitrary finite number of times to generate all nondominated images. This motivates the study of a restricted problem variant in which a maximum admissible path length $K$ is imposed, and only paths containing at most $K$ arcs are considered. We develop general label correcting algorithms for this setting and establish several sufficient conditions under which such a bound is not required, implying that the algorithms compute all nondominated images.

</details>


### [110] [The Pareto Frontier of Randomized Learning-Augmented Online Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06106)
*Mathis Degryse,Imrane Saakour,Christoph Dürr,Spyros Angelopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了带oracle预测的随机在线竞价问题，提出bidding function统一框架，给出了一致性-鲁棒性权衡的紧致界（R≥2.885时），并通过增量中位数实验验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线竞价是online decision-making领域的经典问题，广泛应用于资源分配、分层聚类和近似算法分析。然而，learning-augmented variant下的随机在线竞价算法的理论极限尚不清晰，尤其是 consistency 与 robustness 之间的权衡关系存在理论gap。

Method: 提出并形式化了 bidding function 概念，作为随机竞价策略设计与分析的统一框架。基于该框架，推导了一致性 C 关于鲁棒性 R 的上界和下界，并通过巧妙的构造证明了这些界的紧致性。

Result: 给出了 C(R) 的解析上界与下界，当 R ≥ 2.885 时上下界匹配，完整刻画了该权衡关系。同时将算法应用于增量中位数问题，实验表明该方法在实用聚类场景中有效。

Conclusion: 通过提出 bidding function 抽象框架，解决了先前工作中存在的不一致性问题。当鲁棒性 R ≥ 2.885 时，上下界匹配，有效关闭了理论gap。此外，通过增量中位数问题的实验验证了该算法在实际聚类场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Online bidding is a classical problem in online decision-making, with applications in resource allocation, hierarchical clustering, and the analysis of approximation algorithms. We study its randomized learning-augmented variant, where an online algorithm generates a sequence of random bids while leveraging predictions from an oracle. We provide analytical upper and lower bounds on the optimal consistency $C$ as a function of the robustness $R$, which match when $R \geq 2.885$, effectively closing the gap left by previous work. The key technical ingredient is the notion of a bidding function, a novel abstraction that provides a unified framework for the design and analysis of randomized bidding strategies. We complement our theoretical results with an experimental application of randomized bidding to the incremental median problem, demonstrating the applicability of our algorithm in practical clustering settings.

</details>


### [111] [On the Parameterized Approximability of (Mergeable) Sum of Radii Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06398)
*Ameet Gadekar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了k球半径和问题(k-MSR)的参数化复杂性和算法设计。证明k-MSR是W[2]-难的，排除了高效参数化近似方案；同时给出FPT (8/3+ε)-近似算法，改进自(4+ε)，并通过合适赋值子程序达到(2+ε)-近似，可应用于多种公平性和隐私约束聚类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-MSR问题尽管已被广泛研究，但在参数化复杂性理论中的精确位置仍不清楚，且是否存在高效参数化近似方案（EPAS）这一基础问题未得到解答。同时，现有的约束聚类变体的参数化算法性能也有提升空间。

Method: 1. 硬度方面：通过从支配集问题的归约证明W[2]-硬度，并基于此排除EPAS和sub-exponential时间算法。2. 算法方面：基于可合并约束框架，利用集合覆盖/覆盖算法的技术，结合问题结构设计迭代式聚类方法，获得常数因子FPT近似。

Result: 1. 硬度结果：k-MSR是W[2]-难的，排除了EPAS（除非W[2]=FPT），且在ETH假设下排除了在f(k,ε)·n^{o(k)}时间内运行的算法。2. 算法结果：获得FPT (8/3+ε)-近似，改进了之前的(4+ε)上界；借助合适的赋值子程序可达到(2+ε)-近似，并推广到(t,k)-fair、(α,β)-fair、ℓ-diversity和私有聚类等问题。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了k-MSR问题的参数化复杂性定位，并提供了FPT近似算法的新记录。在约束聚类问题上达到与无约束问题相同的(2+ε)近似比，是一个重要的理论突破。

Abstract: The sum of radii problem ($k$-MSR) asks, given a metric space on $n$ points, to place $k$ balls covering all points so as to minimize the sum of their radii. Despite extensive study from the perspectives of approximation and parameterized algorithms, the exact parameterized complexity of the problem and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes remained open. We advance this understanding on both the hardness and algorithmic fronts.
  We begin by showing that $k$-MSR is $W[2]$-hard parameterized by $k$, thereby pinpointing its location in the $W$-hierarchy. Moreover, via our reduction, we rule out efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS)--that is, $(1+ε)$-approximations running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot \mathrm{poly}(n)$--unless $W[2] = FPT$. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, we further rule out such algorithms running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot n^{o(k)}$, strengthening recent lower bounds for the problem.
  On the algorithmic side, we study $k$-MSR under the framework of mergeable constraints, which captures a broad class of clustering constraints, including fairness, diversity, and lower bounds. We obtain an FPT $(\frac{8}{3}+ε)$-approximation, improving upon the previous best guarantee of $(4+ε)$. Moreover, given access to a suitable assignment subroutine, we achieve a $(2+ε)$-approximation, matching the best known bound for the unconstrained problem. This, in turn, yields $(2+ε)$ FPT-approximations for several important settings, including $(t,k)$-fair, $(α,β)$-fair, $\ell$-diversity, and private clustering.

</details>


### [112] [Fast decremental tree sums in forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06555)
*Benjamin Aram Berendsohn,Marek Sokołowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究两类增量动态图问题（tree-sum和subtree-sum查询），在边删除场景下通过微-宏树分解等技术将查询复杂度降至O(log* n)，无权重时达O(1)，并证明存在渐进普遍最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准动态森林数据结构（如top trees）对删除、更新和查询操作均需O(log n)时间；研究者希望探究删除专用问题是否能够更快解决。

Method: 采用Alstrup等人(1997)的微-宏树分解技术，结合最优小规模实例的预计算数据结构，并深入分析OPT行为；对于子树和查询问题通过建立下界和匹配上界证明紧致性。

Result: tree-sum查询达O(log* n)每操作（无权重时O(1)）；群模型下达到O(OPT(F,m))的渐进普遍最优；子树和查询获得多种变体的紧致复杂度界。

Conclusion: 对于tree-sum查询，提出了基于微-宏树分解的数据结构，将每操作时间从O(log n)降至O(log* n)，在无权重情况下可达O(1)；对于子树和查询，给出了多种变体的紧致界；证明了在群模型下存在渐进普遍最优算法，处理m个操作的时间复杂度为O(OPT(F, m))。

Abstract: We study two fundamental decremental dynamic graph problems. In both problems, we need to maintain a vertex-weighted forest of size $n$ under edge deletions, weight updates, and a certain information-retrieval query. Both problems can be solved in $O(\log n)$ time per update/query using standard dynamic forest data structures like top trees, even if additionally edge insertions are allowed. We investigate whether the deletion-only problem can be solved faster.
  First, we consider $\texttt{tree-sum}$ queries, where we ask for the sum of vertex weights in one of the connected components (i.e., trees) in the forest. We give a data structure with $O(n)$ preprocessing time and $O(\log^* n)$ time per operation, based on a micro-macro tree decomposition (Alstrup et al., 1997). If the forest is unweighted (i.e., all weights are 1 and cannot be changed), then the operation time can be improved to $O(1)$.
  Additionally, we give an asymptotically universally optimal algorithm. More specifically, our algorithm works in the group model, and processes $m$ operations on an initial forest $F$ in running time $O( \mathrm{OPT}(F, m) )$. Here $\mathrm{OPT}(F, m)$ is the number of weight additions and subtractions that a best possible algorithm performs to handle a worst-case instance for a fixed initial forest $F$ and a fixed number $m$ of operations. We achieve this with a combination of the aforementioned decomposition technique, precomputation of optimal data structures for very small instances, and some insights into the behavior of $\mathrm{OPT}$. Note that even the worst-case complexity of this algorithm remains unknown to us.
  Second, we consider $\texttt{subtree-sum}$ queries. Here, the forest is rooted, and a query $\texttt{subtree-sum}(v)$ returns the sum of weights in the subtree rooted at $v$. We show tight bounds for several variants of this problem. [...]

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nitsum是一个分布式LLM服务系统，核心创新是将张量并行度(TP)作为运行时控制平面而非静态配置，通过联合优化TP级别、GPU分配和请求调度，并结合TP感知的权重重用和快速KV迁移技术，在多租户分层SLO场景下实现显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统在多租户环境下面临分层SLO挑战（延迟敏感交互请求与后台任务的混合），现有系统主要依赖请求级控制（如队列和批处理）而保持执行配置静态，导致在多层级争用下难以适应。

Method: 1) 将张量并行度(TP)作为运行时控制平面进行联合优化；2) 同时优化TP级别、预填充/解码GPU分配和请求调度；3) 实现TP感知的权重重用和快速KV迁移以支持频繁的TP自适应。

Result: 在真实轨迹和针对性微基准测试中，Nitsum相比现有最优方法将SLO合规吞吐量提升最多5.3倍。

Conclusion: Nitsum通过将TP作为运行时控制平面而非静态部署选择，实现了SLO合规吞吐量相比现有最优方法提升5.3倍，为多租户LLM服务系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [114] [EdgeServing: Deadline-Aware Multi-DNN Serving at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05527)
*Jiahe Cao,Xiaomeng Li,Qiang Liu,Tao Han,Ning Zhang,Weisong Shi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EdgeServing是一个针对边缘设备的多DNN服务系统，通过时间分片GPU共享和早退出推理提高推理可预测性，并引入稳定性评分量化调度决策对队列状态的影响，在多个硬件平台上表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中，在单一共享GPU上运行多个DNN模型的场景下，调度决策会影响所有并发队列的尾部延迟。现有调度器依赖局部启发式方法，无法捕捉全局影响；而GPU空间共享方法则会牺牲延迟可预测性。

Method: EdgeServing采用时间分片GPU共享策略结合早退出推理以提高推理可预测性；引入稳定性评分量化候选调度决策对未来队列状态的影响；在运行时协同选择模型、退出点和批大小，以最小化预测的系统级SLO影响。

Result: 在多个硬件平台上的实验表明，EdgeServing在SLO违规率和P95延迟方面均优于代表性基线方法，这得益于早退出机制在严格延迟约束下扩展了调度动作空间。

Conclusion: 早退出机制能够扩展调度动作空间，使系统在严格延迟约束下仍能有效优化调度决策，实现更优的SLO合规率和延迟性能。

Abstract: As edge computing expands, serving multiple deep neural network (DNN) models on a single shared GPU has become a common yet challenging scenario, where each scheduling decision affects the tail latency of all concurrent queues. Existing schedulers rely on local heuristics and fail to capture this global impact, while GPU spatial-sharing approaches sacrifice latency predictability. In this paper, we propose EdgeServing, a deadline-aware multi-DNN serving system for edge devices. EdgeServing adopts time-division GPU sharing with early-exit inference for high inference predictability, and introduces a stability score to quantify how each candidate scheduling decision impacts the future queue status. At runtime, it cohesively selects the model, exit point, and batch size to minimize predicted system-wide SLO impact. Experimental results on multiple hardware platforms show that EdgeServing consistently outperforms representative baselines in both SLO violation ratio and P95 latency, enabled by early-exit mechanism, which expands the scheduling action space under tight latency constraints.

</details>


### [115] [A Scalable Digital Twin Framework for Energy Optimization in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05581)
*Raphael Hendrigo de Souza Gonçalves,Wendel Marcos dos Santos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个结合物联网、云计算和机器学习的可扩展数字孪生框架，用于数据中心的能源优化。实验表明该框架能有效降低能耗并提升PUE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心能耗持续增长，传统的能源管理方法难以满足实时监测和优化决策的需求，亟需一个可扩展的智能能源管理解决方案。

Method: 构建了一个小规模受控数据中心实验环境，采集功耗、温度和计算负载等数据；采用LSTM模型预测能源需求，结合IoT数据采集和云计算平台实现实时监控与决策支持。

Result: 实验验证了框架在能源效率方面的改进，包括功耗降低和PUE（电源使用效率）指标提升。

Conclusion: 该框架展示出作为可持续数据中心管理可扩展、成本效益解决方案的良好潜力，但目前仅在受控小规模环境中验证，后续需进一步扩展验证。

Abstract: This study proposes a scalable Digital Twin framework for energy optimization in data centers.The framework integrates IoT-based data acquisition, cloud computing, and machine learning techniques to enable real-time monitoring, forecasting, and intelligent energy management. A controlled small-scale data center environment was developed to monitor variables such as power consumption, temperature, and computational workload. Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models were employed to predict energy demand and support operational decision-making. Experimental results demonstrated improvements in energy efficiency, including reductions in power consumption and enhancements in Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE). Despite being evaluated in a constrained environment, the proposed framework demonstrates strong potential as a scalable and cost-effective solution for sustainable data center management.

</details>


### [116] [Irminsul: MLA-Native Position-Independent Caching for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05696)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Irminsul是一种针对MLA架构的内容寻址缓存方案，通过content-hash键控和k_r的δ-旋转修正，在agentic LLM工作负载中恢复了~83%的缓存命中率，同时降低63%的prefill能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM工作负载中，每轮对话的token位置发生偏移，导致前缀缓存在首次分歧字节处完全失效，引发缓存命中率下降和TTFT（首个token生成时间）激增10-16秒的严重性能问题。

Method: 提出Irminsul系统：在SGLang的radix cache基础上扩展，使用CDC分段的content-hash键控，以及针对64维k_r的δ-旋转规则实现闭环修正。该方案利用MLA的KV分解结构（位置无关的c_KV + 64维可修正的k_r），无需对GQA架构进行改造。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite(16B/2.4B)、Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B和JoyAI-Flash(48B/3B)三个MLA-MoE部署上验证：Irminsul在agentic流量场景下恢复了~83%的精确前缀以上缓存命中率，同时每次缓存命中节省63%的prefill能耗。

Conclusion: Irminsul提议将内容寻址缓存作为服务栈中的一级原语，而非前缀匹配的后期适配。建议在MLA-MoE架构中采用内容哈希键控和δ旋转机制来恢复因位置偏移而失效的缓存命中率。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workloads put bit-identical tokens at shifted positions every turn, voiding prefix caches at the first byte of divergence. Operators report cache-hit regressions ranging from moderate slowdowns to severe TTFT spikes of 10-16s on unchanged content. Prior position-independent caching systems correct RoPE on the full $d_K$-dimensional key, an architectural cost imposed by GQA, not by caching itself. Multi-Head Latent Attention, deployed at scale in DeepSeek-V2/V3/R1, Kimi-K2/Moonlight, GLM-5, and Mistral Large 3, factors each KV row into a position-free $c_{KV}$ and a 64-dim $k_r$ correctable in closed form; this structure motivates content-addressed caching as a natural fit rather than a GQA workaround. We present Irminsul, which extends SGLang's radix cache with content-hash keying over CDC-chunked segments and a $δ$-rotation rule for $k_r$. We evaluate three native MLA-MoE deployments - DeepSeek-V2-Lite (16B/2.4B), Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B, and JoyAI-Flash (48B/3B) - with output-consistency on all three and recovery measured on the two endpoints; Irminsul recovers up to ~83% of prompt tokens above exact-prefix on agentic traffic while delivering 63% prefill energy savings per cache hit. We argue that content-addressed caching belongs in the serving stack as a first-class primitive, not a retrofit over prefix matching.

</details>


### [117] [LLM-Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Task Offloading in Collaborative Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05727)
*Hao Guo,Kaixiang Xv,Ziwu Ge,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 LeDRL：一种将轻量级 LLM（Qwen3-4B）与自注意力增强的 DRL（基于 MAPPO）耦合的混合决策框架，用于协同边缘计算中的去中心化任务卸载。LLM 以结构化提示（节点状态/任务语义/链路动态）生成高层策略先验，经自注意力对齐模块与本地观测融合后注入策略网络；反思评估器把回合结果转化为结构化语义反馈以优化后续提示。仿真中在 10/20 节点网络均取得最高任务成功率（59.46%/63.78%），并在 10 台 Jetson 节点构成的 CoEdgeSys 原型系统（YOLOv8 + COCO）上验证了真实部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同边缘计算（CEC）需要在跨地域的边缘节点间动态决定任务本地执行或多跳转发，以在节点故障、链路中断、拓扑变化下同时保证低时延与高可靠性。现有静态启发式与预训练策略缺乏适应性；而两条主流技术路线各有硬伤：DRL 存在样本效率低、状态/动作空间随规模呈维数灾难、收敛慢、易陷入局部最优，且故障会改变有效动作集导致次优/非法决策；LLM 虽具备上下文推理与泛化能力，可作为启发式先验，但参数庞大导致推理时延高、难以满足边缘实时性，且输出随机性强、决策不一致，无法直接作为在线决策者。动机由此落在"如何在保留 LLM 语义泛化优势的同时满足边缘实时性与可靠性要求"。

Method: 系统建模与问题形式化：时变无向图 G(t)=(V(t),E(t))，节点具算力 F、逐邻居通信队列与速率 R；任务按伯努利到达，具输入大小 S、计算强度 Δ（所需周期 C=ΔS）、截止期 D、最小可靠性 Φ；软件/硬件/链路故障均建模为泊松过程，可靠性为 exp(-(α_j+γ_j)T^c - β_ij T^t)（有限重传下 β 视为等效残余失效率）。以二元决策变量 x_ij(t) 写成 MINLP，约束含时延、资源（算力/带宽/内存）与可靠性，以任务成功率为目标（指示函数形式），并通过从 Bin Packing 的多项式归约证明问题 NP-hard。DRL 侧形式化为 Dec-POMDP：局部观测含到达率、故障率、算力、执行/缓冲队列长度、任务属性、当前转发跳数与累计等待、邻居链路速率与失效率（全部归一化到 [0,1]）；动作集随拓扑动态变化，采用拓扑感知掩码 M_i^t(j) 保证观测与有效动作一致；团队奖励 r_t=Σ(成功指示-违约指示)，与优化目标一致；采用 MAPPO 的因子化策略、裁剪目标、GAE 与熵正则。LLM 侧：构建 P_t=[S_t;T_t;N_t] 结构化提示；维护短期轨迹与长期失败/成功模式的双存储记忆，按 Loc/Type/Sim_task/Sim_load 加权相关性检索 top-K 上下文；反思评估器基于风险摘要 R_t 在 r_t<0 时触发 LLM 内省，生成失败原因与更安全选择并以 FIFO 写入长期记忆；Decision-Making Turbo 按输出 schema 生成决策，schema 违规时安全回退为本地执行。融合机制：LLM 离散输出经 BERT 编码器与投影得到 h^LLM，观测经 f_θ1 编码得到 h^Env，自注意力以 Env 为 Query、LLM 为 Key/Value，融合特征 h=αV+h^Env，再经 O_θ3 输出蒸馏特征 g^LLM；注意力模块以特征 MSE 损失加动作交叉熵损失联合训练；DRL 观测投影为 g^DRL，最终表示 g^F=(1-λ_llm)g^DRL+λ_llm·φ_Last(g^LLM)，λ_llm 按周期性提升-衰减调度更新。总目标为 min(L_πθ + L_Att)。

Result: 仿真（100 时隙、10~20 异构节点、3GHz CPU、链路 10–40 MB/s、节点故障率 0.01、节点出现概率 0.1，10 次随机运行）显示：LeDRL 收敛更快、早期方差更低、最终成功率最高，10/20 节点成功率 59.46±1.42% 与 63.78±1.68%，优于 RATC、AGSP、VDN-TO、MASAC-TO、MAPPO-TO、Reflexion；相较 MAPPO-TO 提升约 12.87%（10 节点）；在失效率 0.25 的执行/传输故障扰动下分别领先约 12% 与 17%（即摘要所述"超过 17%"）；在随机图与环形拓扑两类结构中均保持最优，MAPPO-TO 在环形拓扑下滑明显。推理时延方面，LeDRL 为 0.7046 s（10 节点）/0.7379 s（20 节点），显著低于 Reflexion 的 1.58/3.01 s，但高于纯 DRL（0.003–0.004 s），仍满足 4 s 时限。消融显示：纯 MAPPO 最差；"LLM+MLP+MAPPO"提升有限；换成自注意力（RATC/AGSP+SA+MAPPO）进一步提升但缺少语义精炼仍次优；完整 LeDRL 最优。真实系统 CoEdgeSys（4×Jetson Nano + 6×Jetson NX，千兆有线网，YOLOv8 检测 COCO，actor 网络 <100KB，Qwen3-4B 部署于单卡 RTX 4090 服务器，gRPC 转发，随机注入 DoS 节点状态）在置信度阈值 0.4/0.5 下成功率最高（约 60%），较最优基线提升约 13.5%，且时延接近纯 DRL 基线。

Conclusion: LeDRL 通过"轻量 LLM 语义先验 + 自注意力策略融合 + 反思式记忆反馈"的有效耦合，在去中心化、动态故障的协同边缘卸载任务上，同时改善了任务成功率、收敛速度、拓扑/故障适应性与推理效率，并在 Jetson 原型系统上验证了资源受限下的鲁棒性与可部署性，说明 LLM 语义知识作为决策先验可有效缓解 DRL 的样本效率与局部最优问题。局限与后续方向：LLM 推理仍带来约两个数量级的额外时延且需离线服务器 GPU 支撑，作者计划"将 LLM 与在线执行解耦、仅在训练期保留其鲁棒性收益"，并进一步扩展到更大规模网络。需注意的几点：摘要"超过 17% 提升"仅对应特定故障扰动设定，表 I 相对最优基线的相对增益为 3–13%；LLM 提示调用开销与 token 成本未被系统评估；拓扑感知掩码、记忆检索权重与 λ_llm 调度超参的敏感性分析亦不充分。
