Abstract: Collaborative edge computing uses edge nodes in different locations to execute tasks, necessitating dynamic task offloading decisions to maintain low latency and high reliability, especially under unpredictable node failures. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) and large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for task offloading, DRL often suffers from high sample inefficiency and local optima, whereas LLMs struggle with real-time decision-making. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{LeDRL}, a hybrid decision framework that couples a \emph{lightweight LLM} with self-attention-enhanced DRL for real-time task offloading. LeDRL constructs structured, context-aware prompts capturing node status, task semantics, and link dynamics to derive high-level strategy priors. These are selectively processed by a self-attention-based alignment module for context-aware policy optimization. A reflective evaluator distills semantic feedback from past trajectories to guide future prompts, enabling more informative and temporally generalizable LLM queries. Extensive experiments show that LeDRL outperforms baselines in task success rate, convergence speed, and real-time responsiveness across diverse network scales, achieving over 17\% improvement in success rate. Furthermore, we deploy LeDRL on Jetson-based edge devices using our prototype system \textit{CoEdgeSys}, demonstrating its robustness and feasibility under resource constraints. Our code is available at:https://github.com/GalleyG5/LeDRL.git.

</details>


### [118] [A Privacy-Preserving Machine Learning Framework for Edge Intelligence: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05751)
*Quoc Lap Trieu,Bahman Javadi,Jim Basilakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向边缘智能（EI）的隐私保护机器学习（PPML）框架，包含"云—边缘服务器—边缘设备—传感器"四层架构及训练/推理两套算法，并在 LeNet-5、SqueezeNet、AlexNet 与 FordA、ElectricDevices、ECG5000 时间序列数据集上，对差分隐私（DP）、安全多方计算（SMC）、全同态加密（FHE）三种方案在准确率、响应时间、能耗三个维度进行真实硬件实验与基于 EdgeSimPy 的轨迹驱动仿真，并附带黑盒模型窃取攻击的定量评估。核心结论：DP 性能几乎与明文持平但精度损失明显（AlexNet 在 FordA 上最高约 35%）；SMC 受通信带宽与参与方数量支配（带宽翻倍使 AlexNet 延迟降低约 30%）；FHE 计算开销极高，响应时间约为 DP 的 1000 倍；DP 还能实质降低模型窃取者的数据效率，而 SMC/FHE 因输出明文暴露需额外输出控制才能获得同等抗窃取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能在智慧医疗、智能制造、智能电网与关键基础设施中快速落地，但原始数据在感知、端侧处理、传输与云端分析全链条上均存在泄露风险；与此同时边缘侧算力、内存与时延预算严格受限。三类主流 PPML 方案（DP、SMC、FHE）在保护对象、开销来源与副作用上机理完全不同：DP 通过训练期噪声换取隐私，SMC 以通信与交互轮次换取计算隐私，FHE 以密文计算的乘法深度与数值精度开销换取输入/参数不可见。既有工作多只覆盖其中单一技术、或只关注准确率、或仅在云环境评估，缺少在真实边缘异构硬件上的推理级性能（含能耗）与系统级规模的横向对比，导致实践者难以根据时延、能耗、检测精度与合规约束做出有依据的技术选型。

Method: 1）框架设计：提出四层架构（Cloud / Edge Server / Edge Device / Sensor）与四大组件（Data、Model、System、Application），隐私模块部署于边缘设备层与边缘服务器层；云层训练，边缘层推理。2）算法形式化：Algorithm 1 给出云侧训练与部署准备（DP 走 DP-SGD 并部署明文 DP 模型；FHE 走基线训练 + Concrete-ML 编译并分发 pk/sk/evk；SMC 走 CrypTen 初始化与模型分发）；Algorithm 2 给出逐样本边缘推理与度量采集（DP 明文转发、FHE 编码加密后密文推理再端侧解密、SMC 由 P0 边缘服务器协调的 2PC/3PC 秘密分享推理并记录通信字节与轮次）。3）技术栈：DP 用 TensorFlow Privacy（噪声乘子 σ∈[0.1,0.7]，裁剪 C=1.0，δ=1e-5）；SMC 用 CrypTen（world_size=2/3，Z_{2^64} 环，16-bit 定点）；FHE 用 Concrete-ML（TFHE，128-bit 安全，量化位宽 4/5/6，精度位宽 5/6）。4）实验环境：AWS r6i.4xlarge 训练与精度评测；边缘侧为桌面级边缘服务器（16 核/32 GB）与三类设备（Intel NUC、Jetson AGX、Raspberry Pi 5），采集 500 条推理时延轨迹；数据集为 UEA/UCR 的 FordA、ElectricDevices、ECG5000。5）仿真：基于 EdgeSimPy 构建 4 边缘服务器 + 12 边缘设备、3000 请求、并发用户 30–70、SMC 带宽 250/500 Mbps、线性功率模型与保持不变的启发式调度器，统计平均响应时间与每次推理能耗。6）安全分析：分别刻画三种技术的威胁模型、防护目标、可调旋钮与残余泄露信道（TLS、侧信道、代码溯源、恶意安全性开销等）。7）模型窃取实验：以 FordA/LeNet 为目标，DP-SGD 训练并扫描 σ∈{0.1…1.0, 2.0}，攻击者控制 15%/30% 测试集查询并叠加高斯抖动合成样本训练替代模型，在互斥留出集上报告 macro Acc/Prec/Sens/F1 及 Target−Substitute 的 Δ 指标，并叠加 RDP accountant 计算的 ε，5 个随机种子取均值±标准差。

Result: 效率方面：DP 吞吐与时延最接近明文基线（70 并发下平均响应 0.185 s，仅含 0.0442 s 推理 + 0.1398 s 排队），能耗最低（0.0009–0.0011 Wh/次），但精度随噪声单调下降且随模型复杂度放大——AlexNet 在 ECG5000 降 3–4%、ElectricDevices 降 21–22%、FordA 降 35%，而 LeNet-5/SqueezeNet 分别 ≤3%、13–18%，说明隐私预算需按模型-数据集单独标定。FHE 精度对量化位宽敏感（4→6 位时 AlexNet 提升 10–20%、SqueezeNet 4–9%、LeNet-5 ≤5%），时延随位宽超线性增长（AlexNet 4→6 位涨 7–9 倍，SqueezeNet 3–4 倍，LeNet-5 2–3 倍），精度位宽 5→6 再增 20–60%；整体响应时间约为 DP 的 1000 倍，AlexNet/FordA/70 用户可达约 10,000 s，能耗高达 20.0167 Wh/次（AlexNet）。SMC 呈通信主导：两方相对 DP 约 9 倍开销，三方在 250 Mbps 下约再增 100%，250→500 Mbps 使 AlexNet 两方延迟降低约 30%（100 s→70 s），AlexNet/FordA/70 用户约 70–100 s，推理 11.403 s、通信 9.6 s、排队 55.624 s；能耗 0.0088–0.7436 Wh/次并随带宽提升而下降；参与方数量增加使时延至少线性增长而精度基本不变。响应时间分布上 DP 接近单高斯、方差最小，SMC 呈双峰，FHE 多峰、离散度最大。抗窃取方面：DP 在中等噪声区（σ≈0.5）取得最大 ΔAcc≈0.10（q=0.15），q=0.30 时 ΔAcc 由 σ=0.1 的 0.097 降至 σ=2.0 的 0.024，ΔPrec/ΔSens/ΔF1 与之同步；ε 随 σ 骤降（σ=0.1 时 ε 高达 10⁴–10⁵，σ≈0.5 已低于 10，σ≈1.0 进入 O(1)），形成隐私—效用—可窃取性的三方权衡；SMC 与 FHE 在推理期虽保护输入与参数，但预测结果对查询者明文可见，其窃取曲线接近明文模型，需附加输出扰动、限流或访问控制才能达到同等抗性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在边缘智能语境下对 DP、SMC、FHE 做统一的推理级系统评测，结论是三者不存在通用最优解而呈现清晰的机理分工：若时延与能耗优先（实时推理、能量受限的 IoT 端），DP 是唯一接近明文开销的方案，前提是按模型与数据集精细标定噪声乘子并接受相应精度损失（AlexNet 等复杂模型尤需谨慎），同时可顺带获得对黑盒模型窃取的额外抵抗力；若推理精度不可让步且需输入/参数在推理全程不可见，FHE 在 5–6 位量化下精度可接近基线，但必须以轻量模型（LeNet-5 在 4–5 位已近饱和）、量化调参与向高性能边缘服务器卸载为前提，且需为千倍级的时延与能耗代价预留系统设计空间；若涉及多方协同推理且网络条件良好，SMC 精度与明文几乎一致、性能居中，但必须按威胁模型选择最小参与方数并保证带宽与 TLS 传输，其时延主要由通信轮次与链路容量决定。总体而言，隐私方案不能孤立选取，必须结合检测精度、时延阈值、能耗预算与监管约束在具体边缘场景中联合配置；作者未来工作将聚焦于进一步优化这三种技术并设计新的任务调度算法以改善边缘环境下的整体性能。

Abstract: As Edge Intelligence (EI) becomes increasingly prevalent in domains such as smart healthcare, manufacturing, and critical infrastructure, ensuring data privacy while maintaining system efficiency is a growing challenge. This paper presents a new privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) framework tailored for EI applications, including a four-layer system architecture and training and inference algorithms. We focus on three leading approaches: Differential Privacy (DP), Secure Multi-party Computation (SMC), and Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE), and assess their impact on key performance metrics, including model accuracy, response time, and energy consumption. Results from real implementation and extensive trace-based simulations of inference tasks show that DP generally preserves throughput and latency close to plaintext baselines, while accuracy drops with model complexity (up to 35 percent on AlexNet and under 18 percent on LeNet for FordA). SMC performance is driven by communication; network bandwidth and round complexity determine end-to-end latency. For AlexNet, increasing link capacity from 250 Mbps to 500 Mbps reduces latency by about 30 percent. FHE is highly sensitive to model structure and numerical precision bit width, with tighter parameters imposing substantial compute overhead; we observe roughly a 1000 times increase in response time compared to DP. Beyond efficiency, DP shifts the privacy-utility-extractability frontier by reducing the attacker's data efficiency in black-box model stealing, whereas SMC and FHE, while protecting inputs and parameters during inference, require complementary output controls to achieve similar resistance to extraction. These findings provide critical insights into the trade-offs between privacy, performance, and resource efficiency in edge computing scenarios.

</details>


### [119] [Relay Buffer Independent Communication over Pooled HBM for Efficient MoE Inference on Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06055)
*Tianlun Hu,Tiancheng Hu,Shengsheng Litang,Sheng Wang,Xiaoming Bao,Yuxing Li,Wei Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Lijun Li,Hongwei Sun,Jingbin Zhou\\*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向昇腾平台的“无中继缓冲（relay-buffer-free）”MoE 通信设计：借助全局池化HBM（对称内存分配 + shmem远端访问），把 dispatch 改造为“直接写入目标专家窗口”、combine 改造为“直接从远端专家窗口读取 + 本地加权归约”，移除传统 pack→IPC relay→restore 链路上的大块中继与重排缓冲，仅保留计数、偏移、地址交换与同步等轻量控制状态；并分别针对 prefill（保留较丰富规划状态的四阶段 Layout→Notify→Dispatch→Combine）与 decode（紧凑的 Dispatch→Combine）落地为两套 DeepEP 风格内核调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 推理中，EP（专家并行）要求 token 在设备间大规模交换，dispatch/combine 已成为 prefill 与 decode 的主要瓶颈；已有工作（FUSCO、DeepEP、Tutel、DeepSpeed-MoE 等）表明开销并不只来自网络传输本身，路由驱动的布局变换、临时中继（relay/staging）与输出还原（restore）同样占据 shuffle 总时间的很大比例。传统“buffer-centric”实现将 payload 先打包/置换进 IPC 中继缓冲或通用集合通信（A2A/A2AV），再还原为 expert-major 布局，产生额外 HBM 读写、同步开销与瞬时显存占用，限制了 batch size 与长上下文部署。昇腾平台提供的全局池化 HBM（对称内存 + shmem 远端访问）使得通信域内可以跨 rank 直接寻址设备内存，从而具备摆脱 sender-pack/receiver-unpack 模式、按硬件“读优先”特征重构通信路径的条件。

Method: 设计目标 G1–G3：消除/压缩中继与重排缓冲、以轻量通信状态替代张量型控制、契合昇腾读优先执行特征。①内存基础：bootstrap 阶段建立 world-visible 对称设备虚拟地址空间，各 rank 以确定性偏移划分本地切片，前缀为通信可见状态区、后缀为分配器受控堆，通信控制与 payload 共享同一池化 HBM 基座。②执行模型：payload 与控制解耦，通信状态仅含计数、偏移、交换地址、wait symbol/向量 barrier/核 barrier/状态标志；所谓“无中继”指消除主 payload 路径上的大块 relay/staging/reorder 缓冲，而非零状态。③核心寻址规则：两级偏移——小偏移 s（token 在目标专家流中的本地序号，prefill 为 sendTokenIdx、decode 为 expandIdx）与大偏移 o（源 rank 到专家 e 的块起始行，prefill 为 putOffset、decode 为 ep_recv_count），专家窗口行号 = o_{e,r}+s_{t,j}。④Prefill 调度：Layout 计算 per-rank/per-expert 路由计数与 sendTokenIdx（分块计数+前缀累加，不做任何 payload 重排）；Notify 交换计数元数据生成 recvData(R×E)、putOffset、totalRecvTokenNum、recvTokenNumPerExpert、balanceMatrix；Dispatch 将 X_t 直接写入目标行 putOffset+sendTokenIdx；Combine 由 o+sendTokenIdx 定位远端行并本地加权求和 Y_t=Σ_j W_{t,j}Y^exp。⑤Decode 调度：去掉 Layout/Notify 显式两阶段，将计数、基址偏移（ep_recv_count）与 expandIdx 折入 Dispatch；Dispatch 依据 dstExpertOffset+curExpertCnt 直接落位；Combine 采用读优先路径，由 remoteBase+remoteOffset 计算远端行，直接读取生产者可见的专家窗口，默认走轻量地址握手，亦支持 cached-address 快速路径以省去重复握手。⑥两种调度共享同一 payload 移动规则，差异仅在于保留的规划/协调状态量。实现栈位于上层服务框架（vLLM/SGLang/MindIE）与 AclShmem/Memfabric 之间，含 normal 与 low-latency dispatch/combine 内核、少量集合通信内核、内存分配/buffer 注册/barrier/核切分等运行时组件。

Result: 与 HCCL 启用的 DeepEP 基线对比：Prefill normal 路径，非量化下 dispatch 从约 1.1ms(1024 token)→9.4ms(16384 token) 降至约 1.0ms→6.8ms，combine 同样下降且差距随 token 数扩大；量化下收益仍在但 dispatch 侧因 scale 处理开销而相对收窄。Decode 低时延路径：dispatch 平均加速 20.18%（h=4096 非量化）、31.02%（h=4096 量化）、14.83%（h=7168 非量化）、27.72%（h=7168 量化）；combine 平均加速稳定在 22%~24%（22.75/24.45/22.43/24.34），最小/最大加速区间约 8.9%~39.9%。案例研究：DeepSeek 3.1 配置 dispatch −23.3%、combine −10.8%；Qwen-235B 配置 dispatch −52.8%、combine −18.3%。端到端服务（DeepSeek 3.1）：TTFT 由 11197ms 降至 6793ms，TPOT 由 30.10ms 变为 31.31ms，仍低于目标阈值。调度空间（DeepSeek 3.2，TTFT<5000ms 且 TPOT<60ms）：同时满足双约束的配置数增多，最优吞吐可行点贴近约束边界，并揭示 TTFT 最优配置未必是 TPOT 最优配置这一权衡。作者自述为早期实验，模型覆盖、显存占用测量与更系统的服务基准留待后续版本。

Conclusion: 在具备全局可寻址设备内存的平台上，MoE 通信应被视为“围绕专家路由的 buffer-centric 分布式执行问题”而非单纯的集合带宽问题；减少中间中继缓冲与输出还原、把 payload 直接落位到目标专家窗口、由消费者侧直接远端读取并本地加权归约，是加速 MoE 推理的有效方向。该设计统一了 prefill 与 decode 两个阶段的通信模型：dispatch 直接放置、combine 直接读取+本地归约，prefill 以丰富规划状态换取吞吐，decode 以紧凑控制路径换取时延。局限在于本文为工程报告（华为技术团队发布），实验规模有限、缺少显存/带宽细粒度剖析，且高度依赖昇腾全局池化 HBM 与 shmem 远端访问能力，迁移到 GPU 平台需借助类似 NVLink one-sided symmetric-memory A2A 路径（如 TensorRT-LLM 的做法）。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference requires large-scale token exchange across devices, making dispatch and combine major bottlenecks in both prefill and decode. Beyond network transfer, routing-driven layout transformation, temporary relay, and output restoration can add substantial overhead. Existing MoE communication paths are often buffer-centric, using explicit inter-process relay and reordering buffers around collective transfer. This report presents a relay-buffer-free communication design for MoE inference acceleration on Ascend systems. The design reorganizes dispatch and combine around direct placement into destination expert windows and direct reading from remote expert windows. Built on globally pooled high-bandwidth memory and symmetric-memory allocation, it removes most intermediate relay and reordering buffers while retaining only lightweight control state, including counts, offsets, and synchronization metadata. We instantiate the design as two schedules for the main phases of MoE inference: a prefill schedule with richer planning state for throughput-oriented execution, and a compact decode schedule for latency-sensitive execution. Experiments on Ascend-based MoE workloads show reduced dispatch and combine latency in both settings. At the serving level, the implementation improves time to first token (TTFT), preserves competitive time per output token (TPOT), and enlarges the feasible scheduling space under practical latency constraints. These results indicate that, on platforms with globally addressable device memory, reducing intermediate buffering and output restoration around expert execution is an effective direction for accelerating MoE inference.

</details>


### [120] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FalconGEMM是一个跨平台框架，通过部署模块、执行模块和决策模块三个创新组件，实现低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs）在多样化硬件上的高效部署和自动优化选择，在LLM工作负载中实现峰值突破性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习（尤其是LLM训练和推理）对矩阵乘法性能要求极高，现有GEMM库和传统算法难以满足需求，需要探索更低计算复杂度的矩阵乘法算法（LCMAs）并将其高效部署到多样化硬件平台上。

Method: (1) 部署模块：通过代码生成实现跨硬件和输入配置的便携式执行；(2) 执行模块：采用组并行优化策略，最大化片上数据复用、利用并行资源、降低带宽开销；(3) 决策模块：基于轻量级分析性能模型，根据矩阵形状和硬件配置选择最优策略。

Result: FalconGEMM在多种GPU和CPU架构上实现了峰值突破性能，相比主流GEMM库提升7.59%-17.85%，相比LCMA竞品提升12.41%-55.61%。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM成功实现峰值突破性能，在GPU（H20、A100）和CPU（ARM、x86）等多种硬件上，相比GEMM库（cuBLAS、CUTLASS、Intel MKL等）提升7.59%-17.85%，相比LCMA竞争方案（AlphaTensor）提升12.41%-55.61%，证明了LCMAs在大规模生产部署中的实用性。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [121] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出BalanceRoute系列在线路由算法，解决大模型推理服务中数据并行(DP)负载不均衡问题。该方案包含两个变体：BR-0无需预测基础设施，通过分段线性F-score和两阶段分解实现毫秒级调度；BR-H引入常数视线H和轻量级终止分类器扩展方案。实验在144-NPU集群上验证，相比vLLM基线显著降低DP不平衡度并提升端到端吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务中，当模型通过张量并行(TP)或专家并行(EP)分片并跨多个DP worker复制时，每个decode步骤的同步屏障延迟由负载最重的worker决定。即使DP worker间存在中等程度的不均衡，经过多步累积也会造成大量计算资源浪费。LLM解码的独特挑战包括：请求分配具有粘性（迁移KV缓存代价高昂）、单请求负载随时间增长、请求到达非平稳、路由器需在亚100ms解码预算内处理数百等待请求和数十worker。

Method: 提出BalanceRoute算法族，包含两种在线路由算法：1) BR-0：无需预测基础设施，采用分段线性F-score捕捉"填充安全边界"与"溢出包络"之间的非对称性，通过两阶段分解保持每步成本与毫秒级调度兼容；2) BR-H：泛化BR-0，引入短常数视线H和轻量级终止分类器接口，将F-score扩展为视线折扣形式。

Result: 在144-NPU集群上部署BalanceRoute，基于生产轨迹和公开Azure-2024轨迹评估。对比vLLM基线，BalanceRoute在两个工作负载上均显著降低平均DP不平衡度，并提升端到端服务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute通过设计精巧的在线路由算法有效解决了大模型推理服务中的DP负载均衡瓶颈，兼顾了算法复杂度与实际部署约束，验证了F-score驱动路由策略的有效性。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [122] [TACO: A Toolsuite for the Verification of Threshold Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06118)
*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TACO 是首个支持扩展阈值自动机(ETA)、公开可用且持续维护的阈值自动机验证工具集，集成三个模型检查算法（SMT、ZCS、ACS）与两个半判定过程，在 86 个基准上整体显著优于唯一现有但已停止维护的 ByMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统的核心算法必须具备容错性与可扩展性，验证时既要参数化（进程数任意）又要按"弹性条件"（resilience condition，如 n>3t）对故障数取模推理。阈值自动机（TA）适合表达共识、区块链等算法，且已有多个可判定片段的判定过程，但工具支持严重缺失：唯一公开的 ByMC 已多年未维护，其他文献实现均为不适合非专家使用的原型，PyLTA 只支持分层 TA 且无法复用现有基准库。因此需要一个模块化、可扩展、文档完善且覆盖可判定片段与半判定过程的统一工具套件。

Method: 采用 Rust 实现安全子集，模块化解耦为公开 crate（解析器、预处理器、TA 表示、模型检查器、BDD/SMT 后端）。三种模型检查算法：(1) SMT Model Checker——基于 Balasubramanian 等人的 Presburger 可达性公式 φ_REACH 的 SMT 编码，改进之处是不用非确定性猜测而由求解器消解非确定性，仅适用于单调 TA（MTA）；(2) ZCS Model Checker——用参数区间抽象将共享变量映射到有限域得到 01 计数器系统（ZCS），用 SMT 求解 RC 下所有可能的阈值排序，对每个排序构造 IntervalTA 与 ZCS，用 BDD 表示状态空间，从错误配置反向探索至不动点，再广度优先检查是否存在非虚假错误路径；(3) ACS Model Checker——基于抽象计数器系统（ACS），利用 ACS 构成 WSTS 与良基拟序（wqo）做不动点计算，树结构维护已访问配置以快速比较，虚路径的 SMT 检查按路径长度增量进行以复用信息。ETA 下 ZCS/ACS 仅为半判定。系统模型含 MTA（共享变量只增）与 ETA（允许递减与变量重置，扩展自 Baumeister 等人）；规格为 ELTL_FT 的安全片段（位置计数与守卫的全局量词），输入支持 ByMC 扩展的 .ta/.eta 格式与 TLA+ 片段。

Result: 在 86 个基准的扩展评估中，TACO 至少一个模型检查器在 59 例（计入 ByMC cav15 模式）或 64 例（仅 ltl 模式）上快于 ByMC；不存在 ByMC 能解而 TACO 在 20 分钟超时内解不出的基准；反之有 8 个基准 ByMC 全部模式超时而 TACO 可解；ByMC 还在部分 cc_case* 基准上报语法错误、cav15 模式对安全基准报出无效反例。分算法看：不安全（存在反例）基准上 SMT 模型检查器通常最快（9 个中 4 个最快）；安全基准上 ACS/ZCS 更快（19 个中 15 个由 ZCS/ACS 领先），ACS 在小实例占优、ZCS 在大实例占优。ETA 基准上 ZCS 在所有安全实例上不劣于 ACS（大实例中 01 抽象+BDD 明显优于 WSTS 方法），而 ACS 因错误图深度优先探索在小的不安全实例上更快。求解器选择经验：大 TA 建议 SMT 模型检查器配 CVC5，小 TA 配 Z3；ACS/ZCS 一律推荐 Z3。已知局限：ACS 的 wqo 不支持要求目标位置为空的两类性质（toy、nbacg-asyn-guer01-nbac），随机生成的 Random19 类基准因上下文切换多、抽象区间序多而使 TACO 落后于 ByMC。代码质量方面行覆盖率约 97%。

Conclusion: TACO 以模块化、开放、文档完善的形式实现了阈值自动机参数化验证：三个可判定片段上的模型检查算法加两个超出片段的半判定过程，且是首个支持 ETA 的工具。其实验证明整体性能优于唯一现有工具 ByMC，同时提供可视化、具体反例轨迹、抽象错误路径等面向算法设计的丰富输出与可配置后端（SMTLIB2 求解器、CUDD/OxiDD）。未来工作包括支持活性（liveness）验证并设计降低协议设计者证明负担的新算法，以及从伪代码/领域特定语言到 TA 的自动翻译和图形界面，以提升非专家用户可用性。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [123] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ResiHP是一个面向大规模LLM训练（跨数万个GPU）的弹性训练系统，通过工作负载感知的执行时间预测器实现精确的故障检测，并动态调整并行组大小、模型划分和工作负载调度策略来适应故障场景，在256-GPU集群上相比现有弹性训练系统提升1.04-4.39倍吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模LLM训练中，硬件故障导致设备间性能偏差，降低训练效率。现有弹性系统忽视了数据集中序列长度变化和混合并行下的性能偏差问题，导致：(1) 序列长度变化引起的迭代时间波动会触发虚假慢故障检测；(2) 故障缓解采用混合并行中的独立适配策略，产生不必要的检测开销和低效的弹性训练。

Method: (1) Detector：开发了工作负载感知的执行时间预测器，将故障与迭代时间波动解耦，实现轻量级在线故障检测；(2) Scheduler：动态调整并行组大小、模型划分和工作负载调度策略。

Result: 在256-GPU集群的多种故障场景下，ResiHP相比现有弹性训练系统将训练吞吐量提升1.04-4.39倍。

Conclusion: 通过精确故障检测与细粒度混合并行自适应调整，ResiHP能够实现高效的大规模LLM弹性训练。

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [124] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADELIA，首个利用自动微分和多GPU稀疏结构计算的INLA实现，在大规模时空贝叶斯推断中实现4-8倍梯度计算加速和5-8倍能耗降低，解决了有限差分法随超参数维度增长计算代价高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的INLA实现在超参数优化中依赖中心有限差分（FD）逼近导数，需要2d+1次评估，计算量随超参数维度d线性增长，限制了固定算力预算下的求解效率和时间成本。

Method: 提出ADELIA框架，采用反向模式自动微分（AD）计算精确梯度（计算复杂度与超参数数量d无关），并设计了利用模型稀疏性的结构化多GPU反向传播算法。ADELIA是首个支持AD的INLA实现。

Result: 在10个基准模型（包括真实空气污染监测数据）上，ADELIA实现单次梯度计算4.2-7.9倍加速；在包含190万隐变量的大规模生产模型上可靠收敛，而FD方法在此尺度上表现挣扎；在匹配运行时间的条件下，FD需要扩展到16-32 GPU，能耗仍是ADELIA的5-8倍。

Conclusion: ADELIA展示了自动微分在INLA框架中的巨大潜力，通过利用模型稀疏性和多GPU架构，实现了显著的性能提升和能耗降低。这一工作为大规模时空贝叶斯推断提供了更高效、更实用的计算解决方案。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [125] [ROSE: Rollout On Serving GPUs via Cooperative Elasticity for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06534)
*Wei Gao,Yuheng Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Dakai An,Xuchun Shang,Tianyuan Wu,Lunxi Cao,Shaopan Xiong,Weixun Wang,Ju Huang,Teng Ma,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Bo Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE系统，通过合作弹性机制利用生产环境中未充分利用的GPU资源来加速agentic RL rollout。系统包括SLO安全协同执行器、跨集群权重传输引擎和弹性调度器，在保持服务SLO的同时实现1.20-3.31倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前agentic RL训练效率受限于长尾rollout的多轮环境交互，静态GPU资源配置难以适应：过度配置造成GPU资源浪费于落后者，配置不足则增加竞争并拖慢训练。

Method: ROSE系统包含三个核心组件：(1) SLO安全协同执行器，通过高效的GPU内存和计算共享提升rollout吞吐量；(2) 跨集群权重传输引擎，利用权重分片和稀疏性实现快速权重同步；(3) 弹性rollout调度器，动态配置协同容量并路由轨迹rollout。

Result: 实验表明，ROSE在多种模型规模和集群规模下，相比最先进的资源固定和弹性基线方法，将平均端到端吞吐量提升了1.20-3.31倍。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了合作弹性机制的可行性，通过系统性设计解决了服务优先级与训练效率之间的冲突，为大规模LLM训练系统的资源利用率优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a key driver for improving the multi-step reasoning and tool-use capabilities of LLMs. However, its efficiency is bottlenecked by long-tail rollouts with multi-turn environment interactions, making static GPU provisioning a poor fit: overprovisioning wastes GPUs on stragglers, while underprovisioning increases contention and slows training.
  We observe that production serving clusters routinely leave substantial GPU compute and memory headroom. Based on this observation, we argue for cooperative elasticity: opportunistically repurposing underutilized serving GPUs to execute rollouts. Realizing cooperative elasticity is non-trivial because it must preserve serving Service Level Objectives (SLOs) under bursty traffic and minimize communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present ROSE, a cooperative, resource-elastic post-training system that safely harvests idle compute and memory on serving GPUs to accelerate agentic RL rollouts. ROSE consists of three components: (1) an SLO-safe co-serving executor that improves rollout throughput while preserving serving SLOs through efficient GPU memory and compute sharing; (2) a cross-cluster weight transfer engine that leverages weight shards and sparsity for fast weight synchronization across clusters; and (3) an elastic rollout scheduler that dynamically provisions cooperative capacity and routes trajectory rollouts across dedicated rollout GPUs and opportunistic serving GPUs. Experiments across multiple model sizes and cluster scales show that ROSE improves average end-to-end throughput by 1.20-3.31 x compared with state-of-the-art resource-fixed and elastic baselines.

</details>


### [126] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CCL-Bench是一个基于跟踪的基准测试框架，旨在解决现有ML基础设施评估基准缺乏可解释性的问题，通过记录可复用的执行证据和细粒度指标，揭示硬件互连差异、软件框架调优和并行化策略对LLM训练性能的影响，发现TPU和GPU对带宽提升的响应不同，且跨框架性能差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础设施评估基准仅发布少量端到端数字，无法解释为何某种配置优于其他配置，而LLM基础设施评估涉及硬件加速器、互连带宽、软件框架、并行策略和通信库等复杂配置空间。

Method: 提出CCL-Bench，一个基于跟踪的基准测试框架，为每个ML工作负载记录可复用证据（执行跟踪、YAML工作负载卡片、启动脚本），并开发社区可扩展的工具包以计算细粒度的计算、内存和通信效率指标。

Result: （1）更高的计算-通信重叠可能与更长的训练步时间同时出现，揭示低效的并行化选择；（2）在小型和中型工作负载上，倍增TPU互连带宽比倍增GPU互连带宽带来更高的端到端步时间改善；（3）同一硬件上，最优调优配置在不同训练框架间性能差距可达3倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench通过记录可重复使用的证据（执行跟踪、工作负载卡片和启动脚本），填补了现有ML基础设施基准测试只提供端到端数字而缺乏可解释性的空白，并能够揭示硬件差异、软件框架调优以及并行化策略选择对训练性能的影响。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Active Learning for Communication Structure Optimization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05703)
*Huchen Yang,Xinghao Dong,Dan Negrut,Jin-Long Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于集成（ensemble）的信息论主动学习框架，用**集合卡尔曼反演（EKI）**近似贝叶斯更新，通过候选任务对"通信图参数"后验分布的改变量（KL 信息增益）来度量任务的信息量，并据此挑选用于 LLM 多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）通信结构优化的训练任务。方法采用"两阶段选择"：先用文本嵌入 + 最远点贪心构建紧凑的代表性候选池，再用 **PLS 降维 + 高斯过程代理模型 + 批量 Thompson 采样**做信息量筛选；整个流程无需端到端梯度，天然适配噪声、黑箱的多智能体系统。实验表明，在相同训练预算或相近总 token 成本下，该方法在 MMLU/GSM8K（良性 + 智能体攻击）以及 HumanEval、InterCode-SQL、ALFWorld 上均稳定优于随机任务采样，且提升了下游准确率、运行间稳定性与下尾（Q1/Worst-25%）表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1) 现有通信结构优化方法（AgentPrune 等）均采用**随机采样训练任务**，但任务在难度与领域上差异巨大，对图参数的更新贡献不等价，导致优化结果对训练集高度敏感：独立运行时下游准确率波动大（MMLU 攻击下 ±3.72），且增加训练任务数并不能一致提升精度，峰值后反而下降。
2) 通信结构优化是一个**三层嵌套的黑箱估计问题**：候选任务通过随机、不可导的 LLM-MAS rollout 评估；结构优化在其上用 RL/策略梯度（REINFORCE）进行；任务效用又由"该任务引起的图参数分布变化"定义。这使得基于采样的信息增益估计器（EIG/BALD）成本极高（每个候选需多次 rollout），而基于梯度/敏感度的代理（EGL、EMC、Fisher）在该嵌套黑箱管线中方差大、不可靠。
3) 现有主动学习准则（多样性、不确定性）与参数层面的信息增益之间存在缺口：任务难度、智能体分歧或预测多样性并不能刻画"任务对图参数后验的更新强度"。因此需要一个能在黑箱、噪声环境下高效估计参数级信息增益的选择准则。

Method: **问题设定**：MAS 通信结构表示为时空图 $\mathcal{G}=(V,E^S,E^T)$，引入可训练图 logits $\mathbf{z}=\{Z^S,Z^T\}$，经 sigmoid + Bernoulli 采样得到随机通信掩码 $A$；前向映射 $g=G(q;\mathbf{z},\omega)$，性能分数 $l=\phi(g,q,y)$。优化目标 $\mathbf{z}^\star=\arg\max \mathbb{E}_{A|\mathbf{z}}\mathbb{E}[\phi]$，以 REINFORCE 估计梯度更新。

**效用函数（信息论）**：$U(\mathcal{S})=I(\mathcal{S})=D_{KL}(p(\mathbf{z}|\mathcal{S})\|p(\mathbf{z}))$，即任务（集）引起的图参数先验—后验分布变化；因训练池中参考答案已知，直接计算"实现的信息增益"而无需对观测求期望。

**EKI 效用估计**：用 $J$ 个粒子 $\{\mathbf{z}^{(j)}\}$ 表示参数不确定性；预测步对每个粒子跑一次 MAS rollout 得 $l^{(j)}$，计算经验互协方差 $C^{zl}$ 与分数协方差 $C^{ll}$；更新步 $\mathbf{z}^{(j)}_{\text{post}}=\mathbf{z}^{(j)}+C^{zl}(C^{ll}+\Gamma)^{-1}(l^*-l^{(j)})$，其中 $l^*$ 为目标分数（MMLU 用正确选项对数概率、$l^*=0$；GSM8K/具身等用 0-1 正确性、$l^*=1$），$\Gamma$ 为正则化噪声协方差。仅做**一步** EKI 即可；后验 ensemble 与先验 ensemble 的（对角高斯近似）KL 即为任务效用。附录给出 EKI 与 score-based 后验采样的等价性推导（EKI 更新 = 局部后验协方差预条件化的似然得分）。

**批量扩展**：把标量分数换成向量分数 $\mathbf{l}^{(j)}$（任务维），并通过缓存任务级预测结果实现**复用**，重叠候选子集的前向评估次数从 $J\sum_b|\mathcal{S}_b|$ 降到 $J|\cup_b\mathcal{S}_b|$；另有多目标扩展（把准确率、稀疏度等作为伪观测的独立分量，避免标量化抵消）。

**两阶段选择流程（Algorithm 1）**：① 文本编码器（sentence-transformer，含 MMLU 学科标签）嵌入任务 → 贪心最远点选择构建 $R=50$ 的代表性池；② 代理建模 + 批量 Thompson 采样：PLS 将 384 维嵌入降至 2–3 维（效用监督），GP（Matérn $\nu=2.5$）拟合嵌入→效用映射，通过 GP 后验采样逐轮批量采集待评估任务，用真实 EKI 效用回填并重拟合，最终取 Top-K（K=10）任务训练图（每任务用 2 次，共 20 次 task usage）。MMLU 流水线为 1000→50→10，GSM8K 为 1000→50→10，HumanEval 94→50→10，InterCode-SQL 834→50→10，ALFWorld 20→12→10；EKI 超参：ensemble=6、先验扰动标准差 2.0、观测噪声 0.1、阻尼 0.7，KL 用对角高斯 + $10^{-8}$ 方差下限。代理策略只评估 25/50（MMLU）与 36/50（GSM8K）任务，评估成本降低 50%/28%，与全枚举 Top-10 重叠率 >80%。

Result: **主结果（GPT-4.1-nano、6 智能体）**：MMLU 良性 79.69→80.38（Q1 +0.68，Worst-25% +1.30）；GSM8K 良性 92.78→93.07；MMLU 攻击 76.87→78.32（Q1 +3.27，Worst-25% +4.25），且在相近总预算下优于随机（Random† 77.35，Active 总 0.92M token < Random† 的 1.57M）；GSM8K 攻击 92.90→93.83（Worst-25% +1.24），Random† 92.64。**成本效率**：主动学习用 0.80M（选择）+0.12M（训练）即优于随机训练远超此预算的水平。

**泛化**：HumanEval 77.80→78.80%；InterCode-SQL Success@5 51.45→57.27%（配对置换检验 p=0.037）、AUC@5 28.84→31.67%；ALFWorld Success@30 45.00→51.25%。**规模与骨干**：10 智能体 77.27→78.13%，20 智能体 78.43→80.91%（选择成本 0.8M/2.8M/13.0M，约随 N² 增长）；HumanEval 换 GPT-5-nano 85.60→86.40%。**基线对比（同等选择成本 150 次 rollout）**：EKI 78.32% 与 Fisher-coreset(det) 78.59% 最优，优于 EMC 77.21%、EGL 77.02%、Fisher-coreset(trace) 76.25%；EKI 内存 $O(JN^2)$ 优于 Fisher 的 $O(N^4)$。**消融**：仅信息量选择 77.12%，仅代表性选择 75.74%（≈随机 76.87%），二者结合 78.32% 且方差最小——信息量选择贡献主要增益，代表性选择负责降本并提升候选池质量。**敏感性**：ensemble=6 相对 ensemble=20 的 Top-10 重叠率约 0.75（随机基线 0.2）；一步 EKI 与三步 EKI 重叠 8–9/10，排序稳定。**统计**：bootstrap 95% CI 显示攻击下 GSM8K 三项指标均不含零，MMLU 攻击的 Worst-25% CI 为 [1.53, 6.32]（不含零），其余区间含零，作者将其解释为效应趋势而非决定性差异。**诊断**：信息增益高的任务同时具备较大预测方差与后验 ensemble 方差收缩（KL rank 1: KL=3804，Pred.var=86.3，Cosine=0.056），说明仅不确定性不足以判定信息量，还需更新方向有效。

Conclusion: **结论**：把"训练任务选择"从随机采样提升为显式的优化问题，是提升 LLM-MAS 通信结构优化效率与稳定性的关键。EKI 提供的无导数、集合化的参数级信息增益估计，配合代表性池筛选与代理模型辅助的批量 Thompson 采样，构成一套模块化、可插在任意图优化器之上的主动学习层：在相同训练预算下提升准确率与稳定性，在相近总预算下提升成本效率，并能跨知识/推理、代码生成、多步工具调用、长程具身交互与不同 LLM 骨干泛化。优化后的图会自发抑制恶意智能体到正常智能体的信息流（fake→normal 边被删除），但并非极稀疏，说明轻量训练预算下学到的是主要趋势。

**局限**：① 小 ensemble 相对参数维数（6 vs 36）时 EKI 的可靠性在极端区间尚未刻画，其后验本质是高斯近似，难以覆盖强非高斯/多模态；② 嵌入、代理模型与主动学习管线仍有改进空间；③ 假设任务与答案均已知，无标签/生成任务需改用期望信息增益（EIG/BALD）；④ 预测复用使所有子集共享同一 ensemble 误差方向，可能影响子集排序；⑤ 仅单轮选择、rollout 内图固定，攻击者身份在实验内固定。

**未来工作**：极端 regime 下 EKI 可靠性研究、无标签任务设置、任务生成；批次子集空间的有效采集策略；多轮迭代式（用更新后的图重新选任务）的图内自适应；与 REINFORCE 之外的其他结构优化器联合验证。

Abstract: Optimizing the communication structure of large language model based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) has been shown to improve downstream performance and reduce token usage. Existing methods typically rely on randomly sampled training tasks. However, tasks may differ substantially in difficulty and domain, and thus they are not equally informative for updating communication structure, making optimization under limited training budgets often unstable and highly sensitive to the particular training set. To actively identify the most valuable tasks for communication-structure optimization, we propose an ensemble-based information-theoretic task selection framework. The proposed method estimates task informativeness by how much a candidate task changes the distribution over graph parameters, using ensemble Kalman inversion as an efficient and derivative-free approximation of the corresponding Bayesian update. The resulting estimator is especially suitable for black-box and noisy multi-agent systems. To enhance scalability, we construct a compact candidate pool through embedding-based representative selection and combine the informative selection with surrogate modeling and batch Thompson sampling. We validate our method in both benign settings and settings with agent attacks, demonstrating its effectiveness for communication-structure optimization under constrained computational budgets.

</details>


### [128] [Auto Research with Specialist Agents Develops Effective and Non-Trivial Training Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05724)
*Jingjie Ning,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Hao Kang,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全自主运作的自动研究系统，通过外部评估驱动的闭环实验机制实现。系统由专家智能体组成，能够基于谱系反馈将评估结果（包括崩溃、预算超支、规模失败和精度门缺失）转化为后续程序级配方编辑，无需人工干预。在1,197次主要试验和600次参数优化对照试验中，人类未参与提案选择、配方编辑、分数覆盖或失败试验修复。三项基准测试分别取得0.81%验证bpb降低、38.7% CORE提升和4.59% wallclock时间减少的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动机器学习(AutoML)系统通常依赖人类选择提案、编辑配方或修复失败试验，限制了自动化程度和探索效率。本研究旨在构建一个完全自主的闭环研究系统，能够持续进行实验提案、代码修改、评估反馈和迭代改进。

Method: 1. 构建封闭的实验循环：每次提交包含假设、可执行代码编辑、评估者拥有的结果和塑造下一提案的反馈
2. 使用专家智能体对配方空间进行分区，并跨试验共享测量到的谱系信息
3. 核心创新：谱系反馈机制使智能体能够将评估结果（包括各种失败模式）转化为后续程序级配方编辑，而非一次性建议
4. 严格遵守外部评估器和合法性检查

Result: 在三个主要基准测试中：NanoChat-D12 CORE提升38.7%；CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock减少4.59%；Parameter Golf验证bpb降低0.81%。系统包含157次提交的程序重写审计（如NanoChat注意力核路径变更）。

Conclusion: 该系统能够在无需人工干预的情况下自主编写代码、提交实验、吸收反馈、应用和组合已知技术，并改进公开的起始配方。谱系反馈是关键机制，使系统能够将失败转化为后续改进，体现了真正的自动研究能力。

Abstract: We study auto research as a closed empirical loop driven by external measurement. Each submitted trial carries a hypothesis, an executable code edit, an evaluator-owned outcome, and feedback that shapes the next proposal. The output is not a generated paper or a single model checkpoint, but an auditable trajectory of proposals, code diffs, experiments, scores, and failure labels. We instantiate this loop with specialist agents that partition recipe surfaces and share measured lineage across trials. The central empirical finding is that lineage feedback lets agents turn evaluator outcomes, including crashes, budget overruns, size failures, and accuracy-gate misses, into later program-level recipe edits rather than one-shot suggestions. Across 1,197 headline-run trials plus 600 Parameter Golf control trials after one-time setup and launch, humans did not choose proposals, edit recipes, override scores, or repair failed trials during the search. In the three headline runs, the same submitted-trial loop reduces Parameter Golf validation bpb by $0.81\%$, raises NanoChat-D12 CORE by $38.7\%$, and reduces CIFAR-10 Airbench96 wallclock by $4.59\%$, with each task measured by its own external evaluator and legality checks. The trace includes a strict architecture-domain audit of 157 headline-run submissions and program rewrites such as a NanoChat attention-kernel path change. Within this scope the loop autonomously writes code, submits experiments, absorbs feedback, applies and combines known techniques inside each environment, and improves public starting recipes.

</details>


### [129] [Multiagent Stochastic Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06056)
*Martin Jonáš,Antonín Kučera,Vojtěch Kůr,Jan Mačák,Vojtěch Řehák*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: The paper introduces and analyzes the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, where k agents aim to minimize expected time for any agent to reach a target. The authors study computational and strategic complexity across autonomous and coordinated settings, and propose efficient algorithms validated experimentally.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-agent systems require coordination to achieve common goals efficiently. The MSSP problem formalizes minimizing expected time for any agent to reach a target state, which is a fundamental problem in multi-agent planning under uncertainty.

Method: The authors analyze the computational complexity in autonomous and coordinated settings, and design efficient algorithms for strategy synthesis.

Result: The paper provides complexity analysis and efficient algorithms for both autonomous and coordinated settings, with experimental validation showing scalability.

Conclusion: Efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms are designed and experimentally validated against natural baselines on instances of increasing size.

Abstract: We introduce and study the multi-agent stochastic shortest path (MSSP) problem, in which $k$ agents strive to reach a target state, aiming to minimize the expected time to reach the target by any agent. We analyze the computational and strategy-complexity of the problem in both autonomous and coordinated settings, and we design efficient strategy-synthesis algorithms. The algorithms are experimentally evaluated on instances of increasing size against natural baselines.

</details>


### [130] [Power-Efficiency and Scalability Analysis of Magnetically-Actuated Satellite Swarms via Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06286)
*Yuta Takahashi,Seang Shim,Hiraku Sakamoto,Shin-ichiro Sakai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于凸优化的磁驱动卫星集群功耗评估框架，解决了电磁力/力矩非线性模型带来的非凸功耗约束难题，证明了增加卫星数量可提升编队保持功耗效率，为大规模空间系统的磁驱动编队方案提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间分布式孔径可突破发射尺寸限制，实现高增益、窄波束和大孔径能力，但其长期维护面临轨道动力学不稳定和编队控制一致性的挑战，磁驱动策略虽可实现无推进剂位置控制，但电磁模型非线性导致的非凸功耗约束使系统级配置分析困难。

Method: 建立基于凸优化的分析框架，将电磁力与力矩模型的非线性特性转化为可处理的凸优化问题，评估大规模磁驱动卫星集群的功耗特性。

Result: 增加卫星数量可提高编队保持的功耗效率，磁驱动集群架构相比传统少量卫星电磁编队飞行方案具有更好的功耗效率。

Conclusion: 磁驱动卫星集群架构为构建大规模空间系统提供了一种节能替代方案，可作为传统少量卫星电磁编队飞行概念的补充。

Abstract: This correspondence presents a convex-optimization-based evaluation framework of satellite-swarm-based apertures maintained by magnetic-field interactions. Spaceborne distributed apertures are composed of multiple satellites and are attractive for scientific and commercial missions because their scalability enables high-gain, narrow-beam, and large-aperture capabilities beyond the launch-size limitations. A key challenge is that the long-term maintenance of such virtual structures requires consistent formation control amid unstable orbital dynamics, and magnetic interactions generated by satellite-mounted magnetorquers offer a desirable propellant-free position-control strategy. However, the nonlinearities of the electromagnetic force and torque model lead to a nonconvex power-consumption constraint, making system-level configuration analysis difficult. To address this issue, we develop a convex optimization-based framework to analyze the power consumption of large magnetically actuated satellite swarms. The resulting analysis shows that increasing the number of satellites can improve formation-keeping power efficiency. This indicates that magnetically actuated swarm architectures provide a power-efficient alternative to the conventional few-satellite electromagnetic formation-flight concept for constructing large-scale space systems.

</details>


### [131] [Improving the Efficiency of Language Agent Teams with Adaptive Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06320)
*Elizabeth Mieczkowski,Alexander Ku,Tiwalayo Eisape,Dilip Arumugam,John Matters,Katherine M. Collins,Ilia Sucholutsky,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LATTE（Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution）提出让 LLM 团队自行维护一张"动态协调任务图"（DAG）：节点为子任务、边为依赖、每个节点带执行者与状态，并定义 Discover/Assign/Claim/Complete/Release/Close/Verify 七类图变更算子，采用"Worker 提案—Lead 审批"的混合式集中-去中心协议、基于前沿（frontier）的自调度与心跳式掉队监控。实验表明 LATTE 在三个协作任务、两种基座模型上以约一半的 token、约 2/3 的时间取得最高准确率（80%），并大幅减少覆盖写、并发冲突、冗余输出与通信量，且使团队协调行为变得可解释可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体 LLM 团队的协调设计存在"两个极端"之间的根本张力：高度结构化的方法（MetaGPT、ChatDev 等固定角色/SOP 流水线、预先指定的任务分解）保证了一致性但抑制了 LLM 最关键的动态适应能力，且 Lead 的幻觉或崩溃会沿流水线向下游级联传播；完全去中心化的团队灵活且可扩展，但缺乏协调脚手架会导致相互覆盖、输出不一致、虚报任务完成、错误传播与资源浪费（token、时间、文件操作），在需要序贯推理或共享一致状态的场景下加人甚至会退化。此外，现有多智能体方法普遍缺少针对"协调质量"本身的度量指标与可审计性。论文的动机即：需要一个既维持全局一致性、又能让团队在执行过程中自主重构协调结构的框架，并回答"协调结构是否必须由系统设计者预先规定"这一假设。

Method: 框架受分布式系统调度协议启发，形式化为一个编排框架：（1）动态协调图 G_t=(V_t,E_t,λ_t) 为 DAG，V_t 为子任务节点、E_t 为完成依赖边、λ_t: V_t→(A∪{⊥})×S 记录执行者与状态（pending/assigned/in_progress/done/verified）；前沿 F_t 定义为依赖已全部 done 的 pending 节点集合，天然编码最大并行度。（2）七类图变更算子并给出明确前置/后置条件与不变量：Discover（Lead 或 Worker 新增子任务，唯一会增边者，保证无环）、Assign（Lead 指派）、Claim（Worker 从 F_t 自助领取，模仿 work-stealing，冲突由 orchestrator 按 FIFO 串行裁决）、Complete（Worker 报完成）、Release（Lead 把掉队任务退回 pending）、Close（Lead 强制完成）、Verify（Lead 插入验证节点）。（3）两层混合协调：Worker 依本地轨迹提案，Lead 凭全局视图审批，所有结构更新串行化以避免视图分歧；附录用 MCMC/Metropolis-Hastings 视角把"提案（Worker 局部采样）+ 评价（Lead 接受/拒绝）"解释为对任务图的后验推断与元推理。（4）执行协议分规划阶段（Lead 用 Discover 播种 G_0）与逐轮执行循环：心跳监测（H=4 轮无动作则标记掉队）→ 计算前沿 → 派发 min(|F_t|,|W|) 个 Worker → 并行执行与 Apply → 终止判断；Worker 上下文仅含本地子任务及前驱，Lead 仅见图与消息（实现上 Lead 保留最近 10 条、Worker 20 条消息）。（5）实验设置：1 Lead + 4 Worker、temperature 0.7、单次输出上限 4096 token、最多 40 轮；在探索性数据分析（HR 数据集 400 行、8 个不透明列名、3 个植入性质）、调试（信号处理库植入 8 个 bug）、库扩展（补全 2 个类 + 新建 6 个模块）三任务上，与 Leader-Worker、去中心化、静态图消融（规划后冻结图、禁用 discover/release/verify/self-claim）、MetaGPT（v0.1 固定 SOP 五角色）对比，每条件 2 个模型（Claude Sonnet 4-6、GPT-5.2）×10 次重复，共 300 次试验。

Result: 准确率：LATTE 汇总 80±4%，高于 Leader-Worker 74、去中心化 70、静态图 74、MetaGPT 58；整体 79.7%，显著优于静态图 57.6%（p<0.01）、MetaGPT 33.9%（p<0.01）、Leader-Worker 70.1%（p=0.04），与去中心化 73.9% 无显著差异（p=0.16）。成本：LATTE 平均 token 成本仅为次优静态图的 47.5%（MetaGPT 228.7%、去中心化 120.9%、Leader-Worker 104.2%，均 p<0.01）；wall-clock 为次优静态图的 66.7%（MetaGPT 289.0%、Leader-Worker 105.7%），去中心化 69.3% 但 p=0.34 不显著；表格显示汇总 token 148K vs 297–419K，耗时 3.5 分钟 vs 3.7–11.5 分钟。协调质量：覆盖写 4.3×/trial 对 Leader-Worker 22.8×（5.3× 降幅）与去中心化 35.4×（8.2× 降幅）；并发写 1.0× 对 8.5×/11.5×；浪费字符 5,236 对 45,436/78,062；消息数 20.4 对 31.4/34.8，通信字符 42,484 对 50,073/60,394；仅 48.7% 的轮次有活跃 agent（Leader-Worker 80%、去中心化 100%）；节点完成平均 39.2s vs 静态 75.6s，95 分位 130s vs 294s（2.3×）。行为涌现：Discover 最高频，Claim 高成功率显示去中心自调度生效，Release 在 36% 试验中被触发，Verify 在 19% 试验中被触发且集中在高不确定性（平均 18.1 轮）而非快速完成（8.1 轮）的试验中；动态图规模随时间大于静态图。开销分析：动态规划仅播种 3.8 个节点（14.5s）而静态一次规划 8.3 个节点（16.7s），Lead 活跃率 30.8% vs 14.8%（平均多 0.9 轮），但总执行 9.8 轮/148K token 对静态 15.9 轮/297K token，即每多一轮 Lead 开销约省六轮 Worker 执行。

Conclusion: 结论是：协调结构不必由系统设计者预先硬编码，让智能体在执行中在线共同维护并修订自己的协调图（显式、在线、代理维护的任务图），是同时获得效率、可靠性、适应性与可解释性的可行路径——LATTE 在准确率-成本 Pareto 前沿上支配现有团队设计，并显著降低协调失败。需要指出的局限与保留：图初始化带来规划开销，任务短小或单智能体即可时得不偿失；实验仅覆盖具有天然子任务边界的领域（编码/数据分析），开放式推理任务尚需扩展算子；团队规模固定为 N=5，扩展性未验证；去中心化基线在准确率与时延上的差异部分未达统计显著；图中显式的验证触发机制虽具启发性但尚未被强化；此外"建宽图而非深图""并行安全规则"等关键行为同时写在提示词中，方法的增益有多少来自协议本身、多少来自提示工程难以完全分离；论文正文个别数值与表 2（如去中心化准确率、token 比例）与摘要叙述之间存在轻微不一致。后续方向包括按协调结果做微调/强化学习、扩展到非结构化任务发现、规模化团队规模研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in teams, yet existing coordination approaches often occupy two extremes. Highly structured methods rely on fixed roles, pipelines, or task decompositions assigned a priori. In contrast, fully unstructured teams enable adaptability and exploration but suffer from inefficiencies such as error propagation, inter-agent conflicts, and wasted resources (measured in time, tokens, or file operations). We introduce Language Agent Teams for Task Evolution (LATTE), a framework for coordinating LLM teams inspired by distributed systems, where processors must operate under partial observability and communication constraints. In LATTE, a team of agents collaboratively construct and maintain a shared, evolving coordination graph which encodes sub-task dependencies, individual agent assignment, and the current state of sub-task progress. This protocol maintains consistency while empowering agents to dynamically allocate work, adapt coordination, and discover new tasks. Across multiple collaborative tasks and a variety of base models, we demonstrate how LATTE reduces token usage, wall-clock time, communication, and coordination failures (e.g. file conflicts and redundant outputs) while matching or exceeding the accuracy of standard designs including MetaGPT, decentralized teams, top-down Leader-Worker hierarchies, and static decompositions.

</details>


### [132] [AgenticPrecoding: LLM-Empowered Multi-Agent System for Precoding Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06443)
*Zijiu Yang,Zixiang Zhang,Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang,Zhiguo Shi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AgenticPrecoding 是一个由大语言模型驱动的通用多智能体框架，把"用户级通信需求"自动转化为可执行的预编码求解器：流程分为问题形式化、求解器选择、提示词上采样、代码生成四阶段，其中前两阶段用 LoRA 微调的 Qwen3-8B 注入预编码领域知识，后两阶段用 GPT-5.2 与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 负责提示工程与代码编写，并以外部执行反馈闭环修订代码。在 10 个代表性预编码场景上，该框架在多数设置下取得最优指标并保持 100% 可行率，显著优于 ZF/MMSE/SOCP/MRT/CD 等手工设计的非学习类基线，验证了多智能体架构用于物理层设计的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预编码是 6G 多天线系统中干扰管理与性能提升的核心手段，但现有方法普遍遵循"一个场景、一种建模、一个求解器"的范式（针对全双工、下行 MIMO、认知无线、恒包络、分布式天线、时空符号级、混合预编码等各自定制），难以适应 6G 面向 NTN、ISAC 等高度异构且持续演化的场景。深度学习方法虽能直接从数据学习映射，但在部署条件偏离训练分布时性能退化，且未显式刻画预编码问题的数学结构。已有的 LLM 类工作（LLM-Optira、BeamAgent、LLM-beamforming）要么实现可靠性差、频繁违反约束，要么仍绑定场景专用优化器，通用预编码自动化仍未解决。作者的核心洞察是：端到端推导涉及多类异质推理需求——问题形式化与求解器选择需要预编码领域知识与优化结构判断，而提示组织与代码合成主要依赖通用语言与编程能力，因此应由不同类型的智能体分工承担，而非让单一 LLM 承担全部推理。

Method: 1）统一建模：给出通用预编码优化形式 min f(x;Θ) s.t. 不等式约束 c_ℓ≤0 与等式约束 h_q=0，场景描述子 Θ={Θ_sys, Θ_ch, Θ_obj, Θ_con} 分别刻画系统配置、信道信息、优化目标与运行约束（最大化任务转为最小化负值）。任务被定义为自动求解器生成：输入 (D, Θ)（自然语言任务描述 + 结构化描述子），输出映射 G:(D,Θ)↦(𝒫,𝒜,C,x*)。

2）四阶段多智能体流水线：
- Problem Formulator Agent (PF)：将 (D,Θ) 映射为结构化文本问题规范 𝒫，识别设计变量（波束成形矩阵、符号级向量、混合模拟-数字预编码器或松弛引入的辅助变量）、目标、可行域与场景约束；输出为文本规格而非显式公式。
- Problem Solver Agent (PS)：依据目标类型、约束特征、凸性、变量耦合与硬件限制，从非学习类方法中选择凸化/半正定松弛/逐次凸近似/交替优化/罚函数等算法策略 𝒜。
- Prompt Upsampler Agent (PU)：把 (Θ,𝒜,𝒫) 组织为面向实现的提示 Π，明确变量、目标、约束、算法步骤、数值设置、输入输出格式与可行性检查。
- Code Generation Agent (CG)：生成可执行求解器代码 C。
前两个智能体均基于 Qwen3-8B 用 LoRA 监督微调（rank=8，2 epoch），训练数据来自 20 余篇预编码文献构造的指令-响应对（约 6500 条与 800 条）；后两个智能体分别使用 GPT-5.2（提示上采样）与 Gemini 3.1 Pro（代码生成）。求解器选择范围刻意限定在非学习类方法，以保留数学可解释性并降低对固定训练配置的依赖。

3）反馈驱动修订闭环：执行-评估过程 E 产生反馈 e^(t)（编译状态、运行状态、约束可行性、目标值、数值告警），由修订算子 R 更新 C^(t+1)=R(C^(t), e^(t))，可纠正实现错误、调整数值设置、修复约束违反并改进解质量，直至通过执行评估或达到终止条件。整体流程见文中公式 (5)。

Result: 在 9 个代表性场景 × 6 个 SNR（0–25 dB）的对比（表 I）中，AgenticPrecoding 在几乎所有设置下取得最优值：MU-MIMO 功率最小化 0.3144→0.0008 W，优于 SOCP 代理与穷举 MRT；恒包络 CI 裕度 1.8076→32.1447，优于 CD-CE（30.4381）；1-bit 混合 MISO 裕度 3.4530→61.4033，领先 MRT 1-bit（54.6772）；认知无线组播保密速率 1.8265→8.6522，优于 ZF-like 零陷；全双工 QAM 功率 0.2548→0.0673 W，明显优于出现约束违反（标 *）的 SINR-only；认知无线和率 7.1113→42.2928，显著领先 ZF（28.6721）；认知无线干扰鲁棒性和率 4.5831→33.5494；全双工 QoS 和率 5.7592→27.8034，略优于 RZF；混合预编码鲁棒性功率 0.0897→0.0002 W，优于受限交替优化（0.1576→0.0006）。多基线在严格约束下出现不可行/饱和（如 P=40 W）或数值不稳定（ZF-GS 在 0 dB 不收敛），而 AgenticPrecoding 全场景保持 100% 可行率、极少约束违反，在低 SNR 等噪声受限区间优势最明显。基线为人工选定的非学习类方法（ZF、RZF、MMSE、SOCP 代理、MRT、坐标下降、零陷投影等），学习类方法因场景绑定被排除。

Conclusion: 论文提出 AgenticPrecoding，通过 LoRA 领域适配的推理智能体与通用实现智能体分工协作，并结合反馈驱动的代码修订闭环，弥补了传统场景专用求解器在适应性与实现可靠性上的缺口；10 个异构预编码场景的实验表明其性能稳定领先且保持 100% 可行性，验证了多智能体架构用于物理层设计的可行性与前景。需要说明的局限包括：文中摘要与结论均称覆盖 10 个场景，而表 I 仅列出 9 个（场景 10 疑似在图 3/图 4 的功率最小化案例中单独呈现）；可复现性受限于多个闭源商业模型（GPT-5.2、Gemini 3.1 Pro）的参与；成本与时延、以及多智能体错误传播的定量分析尚未讨论；场景描述子 Θ 仍需结构化输入，并非完全由自然语言驱动。

Abstract: Precoding is a key technique for interference management and performance improvement in multi-antenna wireless systems. However, existing precoding methods are typically developed for specific system models, objectives, and constraint sets, which limits their adaptability to the heterogeneous and evolving scenarios expected in future 6G networks. To address this limitation, we propose AgenticPrecoding, a universal multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end precoding derivation directly from user-level communication requirements. Specifically, AgenticPrecoding decomposes the derivation process into four coordinated stages: problem formulation, solver selection, prompt upsampling, and code generation, assigning each stage to a specialized agent tailored to its specific reasoning demands. We employ two LoRA-adapted reasoning agents to inject precoding-specific domain knowledge for problem formulation and solver selection, while two general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) handle prompt refinement and executable code generation. Furthermore, a feedback-driven refinement mechanism is incorporated to enhance code executability, constraint feasibility, and solution quality. Extensive experiments across 10 representative precoding scenarios demonstrate that AgenticPrecoding achieves superior cross-scenario adaptability compared to conventional optimization-based and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Coordination Matters: Evaluation of Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06557)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Matthew Landers,Afsaneh Doryab*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文针对现有合作式多智能体强化学习评估仅关注结果指标而忽视协调过程的问题，提出协同感知评估视角，并构建STAT测试平台系统评估六种MARL方法。研究发现相同回报表现可能掩盖不同的协调机制，性能受分配压力和冗余选择等因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作式多智能体强化学习基准测试主要关注回报率、成功率或完成时间等汇总指标，这些指标无法揭示智能体在规模化的任务分配场景中如何协调决策。

Method: 提出STAT测试平台，在保持观测访问和任务规则不变的前提下，系统性变化智能体、任务和环境规模；评估了六种具有代表性的基于值函数的多智能体强化学习方法，涵盖不同集中化程度。

Result: 相似回报趋势可反映不同的协调机制（冗余分配、分配多样性、任务完成效率存在差异）；在约束承诺型任务分配中，规模下的性能不仅受制于标称动作空间大小，还受分配压力、稀疏决策机会和相互依赖智能体的冗余选择影响。

Conclusion: 协同感知评估应作为基于回报的基准测试的必要补充，以揭示智能体间的协调机制差异。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) benchmarks commonly emphasize aggregate outcomes such as return, success rate, or completion time. While essential, these metrics often fail to reveal how agents coordinate, particularly in settings where agents, tasks, and joint assignment choices scale combinatorially. We propose a coordination-aware evaluation perspective that supplements return with process-level diagnostics. We instantiate this perspective using STAT, a controlled commitment-constrained spatial task-allocation testbed that systematically varies agents, tasks, and environment size while holding observation access and task rules fixed. We evaluate six representative value-based MARL methods across varying levels of centralization. Our results show that similar return trends can reflect distinct coordination mechanisms, including differences in redundant assignment, assignment diversity, and task-completion efficiency. We find that in commitment-constrained task allocation, performance under scale is shaped not only by nominal action-space size, but also by assignment pressure, sparse decision opportunities, and redundant choices among interdependent agents. Our findings motivate coordination-aware evaluation as a necessary complement to return-based benchmarking for cooperative MARL.

</details>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Anatomy of a Query: W5H Dimensions and FAR Patterns for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05525)
*Vicki Stover Hertzberg,Eduardo Valverde,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST框架用于评估自然语言数据库接口系统，通过FAR结构不变量和W5H语义维度框架对120,464条查询进行验证，发现FAR具有跨领域普适性，而W5H维度分布存在显著差异，尤其是医疗领域的时间维度(80.4%)和人物维度(73.0%)高度集中，因果和机制性推理近乎为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自然语言数据库接口虽日益流行，但缺乏坚实的理论基础来评估和设计这些系统。现有研究缺乏对查询结构的系统性理论框架，导致无法深入理解不同领域查询的语义特征差异。

Method: 研究团队提出QUEST框架，包含两个独立动机的组成部分：(1) FAR结构不变量——假设每个格式正确的查询可归结为Filter（过滤）、Aggregate（聚合）、Return（返回）三种操作；(2) W5H维度框架——假设所有过滤条件可映射到Who、What、Where、When、Why、How六个语义维度。在五个text-to-SQL数据集（共120,464条样本）上进行验证。

Result: FAR一致性在所有领域和模式类型中普遍成立；W5H维度分布存在显著差异。医疗领域查询的时间维度(WHEN: 80.4%)和人物维度(WHO: 73.0%)高度集中，远超通用领域基准。因果(WHY)和机制性(HOW)推理在所有数据集中几乎为零，所谓的HOW例外实际上反映的是定量聚合而非真正的程序性推理。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了实现结构化数据真正机器推理必须跨越的前沿。现有系统在因果推理和机制性推理方面存在根本性缺陷，这不仅是技术问题，更是理论认知层面的挑战，需要重新审视自然语言接口与数据库交互中的深层推理能力。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to databases have gained popularity, yet the theoretical foundations for evaluating and designing these systems remain underdeveloped. We present QUEST (Query Understanding Evaluation through Semantic Translation), a framework resting on two independently motivated components: the FAR structural invariant, which holds that every well-formed query reduces to Filter, Aggregate, and Return operations; and the W5H dimensional framework, which holds that all filtering criteria map to six semantic dimensions (Who, What, Where, When, Why, and How). Validated across five text-to-SQL datasets (n = 120,464), FAR conformance is universal across all domains and schema types, while W5H dimensional profiles vary substantially. Healthcare queries are strongly concentrated in temporal (WHEN: 80.4%) and person-centric (WHO: 73.0%) dimensions far exceeding general-domain benchmarks, and causal (WHY) and mechanistic (HOW) reasoning are near-zero everywhere, with apparent HOW exceptions reflecting quantitative aggregation rather than genuine procedural reasoning. These results identify a frontier that must be crossed for genuine machine reasoning over structured data.

</details>


### [135] [An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)
*Sicheng Pan,Shuxian Wang,Wesley Zheng,Zirong Zeng,Vijay Sharma,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rulescript是一种用于开发查询重写规则的领域特定语言(DSL)，通过关系代数启发的核心语言和匹配-转换两阶段架构，实现了规则定义与执行引擎的解耦，并提供形式化正确性验证。它成功重实现了Calcite的33条规则并部署到两个不同引擎，证明了"一次编写，遍地部署"的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询重写系统存在三个核心问题：(1)重写规则通常需要手动实现，且与特定执行引擎紧密耦合；(2)缺乏形式化的正确性保证；(3)开发新引擎时需要重新实现遗留规则和新规则，导致工程成本高、可移植性受限，且每次新实现都可能引入新的bug。

Method: 提出Rulescript——一种与引擎无关的领域特定语言。其核心是一个关系代数启发的核心语言，通过将规则显式分解为匹配阶段和转换阶段来实现规则定义与执行基础设施的分离。用户通过核心操作符对查询计划进行模式匹配来表达重写规则，并构造语义等价的转换计划。Rulescript具有可扩展性：用户可以自定义操作符来捕获引擎特定语义。集成到现有系统仅需实现一个轻量级适配器，将Rulescript的核心和自定义操作符映射到目标引擎的操作符。

Result: 成功重实现了Apache Calcite中的33条重写规则，并扩展了多种自定义操作符；自动将这些规则部署到CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion两个后端显著不同的引擎中，验证了Rulescript的可移植性。

Conclusion: Rulescript实现了"一次编写，遍地部署"的查询计划重写范式，将规则定义与执行基础设施分离，使得将已有规则部署到新的数据引擎时仅需最小工作量。

Abstract: Logical query plan rewriting transforms a relational database query into an equivalent but more efficient form and is crucial to the performance of database-backed applications. In existing systems, rewrite rules are typically implemented manually, tightly coupled to specific execution engines, and often lack formal correctness guarantees. Consequently, developing a new engine requires reimplementing both legacy and new rules, incurring significant engineering cost, limiting portability, and every new implementation is an opportunity for introducing new bugs.
  We introduce Rulescript, an engine-agnostic domain-specific language (DSL) for developing query rewrite rules. Rulescript separates rule definition from execution infrastructure via a relational algebra-inspired core language and an explicit decomposition of rules into matching and transformation phases. Developers express rewrites by pattern-matching query plans using Rulescript's core operators and constructing semantically equivalent transformed plans, with all rewrites automatically verified formally to ensure correctness. Rulescript is extensible: users can define custom operators in terms of the core language to capture engine-specific semantics. To integrate with an existing system, developers need only implement a lightweight adapter that maps Rulescript's core and custom operators to the operators implemented in the target engine.
  We evaluate Rulescript by reimplementing 33 rewrite rules from Apache Calcite and extending the language with several custom operators. To demonstrate portability, we automatically deploy these rules to CockroachDB and Apache Data Fusion, two engines with substantially different backends. Our results show that Rulescript enables "write once, deploy everywhere" paradigm for query plan rewriting, with minimal effort required to deploy previously written rules on a new data engine.

</details>


### [136] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SkipDisk是一种磁盘-内存混合的近似最近邻搜索系统，通过专用pivot提升过滤下界精度、三级数据剪枝和异步I/O策略，在大幅降低内存占用（仅为HNSW的10%-20%）的同时达到与内存方法相当甚至更低的搜索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于磁盘的ANN方法磁盘访问仍是主要瓶颈，且现有的基于下界的过滤方法过于宽松，无法有效减少磁盘访问，导致高延迟问题。

Method: SkipDisk采用三层设计：(1)为每个点设计专用pivot以改进三角形不等式下界，实现更紧密的过滤；(2)三级数据剪枝策略保留内存中的关键信息数据；(3)异步I/O策略通过将邻居节点存储在内存中解耦内存搜索与磁盘访问。

Result: SkipDisk实现了HNSW约85%的延迟，内存占用约为10%；或HNSW约63%的延迟，内存占用约20%。

Conclusion: SkipDisk在保持与HNSW相当甚至更低的搜索延迟的同时，将内存占用降至HNSW的约10%-20%，验证了所提出的三个核心设计的有效性：专用pivot提升过滤精度、三级数据剪枝降低内存占用、异步I/O策略减少搜索延迟。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering. We further design an estimation-based approach based on this lower bound. Second, to reduce the memory footprint, we employ a three-level data pruning strategy to preserve informative data in memory. Third, to further reduce search latency, we design an asynchronous I/O strategy based on the decoupling of in-memory search and disk access by storing neighbor nodes in memory. Experiments show that our method achieves a latency of 85 of HNSW's latency with approximately 10 memory footprint, and a latency to 63 of HNSW's with a slightly higher memory footprint of around 20.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [DisastRAG: A Multi-Source Disaster Information Integration and Access System Based on Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05210)
*Bo Li,Zhitong Chen,Kai Yin,Junwei Ma,Yiming Xiao,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DisastRAG 是一个面向灾害管理的多路径检索增强生成（multi-path RAG）系统，将大语言模型与三类信息访问通道整合：精选灾害语料的文档检索、关系型灾害数据库的结构化查询，以及超出语料范围时的外部网络回退。其核心目标是弥补现有灾害信息系统与 RAG 框架"单一访问路径"的局限，支撑异构、时效性强、依赖情境的信息需求。实验表明，检索增强相对无检索基线在多选题上带来 12–23 个百分点的提升，在开放式生成的关键点覆盖率上最高提升 10.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害管理需要快速获取分布在结构化业务记录、非结构化机构文档与动态外部来源中的信息，但多数现有灾害信息系统和 RAG 框架围绕单一访问路径组织（通常是纯文档检索），无法处理异构、时效敏感、随情境变化的信息需求。例如，当问题涉及灾情统计数据库记录或语料库未覆盖的最新灾情时，文档式 RAG 会失效。因此需要一种能够统一编排多路径访问、并具备查询理解与策略路由能力的灾害感知信息集成系统。

Method: 系统采用多路径架构，统一封装查询理解（query understanding）、策略路由（strategy routing）、响应生成（response generation）与上下文记忆（contextual memory）四个模块。三条访问通道为：(1) 文档检索——针对精选灾害语料库；(2) 结构化访问——面向关系型灾害记录执行查询；(3) 外部网络回退——处理语料库外请求。检索环节在多个候选池规模（larger candidate pools）、多种检索配置（向量检索、混合检索、重排序深度 shallower/reranking 深度）下进行对比。评估使用四个开源大语言模型，任务涵盖多选题（multiple-choice，封闭式事实选择）与开放式问答（open-ended，以关键点覆盖率 keypoint coverage 计量），并与无检索基线对照；另设案例研究验证结构化访问与网络回退相对纯文档 RAG 的增益。

Result: 1) 检索增强一致优于无检索基线：多选题提升 12–23 个百分点，开放式关键点覆盖率最高提升 10.5 个百分点。2) 候选池规模的影响存在模型能力依赖性：更大的候选池对较弱模型帮助最大，而更强模型对检索噪声（retrieval noise）更敏感。3) 检索策略与任务形态匹配：混合检索（hybrid retrieval）在开放式覆盖率上表现最佳；向量检索与较浅的重排序（shallower reranking）更有利于封闭式事实选择。4) 案例研究显示，结构化访问与网络回退使框架能够回答文档式 RAG 无法覆盖的问题，扩展了系统的适用边界。

Conclusion: DisastRAG 证明多路径、灾害感知的 RAG 架构能有效提升灾害信息问答的准确性与覆盖度，且检索参数（候选池大小、重排序深度、混合策略）应与模型能力、任务类型协同调优：弱模型需更大候选池以保障召回，强模型需更严格的噪声过滤与重排序以保证精度，开放式生成偏好混合检索。该工作为灾害信息系统的异构数据统一访问与情境化检索策略选择提供了可行的架构范式。

Abstract: Effective disaster management requires rapid access to information distributed across structured operational records, unstructured institutional documents, and dynamic external sources. However, most existing disaster information systems and retrieval-augmented generation frameworks remain organized around a single access pathway, limiting their ability to support heterogeneous, time-sensitive, and context-dependent information needs. This study presents DisastRAG, a disaster-aware information integration and access system that combines large language models with retrieval-augmented access to structured, unstructured, and contextual disaster information. The framework is built around a multi-path architecture that supports document retrieval over a curated hazard corpus, structured access over relational disaster records, and external web fallback for out-of-corpus requests, while also incorporating query understanding, strategy routing, response generation, and contextual memory within a unified system. We evaluated the document retrieval performance using four open-source large language models across multiple retrieval configurations on multiple-choice and open-ended disaster information tasks. Retrieval augmentation consistently improves performance over no-retrieval baselines, yielding multiple-choice gains of 12-23 percentage points and open-ended keypoint coverage gains of up to 10.5 percentage points. Results show that larger candidate pools are most helpful for weaker models, while stronger models are more sensitive to retrieval noise. Hybrid retrieval performs best for open-ended coverage, whereas vector retrieval and shallower reranking more often favor closed-form factual selection. Case studies further show that structured access and web fallback extend the framework beyond document-only RAG.

</details>


### [138] [Dynamic Graph with Similarity-Aware Attention Graph Neural Network for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05238)
*Aadarsh Senapati,Neha Kujur,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DG-SA-GNN（Dynamic Graph Similarity-Aware Attention Graph Neural Network），在静态多相似度模型 SA-GNN 基础上引入两项关键改进：一是训练过程中按调度计划（epoch 0、1–9 每 3 轮、10 轮后每 5 轮）动态重建四张用户相似度图，使其拓扑与不断演化的嵌入空间保持一致；二是采用 mini-batch（batch=1024）训练配合 70% 硬负样本 + 30% 均匀负样本的混合采样策略以提升可扩展性与收敛速度。模型主体由四路并行 UserGNN（分别基于 Cosine、Jaccard、Discount PCC、IPI·IJ 相似度图）、Graph Transformer 跨视图融合、以及与 Top-50 物品嵌入交互的 CrossAttention 用户-物品对齐模块构成，配合 BPR 损失与 L2 正则训练。在 MovieLens100K（943 用户 / 1,682 物品 / 稀疏度 93.7%）留一法评测上，最佳变体 DG-SA-GNN(Discount PCC) 取得 Recall@20 = 0.1622、NDCG@20 = 0.0654，召回略优于 LightGCN 基线（0.1580），但 NDCG 略低（LightGCN 为 0.0663）。整体而言模型有效但提升幅度有限、组件消融不充分，可扩展性受 O(|U|²×|I|) 的成对相似度计算瓶颈限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同过滤与主流 GNN 推荐模型（如 LightGCN）存在三个核心缺陷：(1) 依赖静态的用户-物品二部图，未显式建模用户-用户关系，忽略了品味相似用户之间的协同信号；(2) 相似度图一旦构建即在整个训练中保持固定，而用户嵌入在优化过程中持续演化，导致固定拓扑与表征空间逐渐错配，传播过时（stale）的相似性信号并引入噪声；(3) 全图前向传播方式扩展性差，且随机均匀采样的负样本过于简单，梯度信号弱、收敛慢。此外，既往工作（TGAT、TGNN 等）多关注真实交互流的时间演化，而非"由相似度导出的图拓扑在训练过程中的自我演化"，这正是本文试图填补的空白。为此论文提出三个研究问题：动态重建相对静态构建的性能增益（RQ1）、四种相似度函数在动态图设置下各自贡献（RQ2）、mini-batch 硬负采样对收敛速度与最终质量的影响（RQ3）。

Method: 框架由五个组件构成。(1) 动态多相似度图构建模块：基于共评物品集上的 Cosine（角度一致性）、Jaccard（评分物品集合重叠）、Discount PCC（Pearson 相关系数乘以 |C_uv|/(|C_uv|+2) 的共评惩罚因子，负值截断为 0）、IPI·IJ（Discount PCC × Jaccard 混合度量）分别计算用户两两相似度，取 Top-K=30 近邻建边，权重按温度 0.2 的 softmax 锐化：w_uv = exp(5·S(u,v))/Σ_k exp(5·S(u,k))；归一化邻接矩阵 Â = D^{-1/2} A D^{-1/2}。重建调度为 epoch {0,3,6,9,10,15,20}，依据早期嵌入漂移快、后期趋于稳定而采用"前密后疏"的两阶段节奏；四种相似度相互独立，通过 Python multiprocessing pool 的 4 进程并行计算，使等效重建耗时接近单图开销，避免 O(4·|U|²) 的串行累积。(2) 四路并行 UserGNN：每个图独立传播，2 层图卷积含残差连接，层内更新为 h_u^new = α·h_u + Σ_{v∈N_u} w_uv h_v，随后 h_u ← h_u + ReLU(W h_u^new) 并做 L2 归一化；各层输出再经可学习标量 softmax 权重 α_l 跨层聚合。(3) Graph Transformer 融合：四路用户嵌入堆叠为 4×|U|×d 张量，沿"视图维度"作为序列输入 2 层 Transformer 编码器（4 头，前馈维 2d=256，dropout 0.1），对四个视图输出取均值池化得到统一用户嵌入 U。(4) CrossAttention 对齐：物品嵌入先由用户上下文增强 I' = L2Norm(I + RᵀU)；对每个用户取内积得分 Top-50 物品，以用户嵌入为 query、这 50 个物品嵌入为 key/value 做 4 头多头注意力，得到 z_u，再计算 u*_u = L2Norm(u_u + W_c z_u) 精炼用户表示。(5) 训练与预测：每个 epoch 前向计算全量用户嵌入 U 与物品嵌入 I'，每步均匀采样 1024 个用户，正样本从其交互中均匀抽取，负样本以 0.7 概率从当前 Top-200 未交互物品硬负池（每 T 步刷新以摊销检索成本）抽取、0.3 概率全局均匀抽取，以 30% 容易负样本作为正则化器抑制假负样本；损失为 BPR + λ‖Θ‖²（λ=1e-4），Adam 学习率 0.001，梯度裁剪最大范数 5，训练 20 epoch，嵌入维度 d=128；推理得分 ŷ_ui = u*_uᵀ e'_i。评测采用 leave-one-out 划分，指标为 Recall@20 与 NDCG@20。

Result: 在 MovieLens100K 上，DG-SA-GNN(Discount PCC) 的 Recall@20 = 0.1622，为全表最高，略超 LightGCN 基线的 0.1580；DG-SA-GNN(IPI·IJ) 的召回与基线持平（0.1580）；Jaccard（0.1516）与 Cosine（0.1347）低于基线。NDCG@20 方面 LightGCN 最高（0.0663），DG-SA-GNN(Discount PCC) 为 0.0654（次之），IPI·IJ 0.0618、Jaccard 0.0597、Cosine 0.0553。作者据此认为在显式评分数据下，带共评数量可靠性折扣的相关型度量（Discount PCC）最有效，纯粹集合重叠（Jaccard）因丢失评分幅度信息而不足，而 IPI·IJ 的双重惩罚在该数据集上校准良好。作者解释 LightGCN 在 NDCG 上的优势可能来自二部图中直接的物品共现信号，而纯用户相似度图无法捕获该信息，并将其列为未来改进方向（联合建模二部图）。需要指出的是，论文的实证支撑相对薄弱：仅在单一小型数据集（943 用户）上评测、与基线相比仅有约 2.7% 的召回相对提升（且 NDCG 反而落后），且缺少真正支撑三个研究问题的系统消融（未给出动态 vs 静态图、四种相似度融合方式、硬负采样比例、Graph Transformer/CrossAttention 各组件的对比实验数据），与多项工作的比较均为文字描述而非表格数值。模型本身的可扩展性亦受限于成对相似度计算的 O(|U|²×|I|) 复杂度，作者承认在大规模数据集上需引入 FAISS/LSH 等近似近邻方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 DG-SA-GNN 通过"动态多相似度用户图 + 并行 UserGNN + Graph Transformer 跨视图融合 + CrossAttention 用户-物品对齐 + mini-batch 硬负采样"的组合，在 MovieLens100K 上取得了优于 LightGCN 的 Recall@20（0.1622 vs 0.1580），验证了"周期性重建相似度图使邻域拓扑跟随嵌入空间演化"这一设计在召回指标上的可行性与有效性，并确认相关型相似度（Discount PCC）最契合显式评分数据，混合硬负采样兼顾了梯度强度与假负样本风险。论文明确承认的局限与后续工作方向包括：(i) 成对相似度计算的二次复杂度需用 FAISS 近似近邻解决以支撑 Yelp2018 等大规模数据；(ii) 将用户相似度图与用户-物品二部图在同一架构中联合建模以补足共现信号、争取 NDCG 超越基线；(iii) 引入可学习的自适应相似度函数选择权重，替代当前人工设定的 K=30、softmax 温度 0.2 与 0.7/0.3 采样比例等启发式超参；(iv) 扩展至显式建模交互动态的时序推荐场景。总体而言，本文工作属于对既有 SA-GNN 的工程化扩展（动态化 + 训练策略改进），思路清晰、实现完整，但实验规模与消融深度尚不足以充分支撑其组件级结论。

Abstract: Recommender systems are essential components of modern online platforms which presents personalized content in various domain. The traditional collaborative filtering methods depends on static user-item interaction graphs and a limited subset of similarity measures which fail to capture the changing nature of preferences of an individual. Recent graph neural network (GNN) based approaches focus on user-item bipartite graphs which do not use explicit user-user relational modelling and dynamic graph evolution during training. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a Dynamic Graph SimilarityAware Attention Graph Neural Network (DG-SA-GNN) framework that integrates dynamic user similarity graph construction with multi-similarity propagation and attention-based aggregation. The proposed architecture constructs four parallel user similarity graphs using Cosine, Jaccard, Discounted Pearson Correlation Coefficient (Discount PCC), and IPIJ similarity functions, each processed by a dedicated UserGNN module. A Graph Transformer fuses the four graph views, and a CrossAttention module refines user embeddings through interaction with item embeddings. Crucially, the graphs are reconstructed at scheduled epochs during training, enabling the model to adapt to the learned embedding space constituting the dynamic graph component. Mini-batch training with hard negative sampling improves scalability and convergence. Experiments on the MovieLens100K benchmark demonstrate that DG-SA-GNN achieves a Recall@20 of 0.162 and NDCG@20 of 0.065 which is better than the LightGCN baseline in recall. The results validate that dynamic multi-similarity graph construction coupled with attention-based fusion which produce recommendation performance

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Semantic Similarity: Rethinking Retrieval for Agentic Search via Direct Corpus Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05242)
*Zhuofeng Li,Haoxiang Zhang,Cong Wei,Pan Lu,Ping Nie,Yi Lu,Yuyang Bai,Shangbin Feng,Hangxiao Zhu,Ming Zhong,Yuyu Zhang,Jianwen Xie,Yejin Choi,James Zou,Jiawei Han,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出并形式化了「直接语料交互」（Direct Corpus Interaction, DCI）这一新的智能体检索范式：智能体不再通过嵌入模型、向量索引或 top-k 检索 API 访问语料，而是像人类研究者一样，用 grep/rg、文件读取、shell 命令等通用终端工具直接在原始语料上搜索与验证。该方法无需离线索引，可自然适应动态语料，在 BrowseComp-Plus、多跳 QA 与 IR 排序任务上显著优于 BM25、稠密检索与推理型重排器等强基线，并提出「检索接口分辨率」（retrieval interface resolution）这一概念解释其增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索系统（稀疏 BM25 / 稠密向量 / 重排器）通过固定的相似度接口把语料访问压缩为一次 top-k 检索，再进入推理阶段。论文指出这一抽象对智能体搜索构成瓶颈：精确词法约束、稀疏线索的合取、局部上下文校验、多步假设细化难以用现成检索器表达；被早期过滤掉的证据无法被下游更强的推理「找回」，且召回天花板限制了后续推理的上限。随着智能体需要多轮规划、发现中间实体、组合弱线索并根据部分证据修订计划，瓶颈从「后检索推理」转移到「检索接口本身」本身。此外，真实智能体工作空间中的语料常是本地、异构、持续演化的，离线嵌入索引难以维护。因此作者主张：检索应被视为接口设计问题（granularity 决定智能体能看到、验证和操作什么），而不仅是检索器设计问题。

Method: 1) 范式定义：对比「检索器中介访问」（查询→排序 top-k→基于候选迭代）与 DCI（直接用终端工具操作原始语料）。DCI 提供一组小而可组合的原语：find/glob（结构导航）、grep/rg（精确与正则匹配）、head/tail/sed（局部检视）、轻量脚本（python -c），并可通过管道组合以强制词法约束（grep 'foo' f | grep 'bar'）、聚合弱线索、就地验证假设。2) 两个实现：DCI-Agent-Lite（基于 Pi 的最小化脚手架，仅暴露 bash 与 read，无任何检索模块）与 DCI-Agent-CC（基于 Claude Code 的强脚手架，禁用 web-search/web-fetch/subagents 并屏蔽答案泄漏目录），分别配 GPT-5.4 nano 与 Claude Sonnet 4.6。3) 运行时上下文管理：截断（truncation）、压缩（compaction，零 LLM 的内存操作）、摘要（summarization）三种机制，构造 L0–L4 五档策略。4) 评测指标新贡献：提出轨迹级 Coverage（any/mean/all，衡量是否触达 gold 文档）与 Localization（用固定宽度字符段归一化 ψ(a;b)=max(1−log a/log b,0)，衡量能否在文档内收敛到细粒度证据片段），并给出 DCI 工具输出到 snippet 的保守对齐规则。5) 实验覆盖 BrowseComp-Plus、六个多跳 QA 数据集（NQ/TriviaQA/Bamboogle/HotpotQA/2Wiki/MuSiQue，2018 Wikipedia 语料）、BRIGHT 四个子集与 BEIR 两个子集，基线含 BM25、OpenAI-text-embedding-3-large、GTE-Qwen2-7B、Qwen3-Embedding-8B、E5、Rank-R1、Rank1、ReasonRank 以及 R1-Searcher、Search-R1、ZeroSearch、Verl-Tool-Search、ASearcher 等检索智能体。6) 六个研究问题（RQ1–RQ6）：成熟 DCI 智能体能力、增益机制、行为权衡、语料规模扩展、上下文管理影响、工具集表达力，并附失败案例分析。

Result: RQ1（主结果）：BrowseComp-Plus 上同用 Claude Sonnet 4.6，将 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 换成 DCI，准确率 69.0%→80.0%（+11.0），成本 $1,440→$1,016（−29.4%）；DCI-Agent-CC 还超过最强检索基线 GPT-5+Qwen3-Embedding-8B（71.7%）8.3 点；DCI-Agent-Lite 以 $93 成本达 62.9%，与 o3+检索器（66.0%）相当而省 $647。多跳 QA：DCI-Agent-CC 平均 83.0%，超最强检索智能体 ASearcher-Local-14B（52.3%）30.7 点，DCI-Agent-Lite 68.0%；HotpotQA +30、2Wiki +26、MuSiQue +50。IR 排序：DCI-Agent-CC 平均 NDCG@10 68.5，在全部六个数据集上最佳，超 ReasonRank-32B（47.0%）21.5 点；Lite 版 56.7，仍高出 9.7 点。RQ2（机制）：CC 相对匹配检索智能体答对 176 题、仅输 76 题；176 题中仅 34 题是检索器完全未召回 gold，其余 142 题已召回部分或全部 gold（83 题为部分链失效，59 题为召回 100% 却未利用好）。工具分布：Bash 62.4%、Grep 33.0%，Bash 内部以链式搜索 22.3%、局部上下文窥视 18.0%、正则匹配 17.0%、文件定位 14.0% 为主，全文读取仅 9.1%。RQ3：Lite 的平均 gold 覆盖仅 28.0（低于 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 的 56.7），但 coverage_any 相近（70.0 vs 74.0），localization 高达 48.4 vs 21.7，即以「少召回、精细用」换取 +28 点准确率，代价是工具调用约翻倍（35 vs 18）。RQ4（规模）：语料从 100K 扩到 200K，工具调用 38.5→86.9、时延与成本翻倍、准确率降 13.6 点；400K 时准确率降至 37.5%、调用 122.4 次，20 例耗尽工具预算——DCI 深度可扩展但宽度成本陡增。RQ5（上下文管理）：呈非单调，L3 最优（准确率 77），L1 保留证据最多（31.3）但准确率仅 75，L2 最便宜但最差（69），L4 回落（73），存在「选择性遗忘」的最优区间。RQ6（工具表达力）：仅 read+grep 已达 61%，超检索基线 45% 16 点且工具调用量相当；放开 bash 再 +12 点（73%），但成本与时延大幅上升。另附案例分析（成功与失败各若干），失败模式显示窄泛 rg 模式易发散、可能幻觉答案。

Conclusion: 论文将 DCI 形式化为智能体搜索的一种替代检索范式：在排名型 IR、多跳 QA 与端到端智能体搜索基准上，DCI 在无需任何离线索引的情况下已具竞争力，并在 BrowseComp-Plus 上同时提升准确率与降低成本。受控消融与轨迹级分析表明其增益主要源于「检索接口分辨率」：DCI 的成功更多在于把已触达的证据转化为更高价值的局部检视、验证与组合式搜索步骤，而非恢复更完整的 gold 链。作者据此主张，未来工作不应只评测检索模型，还应评测可供强大智能体使用的语料接口与交互粒度；稠密/稀疏检索仍适用于大规模静态语料，但在本地、异构、持续演化的语料环境中，DCI 式终端交互是一个值得重视的接口设计点。

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems, whether lexical or semantic, expose a corpus through a fixed similarity interface that compresses access into a single top-k retrieval step before reasoning. This abstraction is efficient, but for agentic search, it becomes a bottleneck: exact lexical constraints, sparse clue conjunctions, local context checks, and multi-step hypothesis refinement are difficult to implement by calling a conventional off-the-shelf retriever, and evidence filtered out early cannot be recovered by stronger downstream reasoning. Agentic tasks further exacerbate this limitation because they require agents to orchestrate multiple steps, including discovering intermediate entities, combining weak clues, and revising the plan after observing partial evidence. To tackle the limitation, we study direct corpus interaction (DCI), where an agent searches the raw corpus directly with general-purpose terminal tools (e.g., grep, file reads, shell commands, lightweight scripts), without any embedding model, vector index, or retrieval API. This approach requires no offline indexing and adapts naturally to evolving local corpora. Across IR benchmarks and end-to-end agentic search tasks, this simple setup substantially outperforms strong sparse, dense, and reranking baselines on several BRIGHT and BEIR datasets, and attains strong accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus and multi-hop QA without relying on any conventional semantic retriever. Our results indicate that as language agents become stronger, retrieval quality depends not only on reasoning ability but also on the resolution of the interface through which the model interacts with the corpus, with which DCI opens a broader interface-design space for agentic search.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Dependable Retrieval-Augmented Generation Using Factual Confidence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05244)
*Florian Geissler,Francesco Carella,Laura Fieback,Jakob Spiegelberg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段方法预测RAG输出的事实忠实度：第一阶段用共形预测筛选高置信度检索块，第二阶段用注意力分类器检测生成答案与检索上下文的一致性。该方法为构建认证型RAG系统提供了理论基础和实践方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索到的上下文可能包含无关甚至误导性信息，导致生成答案出现事实性错误。需要为RAG系统的输出提供有意义的事实性置信度度量，以确保生成内容的可靠性。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：1) 使用共形预测(Conformal Prediction)筛选高置信度检索块；2) 使用基于注意力的真实性分类器(Attention-based Factuality Classifier)量化生成答案与检索上下文的语义一致性。

Result: 共形预测方法在部分数据集上可将答案质量提升6%；基于注意力的一致性检测器可识别高达77%的不一致答案。

Conclusion: 研究提出了两种技术方案来提升RAG系统的可靠性：共形预测用于过滤低质量检索结果，注意力机制分类器用于检测生成答案与检索上下文的矛盾。这为构建认证型RAG系统提供了方法论基础，但共形预测的交换性假设在实际应用中存在局限。

Abstract: Incorporating specific knowledge into large language models via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widespread technique that fuels many of today's industry AI applications. A fundamental problem is to assess if the context retrieved by some similarity search provides indeed supporting facts, or instead misguides the generator with irrelevant information. It is critical to associate meaningful confidence measures about the factuality of the retrieval process with the generated answers. We present a new, two-staged approach to predict fact faithfulness of the output of retrieval-augmented generations. First, we employ conformal prediction to select only those retrieved chunks who have a high chance to come from the correct source. This approach in itself can improve answer quality by up to 6% in some of the studied datasets, however, the associated statistical guarantees do not hold generally, since the assumption of sample exchangeability depends on the retriever setup. We present diagnostic metrics to assess whether a setup is suitable. Second, we quantify confidence in the consistency of a generated final answer with a given retrieved context, using an attention-based factuality classifier. This approach can detect inconsistent answers with a chance of up to 77%. Our work helps to establish a novel type of certified RAG systems for a broad range of natural language industry applications.

</details>


### [141] [TriAlignGR: Triangular Multitask Alignment with Multimodal Deep Interest Mining for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05249)
*Yangchen Zeng,Hao Peng,Rongfeng Guo,Zhenyu Yu,Zhiyuan Hu,Jinze Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TriAlignGR，一个统一的多任务多模态生成式推荐框架，通过两阶段多模态语义传播解决现有SID方法中的内容降级(SCD)和语义不透明(SSO)两大核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Semantic ID (SID)推荐管道存在两个未充分探索的根本性问题：(1) SID内容降级(SCD)：级联编码和残差量化丢弃了关键的多模态和兴趣级语义；(2) SID语义不透明(SSO)：模型自回归生成SID序列却未真正理解其底层含义，导致幻觉和泛化差。现有工作最多只解决文本-SID对齐，完全未利用视觉语义和潜在用户兴趣。

Method: TriAlignGR通过三个紧密集成的组件解决上述问题：(1) 跨模态语义对齐(CMSA)：通过VLM生成的文本描述和多模态嵌入模型将视觉内容整合到SID构建中；(2) 多模态深度兴趣挖掘(MDIM)：利用LLM思维链推理提取超越表面属性的潜在用户意图；(3) 三角多任务(TMT)：在单一自回归损失下联合训练8个互补生成任务，包括两个新颖的视觉语义任务(VisDesc→SID, VisDesc→Title)。

Result: SID内在携带多模态语义，完成了SID-Text-Image三角关系，无需任务特定塔或复杂损失加权。

Conclusion: TriAlignGR通过两阶段多模态语义传播机制，首次系统性地解决了SID推荐中的内容降级和语义不透明问题，实现视觉语义与文本语义在SID表示中的深度融合。

Abstract: We introduce TriAlignGR, a unified multitask-multimodal framework for generative recommendation that establishes two-stage multimodal semantic propagation: (i) encoding visual semantics directly into SIDs via multimodal embeddings, and (ii) enabling the model to decode these semantics through visual description tasks. Existing Semantic ID (SID) pipelines suffer from two fundamental but underexplored problems: \textbf{SID Content Degradation (SCD)}, where cascaded encoding and residual quantization discard critical multimodal and interest-level semantics; and \textbf{SID Semantic Opacity (SSO)}, where models autoregressively generate SID sequences without truly comprehending their underlying meaning, leading to hallucination and poor generalization. Prior work addresses at most text-SID alignment, leaving visual semantics and latent user interests entirely unexploited. TriAlignGR resolves both problems through three tightly integrated components: (1)~\textbf{Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA)} integrates visual content into SID construction through both VLM-generated textual descriptions and a multimodal embedding model that directly encodes image features alongside text, ensuring that SIDs inherently carry multimodal semantics; (2)~\textbf{Multimodal Deep Interest Mining (MDIM)} leverages LLM Chain-of-Thought reasoning to extract latent user intents (\eg ``productivity-focused lifestyle'' from noise-canceling headphones) beyond surface attributes, enriching SID semantics before discretization; and (3)~\textbf{Triangular Multitask (TMT)} jointly trains on eight complementary generation tasks under a single autoregressive loss -- including two novel visual-semantic tasks (VisDesc$\to$SID, VisDesc$\to$Title) that map VLM-generated image descriptions to SIDs and titles, completing the SID-Text-Image triangle -- without requiring task-specific towers or complex loss weighting.

</details>


### [142] [Decision-aware User Simulation Agent for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05250)
*Yuan-Chi Li,Li-Chi Chen,Sung-Yi Wu,Yu-Che Tsai,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.IR
