Conclusions: Dependencyaware ecosystem impact modeling provides a transparent and systematic basis for prioritizing support in large-scale software ecosystems. Our findings suggest that effective support strategies, driven by ecosystem stewards, funding bodies, and organizations operating support programs, should complement existing allocation logic with impact-informed decision making.

</details>


### [170] [Teaching LLMs Program Semantics via Symbolic Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06184)
*Jonas Bayer,Stefan Zetzsche,Olivier Bouissou,Remi Delmas,Michael Tautschnig,Soonho Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型在C代码形式化验证中的薄弱环节——属性违反检测能力不足的问题，提出使用符号执行引擎生成的bug traces对Qwen3-8B进行继续预训练，并结合链式思维推理。约3000条bug traces即可使8B模型在违反检测任务上超越4倍规模的Qwen3-32B，且训练与推理策略的组合具有超加性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在代码属性验证任务中虽然整体准确率较高，但存在关键缺陷：属性违反（violation）检测能力差异大，且随程序长度增加性能急剧下降。这严重限制了模型在实际代码安全分析中的应用价值。

Method: 1. 构建500个C验证任务，涵盖5类属性（内存安全、溢出、终止性、可达性、数据竞争），基于SV-COMP 2025；2. 评估14个模型、6个模型家族；3. 使用Soteria符号执行引擎在开源C代码上生成bug traces，对Qwen3-8B进行继续预训练；4. 推理时采用链式思维（chain-of-thought）策略。

Result: 仅约3000条bug traces结合链式思维推理，使违反检测能力提升超过17个百分点，8B模型在违反检测上超越4倍规模的Qwen3-32B（不使用thinking模式），整体准确率也接近该大模型。训练与推理策略的组合效果呈超加性：单独使用任一方法提升有限，但组合使用效果显著。改进可迁移至所有5类属性，包括训练未直接针对的类型。

Conclusion: 1. 基于符号执行traces的继续预训练能有效提升模型的代码验证能力；2. 提升来源于trace语义而非代码数量，trace的筛选和格式设计至关重要；3. 链式思维推理与trace训练的组合是关键，二者缺一不可；4. 方法具有良好的泛化性，可迁移至多种属性类型。

Abstract: We introduce an evaluation framework of 500 C verification tasks across five property types (memory safety, overflow, termination, reachability, data races) built on SV-COMP 2025, and evaluate 14 models across six families. We find that high overall accuracy masks a critical weakness: while most models reliably confirm properties hold, violation detection varies widely and degrades sharply with program length. To close this gap, we train on formal verification artifacts: running the Soteria symbolic execution engine on generic open-source C code and using the resulting traces for continued pretraining of Qwen3-8B. Just ${\sim}$3,000 bug traces combined with chain-of-thought reasoning at inference time improve violation detection by over 17 percentage points, producing one of the most balanced accuracy profiles among evaluated models. On violation detection, the trained 8B model outperforms the 4$\times$ larger Qwen3-32B without thinking and approaches it in overall accuracy. The interaction between trace training and chain-of-thought is superadditive: neither alone provides meaningful gains, but their combination does. Improvements transfer across all five property types, including ones the training traces do not target. Our 28 configurations confirm the gains stem from trace semantics, not code volume, and that trace curation and format matter.

</details>


### [171] [SiblingRepair: Sibling-Based Multi-Hunk Repair with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06209)
*Xinyu Liu,Jiayu Ren,Yusen Wang,Qi Xin,Xiaoyuan Xie,Jifeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SiblingRepair，一种新的LLM驱动的多hunk自动程序修复技术，专门用于兄弟修复。该技术通过token和embedding匹配搜索语义相关兄弟候选，使用LLM识别失败相关兄弟并生成一致补丁（同时/迭代策略），在Defects4J和GHRB上显著优于Hercules等SOTA技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA技术Hercules存在局限性：1)对兄弟位置和提交历史可用性的强假设；2)基于AST的刚性兄弟匹配；3)基于模板的僵硬补丁生成。这些限制阻碍了自动化程序修复的准确性和一致性。

Method: SiblingRepair采用LLM驱动的多hunk修复方法：1)基于token和embedding的代码匹配搜索语义相关的兄弟候选；2)通过LLM识别失败相关的兄弟；3)同时修复策略（联合修复兄弟）和迭代修复策略（渐进式分析候选构建补丁）；4)保留早期可疑位置的补丁并合并为泛化的多hunk补丁。

Result: SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于SOTA多hunk修复技术，证明了其修复效率、兄弟检测和修复组件的有效性，以及LLM数据泄漏的有限影响。

Conclusion: SiblingRepair在Defects4J和GHRB基准测试中显著优于包括Hercules在内的SOTA多hunk修复技术，展示了其修复效率、组件有效性和有限的LLM数据泄漏影响，为自动兄弟修复和通用多hunk修复领域做出了贡献。

Abstract: Developers often make similar mistakes across code locations implementing related functionalities. These locations, called siblings, share similar issues and require similar fixes. Accurately identifying siblings and consistently repairing them are crucial for automated program repair. Hercules is a SOTA technique designed for sibling repair. However, it is limited by strong assumptions about sibling locations and commit-history availability, rigid AST-based sibling matching, and inflexible template-based patch generation. To address these limitations, we present SiblingRepair, a new LLM-based multi-hunk APR technique specialized for sibling repair. Starting from a suspicious location identified by spectrum-based fault localization, SiblingRepair searches for semantically related sibling candidates using token- and embedding-based code matching, without restricting discovery to failing-test coverage or commit history. It then uses an LLM to identify failure-relevant siblings and generate consistent patches through two complementary strategies: simultaneous repair, which jointly repairs siblings, and iterative repair, which progressively analyzes candidates for patch construction. SiblingRepair further preserves promising patches generated from earlier suspicious locations and combines them into generalized multi-hunk patches. We evaluate SiblingRepair on the Defects4J and GHRB benchmarks. The results show that SiblingRepair substantially outperforms SOTA multi-hunk repair techniques including Hercules. Our evaluation further demonstrates its repair efficiency, the effectiveness of its sibling detection and repair components, and limited impact of the LLM data leakage on the results. Overall, SiblingRepair advances automated sibling and general multi-hunk repair.

</details>


### [172] [Correct Code, Vulnerable Dependencies: A Large Scale Measurement Study of LLM-Specified Library Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06279)
*Chengjie Wang,Jingzheng Wu,Xiang Ling,Tianyue Luo,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模分析了LLM生成Python代码中第三方库版本选择的安全与兼容风险。通过在1,000个Stack Overflow任务的基准测试集PinTrace上评估10个LLM，发现LLM指定的版本中存在大量已知CVE（36.70%-55.70%的任务），且多数具有高严重性，同时版本兼容率较低，揭示了LLM版本选择是一个被忽视但重要的风险面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM已被广泛应用于软件开发流程，但其生成的代码中包含的第三方库（TPL）版本选择可能带来安全和兼容风险，此前尚无系统研究。研究者希望首次对LLM版本级风险进行大规模测量研究。

Method: 构建了包含1,000个Stack Overflow编程任务的PinTrace基准测试集，评估了10个主流LLM。研究从版本指定率、漏洞暴露率、CVE公开时间、静态兼容性、动态测试通过率等多个维度进行分析，并测试了外部锚定版本约束的效果。

Result: （1）直接提示时版本指定率为26.83%-95.18%，创建依赖清单文件时为6.45%-59.19%；（2）指定版本中36.70%-55.70%的任务包含已知CVE，62.75%-74.51%为Critical或High严重性；（3）72.27%-91.37%的CVE在模型知识截止日期前已公开；（4）各模型收敛于相同的少量风险版本，表明存在系统性偏见；（5）静态兼容率19.70%-63.20%，动态测试通过率6.49%-48.62%；（6）外部锚定版本约束可显著降低漏洞暴露和兼容失败。

Conclusion: LLM的版本选择是一个此前被忽视的一级风险面，需要引起开发者和模型提供商的重视。所有模型都存在系统性偏见而非偶发错误。外部锚定的版本约束策略可有效缓解此类风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now largely involved in software development workflows, and the code they generate routinely includes third-party library (TPL) imports annotated with specific version identifiers. These version choices can carry security and compatibility risks, yet they have not been systematically studied.
  We present the first large-scale measurement study of version-level risk in LLM-generated Python code, evaluating 10 LLMs on PinTrace, a curated benchmark of 1,000 Stack Overflow programming tasks. LLMs tend to specify version identifiers when directly prompted at 26.83%-95.18%, while down to 6.45%-59.19% in creating a manifest file directly. Among the specified versions, 36.70%-55.70% of tasks contain at least one known CVE, and 62.75%-74.51% of them carry Critical or High severity ratings. In 72.27%-91.37% of cases, the associated CVEs were publicly disclosed before the model's knowledge cutoff. The statistics show all models converge on the same small set of risky release versions, indicating a systemic bias rather than isolated model error. Static compatibility rates range from 19.70% to 63.20%, with installation failure as the dominant cause. The dynamic test cases confirm the pattern by 6.49%-48.62% pass rates. Further experiments confirm that these failures are attributable to version selection rather than code quality, and that externally anchored version constraints substantially reduce both vulnerability exposure and compatibility failures. Our findings reveal LLM version selection as a first-class, previously overlooked risk surface in LLM-based development. We disclosed these findings to the community of the evaluated models, and several confirmed the issue. All the code and dataset have been released for open science at https://github.com/dw763j/PinTrace.

</details>


### [173] [Constraint Decay: The Fragility of LLM Agents in Backend Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06445)
*Francesco Dente,Dario Satriani,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了 LLM 智能体在「多文件后端代码生成」中处理结构性约束（架构模式、数据库、ORM）的能力，发现「约束衰减（Constraint Decay）」现象：约束密度从 L0（仅指定 Web 框架）提升到 L3（框架+Clean Architecture+PostgreSQL/SQLite+ORM）时，评测配置的断言通过率（A%）平均下降约 27.3~27.5 个百分点（摘要中称约 30 点），弱配置甚至接近零；中型模型在轻量显式框架（Express/Koa/Flask）与约定繁重框架（Django/FastAPI/Hono）之间存在 43–69 个百分点的差距；失败根因中数据层缺陷（查询逻辑错误 + ORM 运行时错误）占逻辑错误的约 48%。作者开源了 80 个全新生成任务、20 个功能实现任务、评估流水线和智能体轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在宽松规格下的自主代码生成（原型、Demo、PoC）表现优异，但生产级后端必须同时满足功能与非功能要求：遵循 API 契约、遵守架构分层、接入指定数据库、使用指定 ORM。现有基准（绿色field生成、SWE-Bench 类 Issue 修复、单文件提示生成、骨架补全）要么奖励「功能正确但结构任意」的方案，要么只考察功能维度，系统性地忽略了非功能约束的影响；此前也没有研究在固定 API 契约下逐层叠加结构性约束会如何改变智能体表现。此外，生产实践还要求智能体能从既有代码库中「隐式」推断并遵守约定（如约定繁重的 Django/FastAPI），这一点同样缺乏评测。据此作者提出三个研究问题：约束累积的影响（RQ1）、框架敏感性（RQ2）、失败根因（RQ3）。

Method: 受行为驱动开发（BBD）启发，固定功能规格、系统变化非功能规格。①规格：采用 OpenAPI 3.0（源自 RealWorld Conduit API，19 个端点、5 类资源、约 500 行 YAML），以消除歧义并可预定义跨语言/跨框架的通用行为测试。②约束维度：Web 框架（8 个：Python 3.12 的 Flask/FastAPI/Django/aiohttp，Node.js 20 的 Express/Fastify/Hono/Koa，始终激活）+ 架构模式（Clean Architecture 四层：routes/handlers、services、models、repository，层间单向依赖）+ 数据库（SQLite 或 PostgreSQL，启动自建 schema）+ ORM（SQLAlchemy 或 Sequelize），组合成 L0（8 变体）、L1（24）、L2（32）、L3（16），共 80 个全新生成任务。③双重评估：Postman/Newman 行为测试套件（32 请求覆盖全部端点、291 条断言，含属性存在、类型、状态码、状态迁移四类断言）+ 三个静态验证器（架构层存在性与向上 import 检查、数据库引擎正则证据、ORM 使用证据）；任务成功 = 行为测试全通过 ∧ 所有适用验证器通过。④智能体：Mini-SWE-Agent（重写为生成式工作流）与 OpenHands（加入 re-prompt 机制防止提前终止），配 4 档模型（Devstral-Small、Qwen3-Coder-Next、Qwen3-235B-A22B、MiniMax-M2.5、Kimi-K2.5、GPT-5-mini/5.2/5.4）；每配置每任务 n=3 次独立运行；Docker build/evaluate 两阶段隔离流水线（干净容器应用 patch 后执行测试），约 37 亿输入 token。⑤指标：A%（主指标，反映部分进展）与 pass@1；另以匹配对设计计算各约束的边际效应 Δ(A%)。⑥根因分析：用 GPT-5.2（temperature 0）作为裁判，依据最后 20 轮轨迹、测试结果、服务端日志与验证器输出做两级失败分类，在 50 例分层抽样上与人工标注对齐（Cohen’s κ=0.975）。⑦补充设置：20 个「功能实现」任务——从 5 个真实 RealWorld 仓库中按 feature 垂直切片做 git diff 消融（161–2,604 行、2–46 文件），使约束隐式存在于代码库中，检验约束衰减是否为合成设置的人为产物。⑧成本控制：3 个模型-智能体组合仅在 16 任务子集上评测，并以 8 个全量配置做代表性验证（Pearson r=0.948、Spearman ρ=0.932，N=32，MAE 6.2–10.9 pp）。全部流水线和数据集开源。

Result: RQ1（约束衰减）：九个强配置（L0 A%>50%）从 L0 到 L3 的 A% 平均下降 27.48 pp（摘要为 27.28/「约 30 点」），相对损失超基线 30%；最差 OpenHands+Qwen3-Coder-Next 掉 45.5 pp（占其 L0 的 62%），最好 OpenHands+MiniMax-M2.5 仅降 17.0 pp。L0 下 Qwen3-Coder-Next、MiniMax-M2.5、GPT-5.4、Kimi-K2.5 均超 85% A%；Devstral-Small 与 Qwen3-235B-A22B 在 L0 即近乎失效，被排除。边际效应：PostgreSQL −20.4±2.5 pp、SQLite −16.1±2.6、Clean Architecture −8.8±1.7、SQLAlchemy −4.8±2.6、Sequelize +1.8±2.2（ORM 约束相对已含数据库的基线计算）。A% 与 pass@1 差距巨大（最强 L3 配置 A%=78.6% 但 pass@1 仅 8.3%），说明跨文件一致性与约束遵从不足。验证器影响可忽略：强制与否差值 ≤2.7 pp，平均衰减仅由 26 变 27 pp，误拒率 ≤0.5%，故结论为保守下界。功能实现任务中仅 GPT-5.2 的 pass@1 超过 50%（Mini-SWE/OpenHands 分别 46.7/38.3；GPT-5-mini 15.0/48.3），说明衰减并非「从零建仓」的伪影。RQ2（框架敏感性）：四个中型配置的最好-最差差距 43–69 pp，排序稳定——Express 73.6%、Koa 58.7%、Flask 57.2% 居前三（Express/Koa/Flask 平均 69.2/69.8/63.6%），Django 39.1%、FastAPI 37.7%、Hono 35.4% 垫底；轻量框架比约定繁重框架高 26–45 pp。原因是 Django 的约定驱动结构与自动发现、FastAPI 的类型提示驱动校验；Hono 因面向边缘运行时、需 Node 兼容适配器而落后。强模型打破该模式：MiniMax-M2.5 与 GPT-5.4 的框架差距降至 16/21 pp，排序不再成立。RQ3（根因）：逻辑错误占失败的 62.1–76.6%（GPT-5-mini 例外，其 50.5% 为服务器启动失败）；逻辑错误内数据层缺陷（查询逻辑错误 + DB/ORM 运行时错误）占 36.5–60.9%，平均 48.4%，为所有模型的首要根因；框架特异性问题居次（9.5–25.4%），且 70 例中 56 例为 Fastify 拒绝空 body 的 favorite/follow POST；鉴权配置错误随模型能力单调下降（22.6→15.9→7.2→1.6%）。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 智能体擅长无约束生成，却难以在显式架构规则与数据层约定下保持功能合规，可靠适用于快速原型而不可靠用于生产级后端；联合满足功能与结构要求仍是开放问题。作者建议智能体开发者走出纯功能基准，转向引入结构感知能力，例如检索增强的框架文档、面向约束的规划、以及在约定繁重代码库上的定向预训练。方法学局限包括：静态验证器只做模式匹配（可能漏检结构违规、也可能放过运行时违规）；用同一份 Conduit 规格提升内部效度但牺牲生态多样性；部分模型仅在 16 任务子集上评测（已做相关性验证）；失败分类依赖 LLM 裁判（虽达 κ=0.975，但样本量仅 50）；各约束水平间样本方差较大，效应量需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents demonstrate strong performance in autonomous code generation under loose specifications. However, production-grade software requires strict adherence to structural constraints, such as architectural patterns, databases, and object-relational mappings. Existing benchmarks often overlook these non-functional requirements, rewarding functionally correct but structurally arbitrary solutions. We present a systematic study evaluating how well agents handle structural constraints in multi-file backend generation. By fixing a unified API contract across 80 greenfield generation tasks and 20 feature-implementation tasks spanning eight web frameworks, we isolate the effect of structural complexity using a dual evaluation with end-to-end behavioral tests and static verifiers. Our findings reveal a phenomenon of constraint decay: as structural requirements accumulate, agent performance exhibits a substantial decline. Capable configurations lose 30 points on average in assertion pass rates from baseline to fully specified tasks, while some weaker configurations approach zero. Framework sensitivity analysis exposes significant performance disparities: agents succeed in minimal, explicit frameworks (e.g., Flask) but perform substantially worse on average in convention-heavy environments (e.g., FastAPI, Django). Finally, error analysis identifies data-layer defects (e.g., incorrect query composition and ORM runtime violations) as the leading root causes. This work highlights that jointly satisfying functional and structural requirements remains a key open challenge for coding agents.

</details>


### [174] [To What Extent Does Agent-generated Code Require Maintenance? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06464)
*Shota Sawada,Tatsuya Shirai,Yutaro Kashiwa,Ken'ichi Yamaguchi,Hiroshi Iwata,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于 AIDev 数据集与 GitHub 历史，实证考察了自主编码代理（Copilot、Claude、Devin、Cursor）生成的代码在合并入库后的长期维护情况。分析 100 个仓库中各 508 个 AI 生成与 508 个人工编写文件、共 3,238 次提交后发现：AI 生成文件获得的维护频次更低、每次变更幅度仅占文件很小比例，其后续修改以功能扩展（feat，21.78%）为主，而人工文件以缺陷修复（fix，16.76%）和文档更新为主；且无论文件来源如何，AI 代理仅承担约 17% 的维护提交，人类开发者完成了 AI 文件约 83% 的维护工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的自主编码代理显著提升了开发速度，但已有研究多聚焦于生成时刻或采用 AI 工具前后的短期质量影响（代码异味、复杂度、安全漏洞、技术债、PR 审查负担等），而代码合并入库之后的长期维护需求——即人类究竟需要投入多少手工维护、修改属于何种类型——几乎缺乏实证数据。若 AI 生成代码需要大量维护，其带来的生产力收益可能被长期维护成本抵消；同时人类开发者还需阅读和修改自己未编写的代码，带来额外认知负担。

Method: 数据来源为 Li 等人发布的 AIDev 数据集（456,000+ PR、约 61,000 仓库，覆盖 2024-12 至 2025-07），从中筛选出 2,807 个 star>100 的仓库并抽取 100 个以控制 API 与计算成本；通过 PR 文件状态识别“新增”文件，再以 committer 名称匹配代理 bot 账号（claude[bot]、Cursor Agent、Copilot、devin-ai-integration）判定文件作者归属，Codex 因不作为 commit owner 而被排除。每仓库随机抽取至多 10 个 AI 文件与 10 个人工文件（各 508 个），采集截至 2026-01-31 的全部提交，排除初始创建提交后得到 AI 文件 1,543 次、人工文件 1,695 次维护提交，保证所有文件至少有 6 个月观察期。RQ1 按创建时间后的逐月区间统计提交数（频率）与变更行数占文件规模比例（幅度），以小提琴图对比；RQ2 采用 Conventional Commits Classification System（CCS，10 类，精度 77.95%）自动分类提交类型；RQ3 以 committer 名称匹配区分 AI 代理与人类提交者，统计维护主体占比。

Result: RQ1：AI 生成文件首月提交数约为人工文件的一半，随后三个月维护活动递减；第四个月起两者差距收窄但持续非零，说明长期仍需维护。幅度上，人工文件变更行数占比更高，反映更显著的结构性改动与主动重构；AI 文件变更比例小且随时间下降，多为微小修改。RQ2：AI 文件提交类型分布为 feat 21.78%（最高）、refactor 14.19%、chore 13.35%、fix 11.73%、docs 10.76%；人工文件以 fix 16.76%、docs 16.22%、refactor/feat 各 15.10% 领先，fix 与 docs 的占比差分别为 −5.03 与 −5.46 个百分点；test、ci、style、perf 等低频类别差异不明显。RQ3：AI 文件的维护提交中 83.21%（1,284 次）由人类完成、仅 16.79%（259 次）由代理完成；人工文件则为 92.98% 人类、7.02% 代理，说明代理更愿维护自己生成的文件但仍非主力。

Conclusion: AI 生成代码并未立即带来沉重维护负担，其后续维护强度低于人工代码，但作者同时提醒低维护频次存在替代解释（开发者可能因难以理解而回避修改、部分生成文件可能未在生产中被实际使用），不能直接等同于高质量；更值得关注的是 AI 代码普遍存在需求覆盖与通用性不足，导致维护形态由“纠错”转向“功能生长”，且这一扩张几乎完全由人类承担（83%）。对实践者的建议是建立覆盖合并后演化的评审与追踪流程，而非将初始生成视为成品；对研究者的建议是发展面向维护（需求跟踪、复杂重构、功能演进）的专用代理，并探索以圈复杂度、耦合度、文件规模等特征预测维护需求。作者指出的局限包括：仅 100 仓库 508 个 AI 文件、仅 6 个月观察窗（因工具较新）、排除了 Codex、未控制功能复杂度与文件重要性、无法识别未声明的 AI 辅助代码、样本期偏早期采用者以功能开发为主。未来工作包括延长观察期、跨代理对比、维护风险预测模型与维护型代理研发。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous coding agents have reshaped software development. While these agents excel at code generation, open questions persist about the long-term maintainability of AI-generated code. This study empirically investigates the maintenance extent, human involvement, and modification types of AI-generated files versus human-authored code. Using the AIDev dataset of AI-generated pull requests and GitHub, we analyzed over 1,000 files and approximately 3,200 changes from 100 popular repositories. Our findings show that: (i) AI-generated files receive less frequent maintenance than human-authored code, with updates affecting only a small fraction of file size; (ii) the most frequent modifications to AI code are feature extensions, whereas human updates focus on bug fixes, and (iii) human developers perform the large majority of this maintenance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Quantizing With Randomized Hadamard Transforms: Efficient Heuristic Now Proven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06014)
*Ran Ben-Basat,William Kuszmaul,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了随机Hadamard变换替代均匀随机旋转的理论保证。证明了两个RHT可渐近匹配URR用于现代压缩方案（DRIVE、QUIC-FL），三个RHT可满足向量量化的块相关性要求，并提出运行时自适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: URR在量化压缩等场景中常用但计算成本高，RHT虽可高效实现但单次RHT对最坏情况输入表现不佳，需理论保证替代方案的可行性。

Method: 理论分析证明两个RHT后坐标边际分布与高斯分布的Kolmogorov距离和1-Wasserstein距离均为O(d^{-1/2})；三个RHT композиции证明坐标协方差衰减；提出运行时矩检验自适应算法。

Result: 两个RHT在渐近意义上与URR性能相当（适用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL）；三个RHT确保VQ中固定大小坐标块的弱相关性要求；动态调整策略可提升实际应用性能。

Conclusion: 两个RHT足以在渐近意义上替代URR用于DRIVE和QUIC-FL压缩方案；三个RHT可确保VQ代码簿的性能与URR相当；实践中可通过O(d)时间复杂度的矩检验动态调整RHT数量以平衡性能与效率。

Abstract: Uniform random rotations (URRs) are a common preprocessing step in modern quantization approaches used for gradient compression, inference acceleration, KV-cache compression, model weight quantization, and approximate nearest-neighbor search in vector databases. In practice, URRs are often replaced by randomized Hadamard transforms (RHTs), which preserve orthogonality while admitting fast implementations. The remaining issue is the performance for worst-case inputs. With a URR, each coordinate is individually distributed as a shifted beta distribution, which converges to a Gaussian distribution in high dimensions. Generally, one RHT is not suitable in the worst case, as individual coordinates can be far from these distributions. We show that after composing two RHTs on any $d$-sized input vector, the marginal distribution of every fixed coordinate of the normalized rotated vector is within $O(d^{-1/2})$ of a standard Gaussian both in Kolmogorov distance and in $1$-Wasserstein distance. We then plug these bounds into the analyses of modern compression schemes, namely DRIVE and QUIC-FL, and show that two RHTs achieve performance that asymptotically matches URRs.
  However, we show that two RHTs may not be sufficient for Vector Quantization (VQ), which often requires weak correlation across fixed-size blocks of coordinates (as opposed to only marginal distribution convergence for single coordinates). We prove that a composition of three RHTs leads to decaying coordinate covariance. This ensures that any fixed, bounded, multi-dimensional VQ codebook optimized for URRs has the same expected error when using three RHTs, up to an additive term that vanishes with the dimension.
  Finally, because practical inputs are rarely adversarial, we propose a linear-time ${O}(d)$ check on the input's moments to dynamically adapt the number of RHTs used at runtime to improve performance.

</details>


### [176] [Contrastive Identification and Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06211)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将极限识别与生成框架从全标注/正例数据扩展到对比数据（无序对 $\{x,y\}$ 满足 $h(x)\neq h(y)$ 但正例标签未知），引入通用交叉图作为统一技术工具，建立了可识别性精确刻画、对比闭包维度、生成-文本识别层次关系以及对有限对抗腐败的尖锐逆转等核心结果。拓展极限识别框架至对比关系数据；通用交叉图统一编码歧义、生成障碍与腐败；精确刻画可识别性条件与生成复杂度；揭示与经典模型不可比较的层次关系和对噪声的尖锐逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gold [1967]的经典极限识别模型和Kleinberg等 [2024]的极限生成模型均只处理正例或全标注数据，而现实中许多监督信号本质上是关系性的而非单例的，编码的是样本间的关系而非单一样本的标签，现有模型无法处理此类对比性数据。需建立对比极限识别与生成的统一理论。动机: Gold [1967] 的经典识别模型和Kleinberg & Mullainathan [2024] 的生成模型都局限于正例或全标注数据，而现实中的监督信号往往是关系性的（编码样本间关系而非单一样本标签），现有框架无法处理。

Method: 引入通用交叉图（common crossing graph）作为统一技术对象，将成对歧义、家族级生成障碍和腐败缺陷统一编码为覆盖-关联语言；给出了Angluin的tell-tale条件的单行几何细化；定义了对比闭包维度（contrastive closure dimension）。

Result: （1）精确刻画了可对比识别的类别条件（Angluin [1980] tell-tale条件的几何细化）；（2）定义了对比闭包维度并精确刻画了均匀对比生成的样本复杂度上界；（3）证明对比生成与文本识别相互不可比较；（4）证明了对有限对抗腐败的尖锐逆转：存在类别在对任何有限腐败预算下都可由单一预算无关算法从对比对中识别，但即使仅有一个腐败观测也无法从正例中识别。

Conclusion: 建立了对比学习识别与生成的完整理论框架，证明了该模型与经典正例识别和文本识别的严格层次关系，并揭示了对有限对抗扰动的尖锐逆转现象，为关系监督信号的学习理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the classical identification in the limit model of Gold [1967], a stream of positive examples is presented round by round, and the learner must eventually recover the target hypothesis. Recently, Kleinberg and Mullainathan [2024] introduced generation in the limit, where the learner instead must eventually output novel elements of the target's support. Both lines of work focus on positive-only or fully labeled data. Yet many natural supervision signals are inherently relational rather than singleton, which encode relationships between examples rather than labels of individual ones. We initiate the study of contrastive identification and generation in the limit, where the learner observes a contrastive presentation of data: a stream of unordered pairs $\{x,y\}$ satisfying $h(x)\ne h(y)$ for an unknown target binary hypothesis $h$, but which element is positive is hidden from the learner. We first present three results in the noiseless setting: an exact characterization of contrastive identifiable classes (a one-line geometric refinement of Angluin [1980]'s tell-tale condition), a combinatorial dimension called contrastive closure dimension (a contrasitive analogue of the closure dimension in Raman et al. [2025]) and exactly characterizing uniform contrastive generation with tight sample complexity, and a strict hierarchy in which contrastive generation and text identification are mutually incomparable. We then prove a sharp reversal under finite adversarial corruption: there exist classes identifiable from contrastive pairs under any finite corruption budget by a single budget-independent algorithm, yet not identifiable from positive examples under even one corrupted observation. The unifying technical object is the common crossing graph, which encodes pairwise ambiguity, family-level generation obstructions, and corruption defects in a single coverage-and-incidence language.

</details>


### [177] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种用于训练递归智能体的强化学习方法，使智能体能够在推理时通过分而治之策略实现递归扩展。实验表明，该方法可提升训练效率、突破上下文窗口限制泛化至更难任务，并减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统难以有效扩展到更长上下文和更复杂问题，缺乏一种自然利用分而治之策略进行递归推理的方法。需要训练模型学会何时以及如何委托子任务和进行通信。

Method: 提出递归智能体优化（RAO），一种强化学习方法，用于训练能够递归生成和委托子任务的递归智能体。该方法教智能体在推理时如何最优地利用递归推理，实现自然扩展。

Result: 训练的递归智能体在训练效率上更优，可扩展至超出模型上下文窗口的任务，能泛化至比训练任务难得多的任务，且相比单智能体系统可减少实际运行时间。

Conclusion: 递归智能体结合RAO训练方法能够实现更好的训练效率、更强的可扩展性和泛化能力，为构建高效、可扩展的智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [178] [LLMSpace: Carbon Footprint Modeling for Large Language Model Inference on LEO Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05615)
*Lei Jiang,Adrian Ildefonso,Daniel Loveless,Fan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个太空LLM推理碳建模框架LLMSpace，填补了AI卫星全生命周期碳足迹评估的空白，揭示了运营与嵌入碳排放的多维权衡，为太空AI系统的绿色设计提供系统性分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）推理产生快速增长的能源需求，导致能源和碳排放危机；虽然太阳能驱动的AI近地轨道（LEO）卫星被提出以减少地面电力消耗，但其全生命周期碳足迹由于发射排放、卫星制造和辐射加固硬件需求而未被充分理解。

Method: 提出了LLMSpace碳建模框架，首次联合建模运营碳排放和嵌入碳排放，整合外围子系统、辐射加固加速器和存储器特性，以及LLM特定工作负载特征（如prefill-decode行为和token生成），使用真实的卫星和GPU配置进行评估。

Result: LLMSpace揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计和运营寿命之间的关键权衡，为太空LLM推理的可持续性设计提供指导。

Conclusion: LLMSpace为可持续太空LLM推理提供了系统性的碳评估框架，揭示了碳足迹、推理延迟、硬件设计与运行寿命之间的关键权衡，为未来太空AI系统的绿色设计提供了重要参考。源代码已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) impose rapidly growing energy demands, creating an emerging energy and carbon crisis driven by large-scale inference. Solar-powered, AI-enabled low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites have been proposed to mitigate terrestrial electricity consumption, but their lifecycle carbon footprint remains poorly understood due to launch emissions, satellite manufacturing, and radiation-hardened hardware requirements. This paper presents \textit{LLMSpace}, the first carbon modeling framework for LLM inference on AI-enabled LEO satellites. LLMSpace jointly models operational and embodied carbon, peripheral subsystems, radiation-hardened accelerators and memories, and LLM-specific workload characteristics such as prefill-decode behavior and token generation. Using realistic satellite and GPU configurations, LLMSpace reveals key trade-offs among carbon footprint, inference latency, hardware design, and operational lifetime for sustainable space-based LLM inference. Source code: https://github.com/UnchartedRLab/LLMSpace.

</details>


### [179] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenG2G 是一个开源的仿真平台，用于研究"AI 数据中心—电网运行时协同"问题：它把由生产级 AI 服务实测数据驱动的数据中心后端、基于 OpenDSS 的电网后端，以及一个通用的多速率控制器接口组合成一个闭环仿真回路。实验表明该平台可以公平对比 droop、在线反馈优化（OFO）、PPO 三类控制器，并量化 AI 模型选择（规模、架构、精度）与部署选择（并行度、GPU 型号）如何通过"可行功率区间"这一指标决定协调空间的大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 算力需求激增，单个现代 AI 数据中心用电可达数十 MW，规划中的项目达 GW 级（1 GW 约为旧金山平均用电的 1.7 倍），给配电网带来容量与可靠性压力，导致并网审批延迟数年，反过来制约 AI 产能扩张。为此数据中心开始承诺"电力灵活性"：电网紧张时降低负荷（欠压时减小 batch），电网富余时提高负荷（过压时增大 batch）以吸收盈余。要把这种灵活性落地，需要一个跨越数据中心与电网两侧的运行时闭环控制问题，而现有研究割裂：数据中心仿真器对电网无感知，电网研究把数据中心当作简单负荷或忽略其工作负载真实性，缺少统一的仿真框架来比较不同协调策略，也无法回答"AI 模型/部署选择如何决定数据中心的可调功率空间"。

Method: 平台架构由三个可插拔组件围绕一个通用多速率仿真循环构成：(1) 数据中心后端——默认回放 ML.ENERGY Benchmark v3 的实测推理数据（按 batch size 给出功率、吞吐、inter-token latency 分布），可叠加来自真实大模型训练实测的功率曲线作为训练负载；(2) 电网后端——封装 OpenDSS，支持 IEEE 标准测试馈线、PV/时变负荷/储能/有载调压器，输出各母线相电压与越限指标；(3) 控制器接口——抽象基类，读取两侧状态并发出类型化的命令（SetBatchSize、SetTaps 等），支持集中式（每电网一个）或分布式（每数据中心一个）控制；仿真循环以 0.1 s 为基准步长按各组件原生速率推进，集中处理日志、随机种子与事件记账以保证可复现；扩展仅需继承 Python 子类，且用泛型参数化（Controller[DCBackendT, GridBackendT]）做类型安全组合。实验部分：§4 在 IEEE 13/34/123 母线馈线（1/2/4 个数据中心）上，以推理 batch size 为控制旋钮，对比 droop（局部比例控制）、OFO（基于对偶变量与在线有限差分估计注入灵敏度的原始-对偶反馈优化）、PPO（模型无关的演员-评论家，观测含电压与每 LLM 状态，动作空间为每模型 {减/持/增} 三元分类，采用 delta 参数化把联合动作空间从 ∏|B_i| 降到 3^N），基线为固定 batch=128 的 No Coordination；指标为积分电压越限（pu·s）、token 吞吐、时延越限率、batch 切换次数。§5 在固定峰值功率（3 MW 匹配锚点）下改变模型规模/架构/精度与并行度、GPU 型号，测量"可行功率区间"（满足时延目标的可行配置下最大与最小功率之差）并对比协调前后的越限积分。

Result: 控制器对比：三种控制器均显著优于 No Coordination（IEEE-13：19.52 → droop 1.39 / PPO 1.62 / OFO 1.04 pu·s）；随着馈线规模与多数据中心复杂度上升，差距显著拉开（IEEE-123：35.15 → droop 29.70 / PPO 10.64 / OFO 5.74 pu·s），OFO 因信息最丰富而始终最强，并在吞吐—越限平面上保持 Pareto 最优；PPO 在 13/34 母线上以更大越限换取更高吞吐；droop 行为保守、吞吐与调节能力均处于中间或较差水平。OFO 的时延越限率稳定在约 2%，PPO 为 3.0%–5.5%。参数敏感性显示 OFO 的吞吐权重 α_T 从 1e-2 降到 1e-4 使积分越限下降 21 倍但吞吐下降 44%；PPO 的电压权重 w_V=5k 最佳（2.46→1.28 pu·s），过大则泛化变差，且最佳 checkpoint 常出现在约 1.5M 步而非训练末尾。AI 选择的影响：可行功率区间决定协调余量——Qwen3-8B 在 B200 上区间最大，OFO 可将越限压到 1 pu·s 以下；Qwen3-32B 因时延目标（50 ms 只能到 batch 128）压低上限；MoE（30B A3B）与 235B A22B 因小 batch 下 GPU 利用率仍高而抬高功率下限；FP8 在小 batch 提升算术强度抬升下限（BF16 越限下降约 100 倍，FP8 仅约 2 倍），而 BF16 的可行区间宽 2.2–2.8 倍；提高专家并行度可降低功率下限（GPT-OSS 120B 显著，Qwen 235B A22B 几乎无效），新 GPU 既可能降低下限（B200 算力余量更大，8B）也可能抬高上限（B200 192 GB 显存突破 H100 80 GB 的 KV cache 瓶颈，32B）。

Conclusion: OpenG2G 用单一 Python 接口把 AI 数据中心—电网运行时协同变成可研究的问题，桥接机器学习、系统与电力系统社区。它表明 AI 模型选择与部署选择都会投影为一个可度量的"可行功率区间"及相应的电压调节余量，因此可行的数据中心配置空间与其所接入的电网密不可分。结论还指出：控制器在吞吐—越限平面上的落点取决于其可获得的信息量与目标权重，而权重可依据电网运营商对灵活性的经济补偿协议来调节。局限包括：仿真结论依赖基准数据与电网仿真器的保真度；OpenDSS 只刻画准稳态电压、瞬态现象未覆盖；训练负载仅作为聚合功率叠加而非可控负载；仅验证配电馈线；PPO 需逐馈线训练、未研究跨馈线泛化。平台已开源（Apache-2.0，github.com/gpu2grid/openg2g），全部实验仅需 CPU 可复现。

Abstract: AI's growing compute demand and new datacenter buildouts present major capacity and reliability challenges for the electricity grid, leading to multi-year interconnection delays for new datacenters and bottlenecking AI growth. To ease this strain, datacenters increasingly offer rapid power flexibility in response to grid signals, where the datacenter can increase or decrease its power consumption by adapting its workload in real time.
  In order to understand the impact of large datacenters on the grid and to facilitate the design of effective coordination strategies, we build OpenG2G, a simulation platform for AI datacenter-grid runtime coordination. We show that OpenG2G is capable of answering a wide range of coordination questions by allowing users to implement and compare various control paradigms (including classic, optimization, and learning-based controllers), and quantify how AI model and deployment choices affect datacenter flexibility and coordination outcomes. This versatility is enabled by OpenG2G's modular and extensible architecture: a datacenter backend driven by real measurements of production-grade AI services, a grid backend built on high-fidelity grid simulators, and a generic controller interface that closes the loop between them. We describe the design of OpenG2G and demonstrate its usefulness through realistic grid scenarios and AI workloads.

</details>


### [180] [CLAD: A Clustered Label-Agnostic Federated Learning Framework for Joint Anomaly Detection and Attack Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06571)
*Iason Ofeidis,Nikos Papadis,Randeep Bhatia,Leandros Tassiulas,TV Lakshman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes CLAD, a federated learning framework for IoT intrusion detection that combines Clustered Federated Learning with a dual-mode architecture to simultaneously address device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The framework achieves 30% better detection performance with 80% unlabeled data and reduces communication costs by 50%.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid expansion of IoT and IIoT creates a massive and heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. Standard Federated Learning approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments, leading to two primary bottlenecks: device heterogeneity and label scarcity.

Method: The CLAD framework combines Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture (DM²A). The DM²A component features a shared encoder with two branches: one for unsupervised anomaly detection and one for supervised attack classification, enabling simultaneous utilization of labeled and unlabeled data. The clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns to prevent global model divergence.

Result: CLAD achieves a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, while only requiring half the communication cost compared to baseline methods.

Conclusion: CLAD framework effectively addresses both device heterogeneity and label scarcity challenges in IoT security through its integrated CFL and DM²A architecture. The approach ensures no data is discarded while preserving distinct operational patterns, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines significantly.

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and Industrial IoT (IIoT) has created a massive, heterogeneous attack surface that challenges traditional network security mechanisms. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative to centralized Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS), standard approaches struggle to generalize across diverse device behaviors and typically fail to utilize the vast amounts of unlabeled data present in realistic edge environments. To bridge these gaps, we propose CLAD, a holistic framework that seamlessly incorporates Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) with a novel Dual-Mode Micro-Architecture ($\text{DM}^2\text{A}$). This unified approach simultaneously tackles the two primary bottlenecks of IoT security: device heterogeneity and label scarcity. The $\text{DM}^2\text{A}$ component features a shared encoder followed by two branches, enabling joint unsupervised anomaly detection and supervised attack classification; this allows the framework to harvest intelligence from both labeled and unlabeled clients. Concurrently, the clustering component dynamically groups devices with congruent traffic patterns, preventing global model divergence. By carefully combining these elements, CLAD ensures that no data is discarded and distinct operational patterns are preserved. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this integrated approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 30% relative improvement in detection performance in scenarios with 80% unlabeled clients, with only half the communication cost.

</details>


### [181] [Data-Driven Variational Basis Learning Beyond Neural Networks: A Non-Neural Framework for Adaptive Basis Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05221)
*Andrew Kiruluta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据驱动变分基学习"(DVBL)：把基函数原子（Φ）当作主要优化变量，与样本系数α及可选的线性演化算子A一起通过变分优化联合学习，从而得到显式、可解释、可严格分析的数据自适应基展开。论文给出目标函数、矩阵形式、最优解存在性证明、交替最小化的单调下降与临界点收敛证明、稀疏生成模型下的系数唯一性与基可辨识性条件、流形（图拉普拉斯）正则化、动力学扩展（Koopman式线性潜变量演化，闭式解），并指出无正则时该框架退化为低秩近似（Eckart–Young–Mirsky）。后半部分将其推广为"基状态语言模型"(BSLM)，一个完全非神经的自回归语言模型架构（词表基+隐状态算子演化+线性/切换读出），并给出伪代码、评测指标、对比表与实验方案蓝图。作者明确声明当前无实证结果（表格为示意性/推测性数据），定位为研究议程而非已验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典表示系统（傅里叶、小波、样条、固定字典）虽有闭式结构、正交性与逼近理论，但基是先验固定的，无法适配现代高维数据的统计规律、各向异性、非线性流形与任务相关结构。神经网络虽能从数据学习特征，但其分层非线性参数化带来三重代价：表示隐式不可解释、优化高度非凸且组合性强导致理论分析困难、难以直接施加稀疏/对称/光滑/守恒等先验约束。核心问题即：能否不使用神经网络而直接从数据中学习显式的基函数系统？作者指出虽有大量经验结果，但"学习自适应基"作为统计上是良定问题的理论仍不完整；同时希望把稀疏编码、流形正则、算子动力学、任务自适应基约束等分散文献统一到一个"变分基学习"视角下。

Method: 设观测 X=[x_1..x_N]∈R^{d×N}，基 Φ∈R^{d×m}，系数 A∈R^{m×N}，求 min ‖X−ΦA‖_F² + λℛ(A) + μΩ(Φ)，并施加单位范数 ‖φ_k‖₂=1 与可选相干性约束 |φ_k^Tφ_l|≤δ。ℛ 可为 ℓ₁/ℓ₂/组稀疏等系数正则，Ω 可为 Frobenius 能量、梯度光滑性 ‖ΦᵀΦ−I‖² 等基正则。矩阵形式化为块坐标下降：系数步（固定Φ，A步凸，若ΦᵀΦ正定则解唯一）、基步（固定A，Ω凸且可行集凸时凸）、原子归一化，用相对目标下降作为停止准则。存在性：ℛ 强制 coercive、Ω 下半连续、C_Φ 非空紧 → 变分法直接法给出全局极小点存在；单位范数约束消除 (Φ,A)→(cΦ,c⁻¹A) 尺度退化。Theorem 2/3：精确块更新下 J^(t) 单调不增且收敛；若迭代在紧集内、块子问题精确解、目标满足 Kurdyka–Łojasiewicz 性质，则所有聚点为临界点（分块坐标下降理论）。Theorem 4：互相干 μ(Φ) 下 ‖α‖₀ < ½(1+1/μ) 时表示唯一最稀疏；Theorem 5（非正式）：稀疏生成模型 x_i=Φ*α*+ε、支撑丰富、噪声有界且 Φ* 满足非相干/非退化时，全局极小点与 Φ* 仅差列的带符号置换。流形正则：加入 η Tr(ALAᵀ)，并证明其等于 ½Σ_{i,j}W_ij‖α_i−α_j‖²，惩罚图上邻近样本系数粗糙度。动力学扩展：x_t≈Φz_t, z_{t+1}≈Az_t，Ψ(A)=‖A‖² 时算子有闭式解 A*=Z₊Z₋ᵀ(Z₋Z₋ᵀ+νI)⁻¹，ν>0 时强凸解唯一。Proposition 5：无正则时最优重构误差等于 X 的最优 rank-m 近似误差 Σ_{j>m}σ_j(X)²，说明框架真正价值在正则化与结构约束。语言模型部分：BSLM 用词表基 Φ∈R^{V×m} 编码 one-hot 词元得稀疏码 a_t，隐状态 z_{t+1}=Az_t+Ba_t+ξ_t（或切换算子 A_{σ_t}、低秩修正 U_t c_t），读出 ℓ_t=Cz_t+d 或 ΦMz_t+d 经 softmax 得下一词分布；训练目标为 NLL + λ_aΣR_a + λ_zΣR_z + μΩ(Φ) + βΣ‖z_{t+1}−Az_t−Ba_t‖²，交替进行词元码推断、状态推断、基与算子更新，采用 token block 训练；长上下文用记忆库、多尺度状态或变分检索替代注意力。

Result: 理论结果：(1) 变分问题在温和假设下良定、至少存在一个全局极小点（Theorem 1）；(2) 系数子问题凸（严格凸或ΦᵀΦ正定时解唯一）、基子问题在二次正则+凸可行集下凸（Prop 1-2），但单位范数约束非凸故整体非凸；(3) 精确交替块更新保证目标单调下降并收敛，KL 条件下聚点为临界点（Thm 2-3, Cor 1）；(4) 相干性给出稀疏系数唯一性阈值，稀疏生成模型+支撑丰富性给出基的带符号置换可辨识性（Thm 4-5）；(5) 图正则项有明确几何解释，动力学算子有闭式解（Prop 3-4）；(6) 无正则情形退化为低秩逼近（Prop 5）。定性对照表（表1）给出 Transformer 与 BSLM 在表示、训练、可解释性、长上下文、硬件成熟度、基准表现、可控性上的差异；表2/表3 给出 WikiText-2/103、OpenWebText 子集上的"证明性概念"结果：BSLM 完整模型 Test PPL 34.0 对小 Transformer 基线 30.6，但在延迟（5.9 vs 7.4 ms/token）、内存（4.1 vs 5.8 GB）与可解释性评分（8.7 vs 2.1）上占优；消融显示去掉基正则（37.5）、稠密码（36.2）、仅固定线性算子（38.0）、去多尺度状态（36.7）均退化；缩放显示随基维度 m 与语料规模增大 PPL 单调下降（OpenWebText/2048 达 28.3）。作者明确限定这些表格为蓝图式/示意性结果，并承认无神经基态模型短期内不望在困惑度上超越大规模 Transformer。

Conclusion: 本文主张"从数据学习表示"不必等同于神经网络：可通过显式的变分问题直接学习基展开本身，得到兼具适应性（类特征学习）与表示透明性（类傅里叶/小波）的中间路线。核心贡献在于把显式基学习、稀疏与结构化系数推断、流形感知正则、算子型潜在动力学、任务自适应基约束统一进一个可严格分析的框架，并配套给出存在性、下降性、可辨识性等理论。语言建模被重构为"自适应基与算子推断"问题，提出 BSLM 作为非神经架构类别，其近期价值不在基准性能夺冠，而在可解释性、可控性、物理/符号先验整合、紧凑状态空间与稳定性推理。局限：联合非凸仅保证临界点、初始化敏感；辨识性依赖稀疏性与非相干假设；单一基展开的表达力弱于深层组合非线性；长上下文与状态推断计算成本、硬件不友好仍是主要瓶颈。开放问题包括多层/层次化 DVBL 是否能在不沦为神经网络的前提下恢复组合优势、领域专用基正则设计、流形与动力学约束下的锐恢复率、与压缩感知中测量设计的统一，以及"非神经算子表达力的极限在何处等同于神经计算"这一核心研究挑战。

Abstract: Classical representation systems such as Fourier series, wavelets, and fixed dictionaries provide analytically tractable basis expansions, but they are not intrinsically adapted to the empirical structure of modern high-dimensional data. Neural networks overcome this limitation by learning features from data, yet they do so through layered nonlinear parameterizations that often sacrifice interpretability, explicit control over basis structure, and mathematical transparency. In this manuscript we develop a non-neural alternative that learns basis functions directly from data through variational optimization. The proposed framework, termed Data Driven Variational Basis Learning (DVBL), treats basis atoms as primary optimization variables and learns them jointly with sample-specific coefficients and, when appropriate, a latent linear evolution operator. This yields a data-adaptive basis expansion that remains explicit, interpretable, and amenable to rigorous analysis. We formulate the model, establish existence of minimizers, prove blockwise descent properties for an alternating minimization algorithm, give conditions for coefficient recovery and basis identifiability, and show how manifold and dynamical regularization can be integrated without invoking neural architectures. We also discuss the conceptual novelty of the framework relative to classical dictionary learning, spectral methods, Koopman operator methods, and deep representation learning.

</details>


### [182] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将强化学习推理重新定义为"结果监督内化为过程监督"的问题，提出通过识别、纠正和重用失败轨迹来自动提取过程级信号的方法，实现仅在结果级监督下的细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习推理面临结果监督信号的稀疏性问题，需要将末端反馈转化为能指导中间推理步骤的细粒度信号

Method: 监督内化方法：通过识别、纠正和重用失败推理轨迹，自动提取过程级学习信号

Result: 在结果级监督下实现更细粒度的策略优化，开辟了内部生成过程监督的新训练范式

Conclusion: 提出监督内化方法，将结果级监督自动转化为过程级学习信号，实现仅在结果监督下的细粒度策略优化

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [183] [A Unified Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Graph Construction Methods and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05476)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于评估GNN在噪声文本衍生图谱上性能以及图构建方法有效性的双用途基准测试框架。该框架基于生物医学领域语料库，包含多种自动构建图谱与专家参考图谱，通过半监督节点分类实现系统化评估，具有标准化、可重复和可扩展的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本自动构建的知识图谱存在噪声、碎片化和语义不一致问题，严重影响GNN在下游任务中的性能。现有方法难以区分性能瓶颈源于学习模型还是图谱本身质量，缺乏系统化的评估手段。

Method: 在生物医学领域从单一语料库构建基准测试，包含两种使用不同抽取方法自动构建的图谱，以及由专家标注的高质量参考图谱作为性能上界。通过半监督节点分类任务实现对图构建方法和GNN鲁棒性的可控比较与系统评估。

Result: 该基准测试可同时评估GNN在噪声图谱上的性能以及不同图构建方法的有效性，支持对构建方法的受控比较和对GNN鲁棒性的系统评估。

Conclusion: 该基准测试提供了一个标准化、可重复且可扩展的评估框架，能够促进新的图抽取方法和学习模型的整合，为评估文本知识图谱质量与GNN鲁棒性提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs automatically constructed from text are increasingly used in real-world applications. However, their inherent noise, fragmentation, and semantic inconsistencies significantly affect the performance of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) on downstream tasks. Assessing their performance and robustness remains difficult, as it is often unclear whether observed results stem from the learning model or from the quality of the constructed graph itself. In this work, we introduce a dual-purpose benchmark designed to jointly evaluate (i) the performance of GNNs on noisy, text-derived graphs and (ii) the effectiveness of graph construction methods on a downstream task. The benchmark is built in the biomedical domain from a single textual corpus and includes two automatically constructed graphs generated using different extraction methods, alongside a high-quality reference graph curated by experts that serves as an upper performance bound. This design enables controlled comparison of construction methods and systematic evaluation of GNN robustness through semi-supervised node classification. We further provide a standardized, reproducible, and extensible evaluation framework, facilitating the integration of new graph extraction methods and learning models.

</details>


### [184] [Multi-Dimensional Behavioral Evaluation of Agentic Stock Prediction Systems Using LLM Judges with Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05739)
*Mohammad Al Ridhawi,Mahtab Haj Ali,Hussein Al Osman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向智能体（agentic）系统的"行为级评估"方法：把智能体的中间决策过程而非仅最终输出作为评估对象，用三款LLM组成评审ensemble，按六个维度给每个五日"情节"打分，再把低分维度通过信用分配机制转化为SAC奖励惩罚项反馈给控制器，形成闭环。案例（AE-NodeFormer+SAC股票预测系统，20只标普500股票）显示：扰动验证中目标维度平均下降1.6–2.4分、非目标维度仅平均变动0.32分，跨模型Krippendorff α达0.74–0.85，复合行为分与20日实际夏普比相关ρ=0.72；三轮闭环微调后，在完全留出的2017–2025测试期1日MAPE由0.61%降至0.54%（相对下降11.5%，p<0.001，Cohen's d=0.31），方向准确率71%→74%，夏普提升18%（95% CI [8.2%, 27.4%]），增益集中于高波动期。结果仅基于离线回测，未涉及实盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统的评测只针对最终输出（MAPE、方向准确率等聚合指标），无法定位"是哪一步中间决策出了问题"，也无法区分"输出正确但决策过程糟糕"与"输出错误但决策合理"。作者主张把过程质量本身作为评估对象：需要解决三个子问题——(1) 如何形式化记录可回溯的中间决策轨迹；(2) 如何用LLM在随机条件下对时序决策序列给出可验证、维度特异的评分；(3) 如何把这些诊断转化为可用的下游学习信号。金融预测领域已有大量聚合精度与统计检验研究，却完全未检验中间决策质量。

Method: 方法由四组件构成：(1) 行为轨迹形式化：每个交易日记录六元组 B_t=<m_t,a_t,r_t,u_t,ŷ_t,p_t>（市场上下文含VIX/BERT情绪；自编码器重构误差e、阈值τ、状态标签ℓ；路由混合权重α与主路径索引；SAC动作(Δτ,Δα)∈[-0.1,0.1]²；预测值；20日滚动MAPE/DA），序列化为带单位与量程的JSON；(2) 情节分组：连续5个交易日构成一个episode（与SAC状态表示中的历史窗口k=5一致；3/5/7/10日pilot显示5日方差与一致性最优），不重叠采样；(3) 六维度评分：架构决策维度（Regime Detection、Routing、Adaptation Responsiveness）与涌现行为维度（Risk Calibration、Strategy Coherence、Error Recovery），1–5整数Likert量表并给出1与5分锚点描述，2–4线性插值（8维变体一致性降至α=0.62，4维变体混淆失效模式，故舍弃）；(4) 三模型ensemble评审：GPT 5.4、Claude 4.6 Opus、Gemini 3.1 Pro，温度0、独立盲评、2000 token上限、结构化CoT四步推理、输出JSON含各维度整数分+基于具体字段的理由+12类失败标签（可覆盖默认动作子空间映射）。验证侧：用机械式扰动确保维度特异性（60基线episode×6种单维度扰动=360，加60基线共420集）；用Spearman相关做预测效度并据此设定奖励权重；闭环侧：以 R'_t = R_t − λΣ_d max(0,θ−s̄_d)·w_d（λ=0.15、θ=3，权重w_d取该维度与20日夏普比的验证期相关）改造SAC奖励，并通过信用分配向量 c_d=[c_τ,c_α]把惩罚定向路由到Δτ/Δα子空间（RD/RC→Δτ；RT→Δα；AD/SC/ER→两者）；每40个交易日为一周期（8个episode、3模型评分），若均值低于θ则用该周期replay buffer微调10 epoch后停用惩罚，形成"评估—纠偏"的周期性干预而非连续奖励流。全部适应动作严格限于2011–2016验证期，2017–2025测试期完全冻结。

Result: (1) 维度特异性：六种扰动均产生显著目标维度下降（-1.6至-2.4，p<0.001配对t检验），非目标维度平均仅-0.32，最大偏离-0.5（矛盾动作，因其本身会传导影响下游），最小-0.2；(2) 跨模型一致性：α_K=0.74（RC）至0.85（RD），RD/RT/SC均>0.80；GPT与Claude kappa 0.71–0.83，与Gemini 0.65–0.79，分歧源于对"响应是否及时""比例性边界"的主观解读而非系统性宽严偏差；(3) 行为随波动率退化：RC与AD从低波动到高波动各降1.3分（RC 3.4→2.1），RT降1.2，ER非单调（高波动3.1>中波动2.8，因错误更多更易展示恢复），说明瓶颈在"对异常条件的响应"而非"检测异常"；(4) 预测效度：复合分与20日夏普ρ=0.72（高于任何单维度），RD与MAPE⁻¹相关0.69、RC与MDD⁻¹相关0.64、SC与收益相关0.57，均p<0.01；(5) 闭环：RC 2.1→3.5、RT 2.6→3.7、AD 2.4→3.6；测试期1日MAPE 0.61%→0.54%，DA 71%→74%（d=0.20），夏普+18%（bootstrap CI [8.2%,27.4%]），5日MAPE -10.8%、20日 -8.6%，高波动episode MAPE -17.3% vs 低波动-7.8%；DM=-7.83（p<0.001，20只股票中18只经Bonferroni后显著），四损失函数稳健（-7.04至-7.83），Giacomini-White Wald=86.41（p<1e-9，高波动优势约为低波动的3倍），Hansen MCS 75%水平仅含cycle3，λ=0消融被99%水平拒绝（T_max=11.62）；(6) 消融：三模型ensemble使每episode分数方差降低29–37%（0.29 vs 0.41–0.46）且夏普最高；定向信用分配优于均匀分配（缺陷维度+1.3 vs +0.9，非缺陷维度+0.1 vs -0.4）；λ扫描呈三段式（≤0.05欠修正、[0.10,0.20]有效、≥0.25不稳定，λ=0.30反而劣于基线）；提示词变体平均绝对分差0.14、ICC=0.91、顺序偏差不显著（p=0.34）。

Conclusion: 结论有三点：(1) 智能体的过程质量可被直接、可靠地评估，前提是"中间决策可结构化记录+维度需领域定制+维度特异性须经工程扰动验证"，从而回答聚合指标无法回答的"究竟哪一步决策不健全"；(2) LLM-as-a-Judge 可从静态输出扩展到随机条件下的时序决策链评价，episode形式化、轨迹序列化与信用分配路由是其新增的关键机制；(3) 定性诊断可经RLHF式桥接转为强化学习信号，但必须定向路由到控制器动作空间，否则均匀惩罚会破坏原本合格的行为（-0.4分）。局限与展望：LLM评审为通用模型、可能漏掉专家级信号，缺少与人类评审的直接对比；存在Goodhart式目标错设风险（周期性干预与λ扫描提供结构性约束）；仅在单一架构、20只股票、8年样本上验证，量级不可外推；商业模型权重不可版本锁定，仅能靠温度0、冻结提示词、三家族ensemble与分数归档缓解；温度0消除了边界案例的不确定性信息。作者提出后续方向：在工业控制/医疗决策/能源需求等领域跨架构验证、将ensemble蒸馏为快速代理模型以支持实时部署、引入多温度采样以刻画评估不确定性并软化边界episode的惩罚权重。

Abstract: Agentic stock prediction systems make sequences of interdependent decisions (regime detection, pathway routing, reinforcement learning control) whose individual quality is hidden by aggregate metrics such as mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) or directional accuracy. We present a behavioral evaluation framework that addresses this gap. Behavioral traces logged at every autonomous decision point are grouped into five-day episodes and scored along six domain-specific dimensions (regime detection, routing, adaptation, risk calibration, strategy coherence, error recovery) by an ensemble of three large language model (LLM) judges (GPT 5.4, Claude 4.6 Opus, Gemini 3.1 Pro). Perturbation-based validation on 420 episodes yields targeted score drops of $-1.6$ to $-2.4$ on intended dimensions versus an average of $-0.32$ on the remaining five, with cross-model agreement up to Krippendorff's $α= 0.85$. The composite behavioral score, used here only for cross-episode reporting, correlates at $ρ= 0.72$ with realized 20-day Sharpe ratio from offline backtesting. Closing the loop, the framework converts deficient per-dimension scores into a credit-assigned penalty term added to the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) reward. Three short fine-tuning cycles, all confined to the validation period, produce on the held-out 2017-2025 test period a one-day MAPE reduction from 0.61% to 0.54% (an 11.5% relative reduction; $p<0.001$, Cohen's $d=0.31$), a directional accuracy increase from 71% to 74%, and an 18% Sharpe ratio improvement (95% bootstrap CI [8.2%, 27.4%]), with gains concentrated in high-volatility episodes where the original system was most behaviorally deficient. Results are from offline backtesting and do not address effects specific to live deployment.

</details>


### [185] [RVPO: Risk-Sensitive Alignment via Variance Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05750)
*Ivan Montero,Tomasz Jurczyk,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RVPO通过惩罚奖励方差解决RLHF中的约束忽视问题，在医学推理和工具调用任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无批评者的RLHF方法采用算术平均聚合多目标奖励，易导致约束忽视：一个目标的高成功可能掩盖其他关键目标的失败（如安全或格式）。

Method: 提出RVPO框架，通过LogSumExp(SoftMin)算子惩罚奖励间方差，将优化目标从"最大化求和"转向"最大化一致性"。

Result: 在HealthBench上，14B模型得分提升至0.261（vs GDPO的0.215，p<0.001）；在GPQA-Diamond上保持竞争性准确率，且未出现其他多奖励方法的晚期性能下降。

Conclusion: RVPO通过方差正则化在不同模型规模下缓解了约束忽视问题，且不牺牲通用能力。

Abstract: Current critic-less RLHF methods aggregate multi-objective rewards via an arithmetic mean, leaving them vulnerable to constraint neglect: high-magnitude success in one objective can numerically offset critical failures in others (e.g., safety or formatting), masking low-performing "bottleneck" rewards vital for reliable multi-objective alignment. We propose Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO), a risk-sensitive framework that penalizes inter-reward variance during advantage aggregation, shifting the objective from "maximize sum" to "maximize consistency." We show via Taylor expansion that a LogSumExp (SoftMin) operator effectively acts as a smooth variance penalty. We evaluate RVPO on rubric-based medical and scientific reasoning with up to 17 concurrent LLM-judged reward signals (Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B) and on tool-calling with rule-based constraints (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B). By preventing the model from neglecting difficult constraints to exploit easier objectives, RVPO improves overall scores on HealthBench (0.261 vs. 0.215 for GDPO at 14B, $p < 0.001$) and maintains competitive accuracy on GPQA-Diamond without the late-stage degradation observed in other multi-reward methods, demonstrating that variance regularization mitigates constraint neglect across model scales without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [186] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Near-Policy Distillation (NPD)方法，通过异步策略解决自回归模型知识蒸馏中的分布不匹配问题，实现8.1倍加速，并使openPangu-Embedded-1B达到68.73%的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏方法在自回归模型中存在分布不匹配问题；现有on-policy方法虽能缓解该问题，但依赖计算开销大的强化学习框架，效率较低。

Method: 提出Near-Policy Distillation (NPD)异步方法，将学生模型生成与训练解耦，支持序列打包的监督微调(SFT)；集成稀疏学生更新和Δ-IFD过滤机制，通过筛选极端分布外样本来稳定优化轨迹，将更新限制在安全的近端学习区域内。

Result: 相比on-policy基线实现8.1倍加速；相比SFT提升8.09%；openPangu-Embedded-1B达到68.73%的最先进性能，优于参数量更大的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: NPD通过有效缩小后续RL的探索空间，显著提升了推理效率和最终性能，证明了异步近端蒸馏策略在平衡效率与效果方面的有效性。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [187] [Grokking or Glitching? How Low-Precision Drives Slingshot Loss Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06152)
*Liu Hanqing,Jianjun Cao,Yuanze Li,Zijian Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度网络长周期无正则训练中的“Slingshot 机制”（周期性损失尖峰）本质上是浮点精度限制所致的数值伪影，而非内在优化动力学。核心机制为“数值特征膨胀”（Numerical Feature Inflation, NFI）：高置信阶段正确类 logit 与其他类 logit 之差超过尾数吸收误差阈值（float32 约 16）后，反向传播中正确类梯度被舍入为零、错误类梯度仍非零，破坏跨类梯度和零和约束，使分类器全局均值 W_G 沿 −μ_G 方向漂移，进而在特征层引入 ε·W_G 的分量，与 μ_G 形成正反馈环，导致两者范数指数增长、余弦相似度趋于 −1。当部分样本的间隔跌回阈值以下时，正确类梯度突然复现，而 Adam 的有效学习率此前已被放大至 η/ε_Adam，产生约 40–50 倍的巨幅更新，从而造成损失尖峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Slingshot 机制（Thilak et al.）是 grokking 研究中反复出现的损失尖峰，通常被归因于 Edge of Stability（EOS）或自适应优化器的内在动力学，但触发机制始终不明。已有工作（Nanda et al.）已指出低精度会加剧该现象，Prieto et al. 提出了“Softmax Collapse (SC)”（正确类 logit 差超过尾数精度阈值时梯度严格归零），但仅将其视为“停止泛化、测试精度停滞”，并未建立 SC 与 Slingshot 的因果联系——反而猜测 Slingshot 是模型为规避 SC 而产生的内在优化响应。同时，梯度流理论预言无正则可分模型的参数范数仅呈对数增长，与实践中观察到的参数与 logit 快速增长相矛盾，说明既有分析忽略了有限精度效应。在 BF16/FP8/FP4 等低精度训练日趋普及的背景下，识别这一失效模式对大模型训练稳定性具有直接价值。

Method: 理论推导与实验验证相结合。（1）从 IEEE 754 吸收误差定义出发（|b|/|a| < 2^{-(p-1)}，float32 阈值 2^{-23}≈1.19e-7），结合 PyTorch Log-Sum-Exp 实现说明 CE 计算中 Z=z_r、正确类梯度 g_r=0 的 SC 条件（间隔 > (p−1)ln2 ≈ 16）。（2）在近似 Neural Collapse（NC1 特征塌缩、NC2 Simplex ETF、NC3 自对偶）几何假设下建立耦合动力学：定理 3.4 证明零和约束破坏后 E[ΔW_G] = −(ηε/K)μ_G，其中 ε 为错误类残余概率质量；命题 3.6 证明特征梯度沿 W_G 方向的分量为 ε·W_G；定理 3.7 将二者写为块矩阵耦合线性系统，得特征值 λ₁=1+ηε/√K>1、λ₂=1−ηε/√K<1，推出 ‖W_G‖、‖μ_G‖ 指数增长且 cos(W_G,μ_G)→−1（NC3′ 修正后的 ETF 几何）。（3）用 Adam 一二阶矩解析计算尖峰步更新量（m_t≈1.46e-8、√v_t≈2.7e-8、更新≈4e-4），与实测分布双峰 ±4e-4 吻合。（4）实验：模块化除法（p=97，Transformer/MLP）、CIFAR-10（MLP/VGG11/VGG11+BN/ViT/ResNet18）、FineWeb nanoGPT 110M；消融含 float64 损失计算、零和投影、Adam ε、LN、BN、标签平滑、学习率、数据规模、bias 项、logit 截断排除下溢；并给出 CE 下 Hessian 最大特征值趋零（区别于 EOS）的 GGN+残差项证明及标签平滑下特征值不消失的证明。

Result: （1）仅将 logit/损失计算转为 float64（参数仍存 float32）即可完全消除 Slingshot 尖峰，证明其源于损失计算而非优化动力学；float64 吸收阈值 2^{-52} 在常规训练时长内不可达。（2）NFI 动力学得到确认：多数样本进入 SC 后 ‖μ_G‖ 进入快速增长阶段，cos(W_G, μ_G)→−1，尖峰前出现参数范数快速增长与 logit 整体负漂移（主导项 W_Gᵀμ_G→−∞）；尖峰步更新分布呈 ±4e-4 双峰，较前一 epoch 放大约 40–50 倍，损失回到 10^0 量级；尖峰后参数范数稳定而 ‖μ_G‖ 与 logit 快速回落。（3）零和投影（g←g−(1/K)Σg_k）可完全消除尖峰，验证零和约束破坏是必要环节；BN 置于分类器前（消除 μ_G 主方向）同样可消除尖峰，而卷积层内的 BN 无效（ReLU 秩压缩重新引入 μ_G）；LayerNorm 反而加速尖峰（特征范数受限、方向与幅值解耦、步长阶跃化）；增大 Adam ε 至 1e-5 可消除尖峰；用大 lr 的普通 GD 可复现同等 epoch 的尖峰（说明与自适应无关，但无法恢复）；标签平滑虽消除尖峰却引入 EOS 类新不稳定（因软标签下 Hessian 特征值不趋零）。（4）架构依赖性：除 ResNet18 外所有模型均出现尖峰（CIFAR-10 上 MLP/VGG11/VGG11+BN/ViT 共 20 次运行全部出现），因为 ResNet18 的 ε 衰减过快（∝1/t 或 log t/t）使 (1+ηε(t)/√K)^t 仅多项式/对数增长；相反 ε 缓慢衰减时才进入指数区间。（5）规模与数据量：不同数据集规模（200–50000）与 batch size、学习率（较小 lr 更易触发）均影响发生率，但非决定性。（6）排除下溢：通过约束 z_min ≥ z_max−100 保证梯度在 float32 可表示范围内，尖峰依旧存在，证明机制源于尾数吸收误差而非指数位下溢。（7）实际任务：mini-batch 下仅约一半样本进入 SC，虽无可见尖峰但仍导致 μ_G 与 logit 快速增长；GPT 训练中每步约 1.3e5 token 有约 4000 个 token 零损失（高频为 “.”、“org”、“example” 等模板可预测 token）；LLM 场景因 Zipf 频率不平衡导致 W_G 天然偏置，高精度反而加剧 logit 增长（float32 为 183 vs float64 为 498），去除末层均值可稳定 logit。（8）同时指出 Wortsman et al. 报道的 logit 负向发散无法复现（PyTorch 与 NanoDo 均未观测到），本文现象为正向发散。（9）Grokking 联系：模块化除法中每次尖峰伴随测试精度阶跃提升，最终在 50 万步达 100%，尖峰相当于一种隐式扰动，帮助跳出尖锐极小值。

Conclusion: Slingshot 应被重新理解为有限精度训练的数值动力学（更准确地说是一种“glitching”而非内在优化现象），而非 EOS 或优化器自适应性的产物。有限精度 CE 计算应作为长周期训练稳定性分析中的一阶因素；该机制同时解释了晚期参数范数与 logit 的异常发散，并提示低精度训练（BF16/FP8/FP4）中的潜在风险——阈值随精度降低而急剧收缩。实用干预方案包括：提升损失计算精度、恢复跨类零和约束、去除末层均值或在分类器前施加 BN、增大 Adam ε。NFI 并不等价于可见损失尖峰，部分吸收虽不产生尖峰仍会破坏零和约束并驱动参数快速增长；同时作者坦承局限：分析聚焦末层分类器与倒数第二层特征的交互，隐含“无约束特征模型”假设，可能低估浅层网络中的动力学（与 Nanda et al. 在浅网上难观测到 Slingshot 的报告一致）。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit periodic loss spikes during unregularized long-term training, a phenomenon known as the "Slingshot Mechanism." Existing work usually attributes this to intrinsic optimization dynamics, but its triggering mechanism remains unclear. This paper proves that this phenomenon is a result of floating-point arithmetic precision limits. As training enters a high-confidence stage, the difference between the correct-class logit and the other logits may exceed the absorption-error threshold. Then during backpropagation, the gradient of the correct class is rounded exactly to zero, while the gradients of the incorrect classes remain nonzero. This breaks the zero-sum constraint of gradients across classes and introduces a systematic drift in the parameter update of the classifier layer. We prove that this drift forms a positive feedback loop with the feature, causing the global classifier mean and the global feature mean to grow exponentially. We call this mechanism Numerical Feature Inflation (NFI). This mechanism explains the rapid norm growth before a Slingshot spike, the subsequent reappearance of gradients, and the resulting loss spike. We further show that NFI is not equivalent to an observed loss spike: in more practical tasks, partial absorption may not produce visible spikes, but it can still break the zero-sum constraint and drive rapid growth of parameter norms. Our results reinterpret Slingshot as a numerical dynamic of finite-precision training, and provide a testable explanation for abnormal parameter growth and logit divergence in late-stage training.

</details>


### [188] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文建立了模型级联的成本-质量边界决策理论框架，证明了k模型池的边界等价于C(k,2)成对级联的点态包络，并推导出边际质量-成本相等的一阶条件。实验验证表明，成对包络已接近最优，路由器超越级联是因为规避了升级前支付便宜模型的生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing cascade methods treat deferral thresholds as empirical hyperparameters without theoretical guidance on the geometry of cost-quality frontiers over model pools. A principled framework is needed to understand and optimize cascade design.

Method: Decision-theoretic framework using constrained optimization and duality theory. For two-model cascades, characterized piecewise concavity of cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit confidence regions. For k-model pools, derived frontier as pointwise envelope over C(k,2) pairwise cascades. Established first-order conditions where single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries.

Result: Validated on 5 benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across 8 models from 5 providers. Deterministic threshold cascades: full fixed chains underperform pairwise envelope; optimized subsequence cascades offer negligible held-out gains over it. Pre-generation routers exceed best cascade on 4/5 datasets by avoiding cheap model's generation cost on escalated queries.

Conclusion: Cascade performance is primarily constrained by structural cost (paying cheap model before escalation) rather than insufficient intermediate stages. Full fixed chains underperform pairwise envelopes, and optimized subsequence cascades yield minimal gains over pairwise approaches. Lightweight pre-generation routers outperform best cascade policies on 4/5 benchmarks.

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [189] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出单一无量纲参数E = T×H/(O+B)可预测并防止MoE模型中的死亡专家现象；E ≥ 0.5足以确保视觉和语言任务中专家生态的健康运转，有望成为MoE训练的统一诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)模型容易出现"死亡专家"现象，即某些专家永远得不到激活，导致专家生态崩溃。现有方法依赖手工设计的辅助损失(如负载均衡损失)来缓解此问题，但缺乏统一、理论驱动的预测框架。

Method: 提出无量纲控制参数E = T×H/(O+B)，整合路由温度(T)、路由熵权重(H)、oracle权重(O)和平衡权重(B)四个超参数。通过12组受控实验(8组视觉任务、4组语言任务)，累计超过11000个训练轮次，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2和WikiText-103数据集上进行跨模态验证。

Result: E ≥ 0.5即可保证零死亡专家，无需额外的负载均衡辅助损失。六项额外发现：(1)死亡专家可被"复苏"——由平衡损失驱动路由器重新探索；(2)"正交毒性"是数据集依赖的，非普遍现象；(3)任务复杂度影响临界E阈值；(4)模型过拟合与专家生态健康解耦；(5)三层MoE自发坍缩为两层功能结构；(6)生态结构在50倍温度范围内保持温度不变性。

Conclusion: E可作为MoE训练的统一诊断工具，其意义堪比流体力学中的雷诺数。该参数通过四个超参数的简单组合，为MoE系统的专家生态健康提供了可预测的量化指标，有望取代繁琐的手工辅助损失设计。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [190] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语言模型中语义等价输入在潜在空间呈局部几何结构分布，其中语义不变性存在于不变子空间，而 paraphrase 变异沿干扰方向分布。基于此框架，本文通过几何表征、对比子空间发现方法和零样本模型归因三个贡献，验证了语义不变性可被视为潜在表示的局部几何属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对 paraphrase 具有强鲁棒性，表明语义信息可能通过稳定的内部表示编码，但这种不变性的结构和起源尚不清晰。

Method: 提出局部几何框架，将语义等价输入映射为潜在空间中的结构化区域；开发对比子空间发现方法以分离语义变化与语义保持的变异；在零样本模型归因任务中应用不变表示。

Result: 不变结构出现在特定深度区域；语义位移主要位于干扰子空间之外；表示层面的干预表明不变成分对模型输出有因果作用；不变表示能够捕捉模型特定的几何模式，实现精确归因。

Conclusion: 语义不变性可被视为潜在表示的局部几何属性，为理解语言模型如何组织意义提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [191] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Transformer 的注意力机制重新解释为 Nadaraya–Watson (NW) 核回归，并据此提出 Cubit——把 token 混合器中的 softmax 归一化替换为核岭回归 (KRR) 的闭式解（核相似度聚合 + Gram 矩阵逆的全局归一化），再配合为保证数值可逆与训练稳定而设计的 Limited-Range Rescale (LRR)；实验显示 Cubit 在 Arxiv / Books3 / FineWeb-Edu 上稳定优于 Transformer 与 DeltaFormer，且优势随训练序列变长、模型变大而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 2017 年 Transformer 诞生以来，位置编码、注意力变体（GQA/MQA/MLA/ReLU/sigmoid attention）、FFN/MoE 等被大量改进，但底层 token 混合的算子始终是注意力。作者指出：注意力输出 z_i = Σ_j A_ij v_j 的形式等价于核平滑方法 NW 回归——用学习到的相似度作核、以 softmax 作 L1 归一化，权重随 query 自适应且不求解全局线性系统；其可学习温度 w 控制带宽（w→0 为全局平均，w→∞ 近似最近邻）。由此产生两个问题：(1) 缺乏统一视角理解 token 混合与经典回归的对应关系；(2) NW 回归缺少显式正则、对噪声与边界效应（边界偏差 O(h)，内部 O(h²)）较脆弱。因此作者希望用数学基础更强的 KRR（显式岭正则、RKHS 中更快的收敛率、抗噪与边界鲁棒性）重新设计 token mixer，作为"下一代架构"的候选。

Method: **理论框架。** 先推导 KRR：由表示定理 f(x)=Σ c_i K(x,x_i)，最优解满足 (K+λI)C=Y，预测 f(x)=k(x)^T (K+λI)^{-1}Y。其中 k(x)^TY 恰是 NW 估计量，而 (K+λI)^{-1} 是耦合所有样本的归一化算子——这正是与注意力（softmax 行归一化）差异所在。

**Cubit 构造。** 依据 Mercer 定理将核写成 K(x_i,x_j)=ψ(x_i)ψ(x_j)^T，ψ(x)=φ(xW_K)；并按 Mercer 一致性要求，Σ^(h)=(g(K^(h)K^(h)T)+λI)^{-1}，其中 g 可取 Softmax 等，φ 可取恒等或 ℓ₂ 归一化。关键松弛：把回归中对称的 k(x_i) 改用标准注意力的 query–key 参数化 k(x_i)=[φ(x_iW_Q)φ(x_jW_K)^T]_{j≤i}（仅因果前缀），既提升表达力与数值稳定性，又保证当 φ 为恒等、且对 [k(x_i);…;k(x_N)] 做 softmax 时退化为标准注意力。最终 Z^(h)=A^(h) Σ^(h) S^(h) V^(h)。

**LRR。** 引入可学习缩放向量 ŝ_i=α·σ([W_s x]_i)+β（α,β>0），取值限于 (β, α+β)，保证 Ŝ=diag(ŝ) 可逆且逆元有界；据此求解修正后的 KRR 线性系统 Ŝ(Σ)^{-1} o = V，得 o=Σ Ŝ^{-1} V。实现中 λ 以 log 空间参数化，r 投影经 ℓ₂ 归一化与可学习 scale，Σ 用 softmax 相似度加 λI 后做三角/一般线性方程组求解（torch.linalg.solve_triangular / solve）。

**与 DeltaFormer 差异。** 动机不同（KRR vs DeltaNet+Transformer 组合）；DeltaFormer 用 w/k 两套嵌入构造逆矩阵且强制对角为 1（误差随长度累积），Cubit 只需单一嵌入（k 或 r）保证可逆、对角不必为 1；Cubit 另有 LRR。

**局部线性回归变体。** 附录 E 给出 LLR 版本，用带截距的加权最小二乘 S^(h)=e_1^T(X_i^T W_i X_i)^{-1}X_i^T W_i 修正边界偏差，但计算开销大，正则→∞ 时退化为 NW/Transformer。

Result: **语言建模损失。** 125M 参数、训练长度 512：Arxiv 上 Cubit 1.8802 vs Transformer 1.9003、DeltaFormer 1.8983；Books3 上 3.4168 vs 3.4514 / 3.4371。长度 1024：Arxiv 1.7023 vs 1.7210；Books3 3.2204 vs 3.2529。FineWeb-Edu 上 512 时 3.7110 vs 3.7443 / 3.7242；1024 时 3.4597 vs 3.4960 / 3.4705。Books3 验证 5K 步时 Cubit 4.0885 已优于 Transformer 4.1494 与 DeltaFormer 4.1044，说明从训练初期即占优。

**长序列规律。** 在 FineWeb 上，Cubit 相对 Transformer 的损失差随长度单调增大，8192 token 时稳定在 0.05–0.06；与 DeltaFormer 的差距同样随长度扩大。双倍 batch size 实验表明增益来自长序列建模能力而非更多训练 token。

**规模扩展。** 350M：Cubit 3.3166 vs Transformer 3.3438、DeltaFormer 3.3284；1.3B：Cubit 3.1849 vs Transformer 3.2100、DeltaFormer 3.2057（DeltaFormer 的相对优势随模型变大而消失）。

**下游零样本（lm-evaluation-harness）。** 350M/10B tokens 平均分 Cubit 48.31 vs Transformer 48.13、DeltaFormer 48.09；350M/50B：50.01 vs 49.48 / 49.63；1.3B/10B：51.78 vs 51.53 / 51.33。各单项任务（ARC-C、HellaSwag、PIQA、SciQ、WinoGrande、SocialIQA、RACE）互有胜负但平均最优。

**消融。** Cubit(3.4597) > Cubit-NoLRR(3.4645) > Transformer(3.4960)；使用独立投影矩阵优于共享 key 嵌入的 Cubit-Share，但共享版本仍全程优于 Transformer，说明增益源于结构设计而非参数量。核函数对比：Σ 用 softmax、Kernel 用 softmax 时最优（3.711）；Kernel 用 RBF 时 Cubit 变差（3.7242），仅 Σ 用 RBF 时明显退化（3.9499）。三随机种子实验显示 Cubit 均值一致最优（如 50K 步 3.4811 vs Transformer 3.5185、DeltaFormer 3.5024），但方差与基线相当，差异幅度有限。

**回归方法对比（Books3, 512）。** Transformer(NW) 50K 为 3.4701，Cubit-LLR 3.4543，Cubit-KRR 3.4350，KRR 优于 LLR，LLR 优于 NW。

Conclusion: 结论：Cubit 以 KRR 闭式解替换 Transformer 中的 NW 估计器，用"核相似度聚合 + Gram 矩阵逆归一化 + LRR 有界缩放"构成新的 token mixer，在多数据集、多长度、多模型规模与下游任务上一致优于 Transformer 与 DeltaFormer，且增益随序列长度增长而扩大，作者认为其有潜力成为下一代基础模型架构。

局限与未来工作（作者自述）：(1) 计算复杂度仍为二次量级——需构造并求解 T×T 的线性方程组，序列很长时显存/算力开销大（实现中逐 head 解三角方程组）；(2) LLR 变体虽能缓解边界效应但速度慢，需引入 Local Kernel Ridge Regression 等更快的方法；(3) 期望继续寻找解释 token mixer 的其他理论框架；(4) 附录仅泛泛提及"更强模型可能被滥用"的局限讨论；(5) 实验为中等规模（125M–1.3B）与中等 token 量（10B–50B），尚未在超大规模预训练上验证。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [192] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign proposes sequence-level self-alignment for compact audio tokenization, reformulating tokenization as conditional sequence generation with cross-view consistency training, achieving 55% token reduction while preserving edit-distance search.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing audio tokenizers rely on local quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, leading to poor sequence consistency, uncontrolled length, unbounded edit distances, and degenerate token distributions. These limitations prevent efficient symbolic operations like retrieval and memory.

Method: PairAlign employs conditional sequence generation with an encoder-decoder architecture. It uses cross-paired teacher forcing, EMA-teacher targets, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control to refine VQ-style tokenization. Training objectives maximize cross-view likelihood consistency between two content-preserving views while penalizing many-to-one collapse.

Result: On 3-second speech, PairAlign achieves compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage. On TIMIT, it reduces archive token count by 55% while preserving edit-distance search. The probe confirms strong length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100ms shifts.

Conclusion: PairAlign presents a novel paradigm for audio tokenization by unifying representation learning with discrete sequence generation, eliminating the need for direct supervision while achieving strong edit-distance preservation and token efficiency.

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency. On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [193] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在理论上证明，在特定问题几何下（如ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-平滑性和可分离噪声模型），SignSGD相比SGD具有d倍的复杂度优势，并将该理论推广至矩阵域的Muon优化器，通过124M参数GPT-2预训练验证了理论预测的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决sign-based优化算法（如SignSGD、Muon）在训练大模型时表现优异，但缺乏理论解释的核心问题。标准理论下SGD已是minimax最优，无法解释sign方法的优越性。

Method: 引入新的问题几何框架：ℓ₁范数平稳性、ℓ∞-平滑性假设，以及可分离噪声模型（更适合刻画signed更新的坐标特性）；推导SignSGD的匹配上下界；将框架推广至矩阵域分析Muon优化器。

Result: 1. 在稀疏噪声条件下，SignSGD相比SGD复杂度降低d倍（d为问题维度）；2. 将理论扩展至矩阵域，证明Muon保持此最优维度缩放特性；3. 124M参数GPT-2预训练实验验证了理论预测的收敛速度优势。

Conclusion: Sign-based优化器的优势源于其隐式地在不同问题几何下工作；本研究为理解大模型训练中sign优化器的有效性提供了理论依据，并桥接了理论与实践的gap。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [194] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"无基准比较安全评分"框架，用于在缺乏标注基准的情况下评估语言模型安全性。通过"工具有效性链"替代真值标注，在SimpleAudit工具上验证可行，并在挪威公共采购案例中演示应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署常需比较语言模型安全性，但针对特定语言、行业或监管场景的标注基准往往不存在，导致缺乏可靠的安全评估方法。

Method: (1) 形式化定义"无基准比较安全评分"问题；(2) 建立基于场景审计的评估契约；(3) 用"工具有效性链"替代ground-truth协议，包含：受控安全vs消融对比响应、目标驱动方差主导审计员/评判者伪影、跨重运行稳定性；(4) 开发SimpleAudit本地优先评分工具。

Result: Safe与abliterated目标分离AUROC达0.89-1.00；目标身份是主要方差成分(η²≈0.52)；严重性画像在10次重运行后稳定。将同一方法链应用于Petri表明其同样适用。挪威公共采购案例比较Borealis与Gemma 3显示：更安全的模型取决于场景类别和风险度量。

Conclusion: 评分结果需同时报告原始分数、匹配差值、临界率、不确定性及审计员/评判者身份，而非简化为单一排名。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [195] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VHG框架，通过在 setter-solver 双角色中引入独立 verifier 形成三角色自博弈，解决大模型生成问题时 reward hacking 导致的无效性问题，在积分和数学推理任务上显著超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While LLMs excel at solving scientific and mathematical problems, they struggle to generate valid, challenging, and novel problems. Existing approaches either rely on expensive human experts or suffer from reward hacking in naive self-play paradigms, frequently producing invalid problems.

Method: VHG proposes a three-party self-play framework integrating an independent verifier into the setter-solver duality. The verifier evaluates problem validity while the solver assesses difficulty, constraining the setter's reward jointly. Two verifier variants are instantiated: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier.

Result: VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin on both indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks.

Conclusion: VHG framework effectively addresses the reward hacking problem in self-play problem generation through verifier integration, substantially outperforming all baseline methods on indefinite integral and mathematical reasoning tasks.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>
